Still present: world sprites (sprite_pass.cpp:41) and debug lines (debug_draw_pass.cpp:38) render into the HDR target, so the default ACES_FITTED tonemap and bloom apply to 2D art, and debug colours scale with exposure. This is the design's choice, and nothing in docs/api tells a 2D project to use NO_POST_PROCESS / camera postProcess = false. Whether to document the choice or change the default is a product decision. *Fix:* Options. (A) Docs only (renderer-owned): in rendering-a-frame.md's 'Sprites and debug drawing' section and handbook renderer.md, state that WORLD sprites and debug lines go through exposure, bloom and the tonemapper, and that 2D projects should set camera_component postProcess = false (view_flags::NO_POST_PROCESS, tonemap NONE) or tonemap_operator::NONE. The 2D demos should then do so. (B) Behaviour: draw debug lines after the tonemap into LDR (a frame-ordering change; the depth buffer is still available), and/or give views with no 3D geometry tonemap NONE by default (a heuristic that changes looks silently). Recommendation: A now; consider moving debug lines after the tonemap as a separate design change.

### editor (4)

- **F655** MINOR play.state time fields are inconsistent across stop and pause (`engine/src/editor/core/play_controller.cpp:1`): same as F730.
- **F656** MINOR asset.delete removes the currently open scene (and the startup scene) without a guard; odd error codes for missing files (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_asset.cpp:1`). Still present. asset_delete (commands_asset.cpp:381-419) has no check against context.scene_path() or the startup scene. A missing asset.import source answers ASSET_NOT_FOUND (235-239), and editor_error.cpp:158-159 deliberately maps engine PROJECT_NOT_FOUND to ASSET_NOT_FOUND. Refusing versus warning, and the error-code taxonomy for missing external files (PATH_NOT_FOUND is reserved for reflection paths, editor_error.h:33), are protocol choices the docs do not settle. *Fix:* Options for the open scene: (a) refuse with INVALID_ARGS "close the scene first (scene.close)"; (b) allow it but emit a `warning` event and mark the scene unsaved. For the startup scene: refuse, or warn. For error codes: IO_ERROR for unreadable external files (asset.import source, a project.open directory), or keep ASSET_NOT_FOUND as 'a reference did not resolve' (ai-protocol.md:53). Recommendation: (a) refuse deleting the open scene, warn with a `warning` event when deleting the project's startup scene, answer IO_ERROR for an unreadable asset.import source, and keep project.open's mapping because a missing project is a reference that did not resolve. Document the choice in ai-protocol.md.
- **F730** MINOR play.stop reports simulationFrame 0, and fixedStepIndex and simulationTime continue across play sessions (`engine/src/editor/core/play_controller.cpp:90`). Still present. stop() zeroes mSimulationFrames before emit_state() (play_controller.cpp:89-91), so the play.stop reply and event always show simulationFrame 0. simulationTime and fixedStepIndex are read from the engine's process-wide clock (201-202) and are never reset at play.start. Whether play.* should report session-relative or engine-wide time is a contract the design does not settle (04_editor.md:877 lists only {state, frame, simulationTime}). *Fix:* Options: (a) session-relative: play_controller records time.time and fixedStepIndex at start() and reports differences, and the stop reply carries the ended session's values; (b) keep the engine's process-wide clocks, which are what game code reads (06_engine_loop.md:129), document that in ai-protocol.md and the play.* result schemas, and fix only simulationFrame: keep the ended session's count until the next start(), i.e. delete the zeroing at line 90 (start() already resets it at 57); (c) reset the engine's fixed-step clock at play.start (an engine API change that affects scripts and physics timing). Recommendation: (b). It needs no engine change and matches what scripts observe; the paused time advance (F655) goes to the engine owner if confirmed.
- **F740** MINOR Token can only be passed on the command line and is compared with a non-constant-time == (`engine/src/editor/core/editor_options.cpp:97`). Still present. --ai-token is the only source (editor_options.cpp:97-99), so the token shows in the process list. The comparisons use plain == / != (protocol_server.cpp:354, commands_session.cpp:20). 04_editor.md 3.12 specifies only the command-line form, so adding a new way to pass the secret is a product choice; the constant-time compare alone would be trivial. *Fix:* Options: (a) keep --ai-token and add a CONJURE_AI_TOKEN environment variable, read when --ai-token is absent; (b) add --ai-token-file <path>; (c) have the editor generate a random token and write it next to .conjure/ai_port with owner-only permissions (needs per-OS ACL code), and clients read it; (d) document the exposure (loopback-only server) and change nothing. Recommendation: (a), plus a constant_time_equals(string_view, string_view) helper used in protocol_server.cpp:354 and commands_session.cpp:20, plus a note in --help and ai-protocol.md that the command-line form is visible to other local users.

### physics (4)

- **F421** MINOR Characters render with the scale they had at creation and are not carried by moving ground (`engine/src/physics/physics_world_characters.cpp:242`). Both parts are present. row.lastScale is set only in create_character (physics_world_characters.cpp:132), and pull_characters writes it into interpolated scale (:242-243), so a runtime scale change never renders. step_characters adds only the vertical ground velocity (:194-202). The scale part is a clear small bug. Carrying characters on moving ground interacts with the pull writing the achieved velocity back into character_controller.velocity (:249), which the next step reuses as the desired velocity, so adding ground velocity naively would compound every step. That needs a semantic choice. *Fix:* Scale part (do now, no decision needed): in push_changed_components' visitCharacter (physics_world_sync.cpp:252), after compose_world_pose, set `row->lastScale = pose.scale;`. In push_hierarchy_rows (sync.cpp:296-302), for CHARACTER rows set `row.lastScale = pose.scale;` (BODY rows keep the mark_rebuild). Moving ground, options: (a) the engine carries characters: when ON_GROUND, add the horizontal part of character.GetGroundVelocity() to the velocity passed to SetLinearVelocity in step_characters, and subtract it again when pull_characters writes controller->velocity, so the component keeps the character's own (ground-relative) velocity and nothing compounds; (b) document that scripts must add the ground velocity themselves. That needs a new groundVelocity field in character_controller (a 68-byte component with a C# mirror and layout hash, so a physics/managed/bindings change), because KINEMATIC_MOVES_WITH_TRANSFORM platforms never have their velocity pulled into rigid_body; (c) document the limitation only. Recommendation: the scale fix now plus (a). It stays inside physics, matches Jolt's own character sample, and is what users of a moving-platform recipe expect. *Test:* tests/physics/test_character_controller.cpp: "character_controller - a runtime scale change reaches the interpolated pose" (set local_transform.scale = 2 on a character, run_steps(2), CHECK interpolated current.scale == vec3(2)). If (a) is chosen, also add "character_controller - a character standing on a moving kinematic platform is carried with it": KINEMATIC_MOVES_WITH_TRANSFORM plate moved 2 m/s along x, character with velocity 0 on it, 60 steps, character x within 5 cm of the plate's x.
- **F621** MINOR shape_cast takes a direction that includes the length while raycast takes a ray plus maxDistance (`engine/include/conjure/physics/physics_world.h:253`). Still as described: physics_world.h:250-258 shape_cast takes a direction that carries the length, while raycast/raycast_all (213-227) take maxDistance, and raycast_all/overlap_* return the count written. Both shapes are what 14_physics.md specifies (3.8 lines 543-551 'distance = fraction * length(direction)', 4.2 line 683 '// includes length', 546 'returns the count'), and the bindings already adapt (entries_physics.cpp:254-258). Changing the signature is a public API change that bindings must follow. *Fix:* Options. (A) Keep the design signatures and remove the ambiguity (recommended). Rename the parameter `direction` to `sweep` in physics_world.h:256 and in the shape_cast definition (physics_world_queries.cpp:360ff). Write its summary as 'Sweeps the shape from start by `sweep` (direction times distance in metres; a unit vector sweeps 1 m); the closest hit.' Add to raycast_all/overlap_sphere/overlap_box: 'returns the number written, at most out.size(); a result equal to out.size() may be truncated.' Mirror both sentences in docs/api/physics/physics-world-and-queries.md. (B) Change shape_cast to (shape, start, unit direction, maxDistance) and return total counts. This means editing 14_physics.md 3.8/4.2 and bindings entries_physics.cpp:214-220,254-258, and the span collectors could no longer ForceEarlyOut, which adds query cost (multi). Recommendation: A.
- **F628** MINOR Capsule height and angle units differ between sibling physics/renderer components (`engine/include/conjure/physics/character_controller.h:47`). Still as described: character_controller.h:47-48 uses a total `height` and `maxSlopeAngleDegrees`, while capsule_collider (components.h:101) and capsule_shape_desc (queries.h:72) use halfHeight, and other authored angles are in radians. But docs/design/14_physics.md:211-213 specifies exactly these fields, the field names and comments state the units (character_controller.h:42-47, bodies-and-colliders.md:109), and changing them would alter the scene format and the meaning of the 68-byte C# CharacterControllerData mirror (PhysicsMirrors.cs:222-247). Whether to unify is a design call. *Fix:* Options. (A) Keep the fields and document the conversions (recommended). In character_controller.h:47 change the comment to `// total capsule height; the equivalent capsule_collider / capsule_shape_desc has halfHeight = height / 2 - radius`. On line 48 add `// degrees (light cones and camera FOV are radians)`. Add the same two sentences to the 'The character controller' paragraph of docs/api/physics/bodies-and-colliders.md (line 109). (B) Re-express the controller as radius + halfHeight and maxSlopeAngleRadians. This touches 14_physics.md 2.3, physics_world_characters.cpp:33,61,71, physics_world_sync.cpp:243, the C# mirror, tests and every saved scene. It is format-breaking and multi-area. (C) Add a display unit to the reflection field attributes (core) so the inspector converts. Recommendation: A now; B only together with a planned scene-format version bump.
- **F629** MINOR Component type names and registered names follow no single rule (`engine/include/conjure/physics/components.h:214`). Still present: components.h:204/214 registers physics_material as "conjure.physics.material". Every other physics component registers under its struct name, and 14_physics.md:139 plus bodies-and-colliders.md:57 say "conjure.physics.<name>". The renderer `_component` suffix and the audio prefix belong to other modules. The registered string is the serialised scene key and the C# [NativeComponent] key (PhysicsMirrors.cs:207, test_components.cpp:53,63, 15_bindings.md, csharp-api.md), so renaming it is a format/ABI choice across modules. *Fix:* Options. (A) Keep every registered name and make the physics docs state the exception (recommended). In components.h:13-14 change 'named "conjure.physics.<name>"' to 'named "conjure.physics.<struct name>" (physics_material registers as "conjure.physics.material")'. In docs/api/physics/bodies-and-colliders.md:57 add the same note, and in the physics_material row (line 68) add 'registered as `conjure.physics.material`'. (B) Register it as "conjure.physics.physics_material". This edits components.h:204,214, PhysicsMirrors.cs:207, BindingProbes.cs, test_components.cpp, docs/api/bindings/csharp-api.md and docs/design/15_bindings.md, and breaks saved scenes (multi). (C) Adopt a repo-wide rule (`conjure.<module>.<struct name>`, drop the `_component` suffix in renderer). This is a large multi-module rename. Recommendation: A, and settle C (if wanted) before any public release.

### build (3)

- **F613** MINOR Absolute build-machine source paths are embedded in every binary (`engine/include/conjure/core/log.h:272`). Still present: CONJURE_LOG (log.h:272) and CONJURE_ASSERT (assert.h:95) pass __FILE__, and conjure_platform_defines (cmake/ConjureWarnings.cmake:56) sets no path mapping, so absolute checkout paths are embedded in binaries. Stripping them in every configuration would also turn the paths in log records, doctest failure output (tests/support doctest.h:86) and asserts relative, which hurts IDE click-through in development, so this is a policy choice. *Fix:* Options: (a) map in all configurations: MSVC `/d1trimfile:${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/` (undocumented but widely used) and GCC/Clang `-fmacro-prefix-map=${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/=` on conjure_platform_defines. Use the macro-only map, not -ffile-prefix-map, so debug info stays usable; (b) the same flags only under `$<$<CONFIG:Distribution>:...>`, so shipped binaries are clean and Debug/Development keep absolute paths for IDE navigation; (c) leave as is. The tests that compare against __FILE__ (test_assert.cpp:94, test_log.cpp:58, test_assert_capture.cpp:47) pass under all options. The PDB path in the PE debug directory is a separate leak (/PDBALTPATH) worth handling in the same change. Recommendation: (b), plus a note in the build-pipeline handbook.
- **F772** MINOR Nine of twelve dependencies are pinned to mutable tags (`third_party/CMakeLists.txt:33`). Accurate: cgltf, miniaudio, glm, yyjson, doctest, glfw, glslang, Jolt and imgui use tags (third_party/CMakeLists.txt:33, 39, 72, 99, 119, 146, 210, 233, 331). The project rule explicitly allows this: AGENTS.md:48 and the header at :3 say "pinned to a tag or commit". Whether to tighten it is a supply-chain policy choice with a clone-time cost. *Fix:* Options: (a) Keep tags. The rule allows them, and the CI _deps cache is keyed on this file's hash. (b) Use 40-character commit hashes with GIT_SHALLOW FALSE. Immutable, but fresh configures and CI caches grow a lot (full histories of glslang, Jolt, imgui, glfw). (c) Switch to URL archives (https://github.com/<org>/<repo>/archive/<sha>.tar.gz) with URL_HASH SHA256=... Content-verified, no git history, fastest; _deps re-downloads once. Recommendation: (c) for all twelve dependencies, the tag kept as a trailing comment. Then reword AGENTS.md:48 and the file header to "pinned to a commit and verified by hash".
- **F781** MINOR Development links with /INCREMENTAL, the configuration the benchmarks are measured in (`cmake/ConjureConfigs.cmake:49`). Still present: cmake/ConjureConfigs.cmake:48-50 copies RelWithDebInfo linker flags (`/debug /INCREMENTAL` on MSVC) into Development, and nightly.yml:295-330 measures the enforced benchmarks in windows-msvc-dev. Development is also documented as the day-to-day configuration (ConjureConfigs.cmake:4-5), where incremental linking is a deliberate convenience, so trading link time against benchmark fidelity is an owner choice. *Fix:* Options: (A) Development on MSVC links `/debug /INCREMENTAL:NO /OPT:REF /OPT:ICF` (most faithful to Distribution, slower links, ICF folds functions and confuses debugging); (B) `/debug /INCREMENTAL:NO` only (removes the incremental-link thunks and padding that skew benchmarks, keeps debugging exact, costs full links of conjure_shared); (C) keep /INCREMENTAL and measure benchmarks in a separate configuration or document the bias. Recommendation: B, implemented by replacing '/INCREMENTAL' with '/INCREMENTAL:NO' in the derived *_LINKER_FLAGS_DEVELOPMENT values when MSVC; note the `if(NOT CMAKE_<kind>_LINKER_FLAGS_DEVELOPMENT)` guard means existing trees keep the old cache value until a fresh configure (or the derivation must FORCE the corrected value).

### docs (3)

- **F165** MAJOR entity_duplicate refuses with STRUCTURE_LOCKED inside a scope; the binding design promises a deferred form (`engine/src/bindings/entries_entity.cpp:345`). Still present: entity_duplicate refuses with STRUCTURE_LOCKED inside a scope (entries_entity.cpp:345-352, pinned by test_span_scope_routing.cpp:85-88 and documented in README.md:124-125 and handbook bindings.md:85), while 15_bindings.md B7 (:26), the 3.3 table (:419) and :428 ('none in the table today') promise a deferred form. A deferred form would need entity_archive::capture to work on a locked world, which it refuses today (engine/src/ecs/entity_archive.cpp:53), so the owner must choose between amending the design and building that path. *Fix:* Options: (a) Amend the design to match the code: remove entity_duplicate from the B7 deferred list (:26), set the 3.3 locked column (:419) to "-STRUCTURE_LOCKED: capturing a subtree is refused on a locked world (entity_archive::capture)", change :428 '(none in the table today)' to '(entity_duplicate)', and rename the test-list case at :765 accordingly. Docs only. (b) Implement the deferred form: allow a read-only capture on a locked world in ecs (columns cannot move while locked), record commands().instantiate(archive) plus an ATTACH fixup, and regenerate guids for the copy (the same problem as F164). This touches ecs, scene and bindings. Recommendation: (a) now; revisit (b) together with the F164 prefab adoption work.
- **F195** MAJOR Binding documentation layout promises user-facing pages that do not exist (getting started, concepts, FAQ, gotchas, pipeline internals) (`docs/design/16_documentation.md:29`). Still present. 16 section 2 (lines 29-35, 47) and D6 (line 16) promise docs/api/getting-started/, concepts/, scripting/csharp/, faq.md, gotchas.md and six handbook pipeline chapters. docs/api holds only module folders plus glossary.md and index.md, and mkdocs.yml has no such nav entries. handbook/index.md section 3 already re-scopes some handbook chapters, but nothing records the deviation in 09 and no user-facing 'build, open the editor, first project' page exists. Which pages to write and which to drop from a binding design is a product choice. *Fix:* Options: (a) Write every promised page: a large docs effort that needs editor, runtime and scripting walkthroughs. (b) Amend 16 section 2 and D6 to the shipped layout and record the deviation in 09: per-module wiki folders replace concepts/; docs/api/bindings replaces scripting/csharp/; faq and gotchas become WARNING/TIP callouts on each page; editor internals, AI-protocol internals and export are covered by handbook modules/editor.md, modules/engine.md 7 and modules/runtime.md; the pipeline chapters stay planned in handbook/index.md 3. (c) Recommended hybrid: do (b), plus write one docs/api/getting-started.md page with the badge. Its steps: build with `python tools/build.py`; `build/<preset>/bin/conjure_editor --project <dir>` (try tests/assets/projects/minimal), or create a project with `project.create`; add an entity and save the scene; add a C# script with `script.create` / `script.attach`; export with `conjure_editor --project <dir> --headless --export <out>`; run with `conjure_runtime --project <out>`. Link it from docs/api/index.md, the mkdocs.yml nav and handbook/getting-started.md.
- **F769** MINOR D8 ('complete, compile-checked snippets ... kept under 30 lines') is neither enforced nor met (`docs/design/16_documentation.md:18`). Still present. D8 (docs/design/16_documentation.md:18) requires compile-checked snippets under 30 lines, but tools/check_docs.py:30 has only MAX_PAGE_LINES = 400 and no snippet compile target exists. A re-count today finds the same ten fenced blocks over 30 lines (longest: docs/api/assets/database-and-import.md:130, 70 lines). Line 120 says mkdocs build --strict runs in a 'docs' job, but it is a step of style-and-headers (.github/workflows/ci.yml:196-201). Whether to build the enforcement or relax a binding decision is the owner's call. *Fix:* Options: (a) Enforce D8: add a fenced-block line cap to tools/check_docs.py with a self-test case, split the ten long samples (assets, audio, core and ecs wiki pages, module-system/creating-a-module.md) and add a doc-snippet compile target. This spans tools, several modules' docs and build, and is large. (b) Relax D8 to current practice: 'Examples are complete snippets drawn from tests where possible and kept short (aim for under 30 lines); signatures stay correct through the definition of done (D7), not by compiling the pages', and record the deviation in 09. (c) Option (b) now, plus a line-cap check in check_docs.py later as a tools task. Recommendation: (b), docs only. Whichever option is chosen, change 16_documentation.md:120 to: 'CI: `docs.check`, the checker self-test and `mkdocs build --strict` run in the Documentation step of the `style-and-headers` job (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`).'

### assets (2)

- **F021** MAJOR includeAll cook fails on any NO_IMPORTER, MISSING_SOURCE, META_INVALID or DUPLICATE_UUID record (`engine/src/assets/asset_cooker.cpp:120`). Still present, and deliberate. With includeAll every record becomes a root (asset_cooker.cpp:72-75); any record that can_cook() rejects becomes a failure (:120-126), and that aborts the cook (:178-197). The test 'asset_cooker - one failing asset writes nothing and lists every failure' (test_asset_cooker.cpp:148-179) explicitly expects a NO_IMPORTER orphan.xyz to fail an includeAll cook, and export_pipeline.cpp:242 sets includeAll whenever no startup scene exists. Whether stray or deleted files should block an export is a product choice. *Fix:* Option A (recommended): with includeAll, take as roots only records that can be cooked. Skip NO_IMPORTER and MISSING_SOURCE records with a cook_report warning ("'<path>' skipped: record is NO_IMPORTER"). Keep META_INVALID and DUPLICATE_UUID as hard failures, since they are broken project state the user must fix. Keep the hard failure for any non-cookable record reached through a dependency or named in roots/alwaysInclude. Then update the test's orphan.xyz expectation (one failure, broken.ta, plus one warning) and the includeAll rows in docs/api/assets/cooking-and-packs.md and docs/handbook/modules/assets.md. Option B: keep the cooker strict and change engine/src/engine/export_pipeline.cpp to refuse an export without a startup scene, or to root the cook at every scene asset instead of includeAll (engine area). Option C: also purge MISSING_SOURCE records that the editor deleted outside the database (separate database work).
- **F308** MINOR Binding a uuid no source knows takes the source lock and two hash lookups on every bind (`engine/src/assets/asset_manager.cpp:182`). The cost is real but it buys a documented contract. find_or_create queries the sources whenever a slot's type is still INVALID (asset_manager.cpp:179-189), and asset_manager.h:172-174 promises that 'When the slot's type is not yet known it is taken from the source', so a slot bound before its asset was imported or created learns its type later. The finding's fix (query only when created) would break that: bind_typed would store the requested type at :1062-1065 instead of detecting a mismatch. A correct cache needs a change signal from asset_source, which is a public interface. The cost only applies to uuids no source knows (already-broken references). *Fix:* Option A (recommended): won't fix. Lookups happen only for unresolved uuids; optionally document in asset_manager.h that binding an unknown uuid re-queries the sources. Option B: add `virtual u64 generation() const noexcept` to asset_source (the database bumps it on record changes, packs return a constant), store per slot the summed generation of the last miss, and skip the query while it is unchanged. That is a public interface change for every source implementation, plugins included. Option C (the finding's fix): query only on creation and on add_source/swap_source. Not recommended: assets created or imported after a slot exists (editor create, hot-reload add_file) would no longer give that slot its type, and type mismatches would surface later as FAILED(TYPE_MISMATCH) instead of an unbound ref plus ERROR.

### bindings (2)

- **F436** MINOR null string / List / array fields come back as empty after a hot reload or snapshot (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Runtime/FieldBlob.cs:463`). Still present: FieldBlob.cs:463 writes a null string as "", ScriptClassInfo.cs:64-71 writes count 0 for a null collection, and Read (:128-172) always assigns a new list or array. Neither the blob (script_fields.h:21-25) nor the scene JSON (script_fields.cpp:80-82 requires is_string) can represent null. Design 03 3.8 promises that fields passing 3.7 'survive' but says nothing about null, so the intended semantics are an owner choice. *Fix:* Option A (recommended): document Unity-like semantics, docs only. In docs/api/bindings/csharp-api.md, in the field paragraph at :39-40, add: 'a null string, List or array field comes back as "" / empty after a hot reload, a scene load or entering play; initialise collections in the field initialiser and test Count rather than null'. Optionally add the same note to design 03 3.8 Guarantees. Option B: encode null. Use the reserved u16 of the field header (script_fields.h:22) as a NULL flag in managed FieldBlobWriter/Reader and native field_blob_writer/reader, map it to JSON null in blob_to_json/json_to_blob, and accept null in is_scalar_compatible for STRING and LIST. This is a wire and scene-format change across managed and scripting with a FIELD_BLOB_VERSION bump. Recommend A: it matches the Unity convention users expect and needs no format change.
- **F438** MINOR Scene.ActivePath returns the most recently loaded scene, including additive ones (`engine/src/bindings/entries_scene.cpp:165`). Still present: scene_active_path returns path_of(loaded.back()) (entries_scene.cpp:165-166), the most recently loaded scene, additive ones included, and test_prefab_scene_bindings.cpp:166-169 pins that (active == sceneB after two loads). The design names the entry and the C# property ('the asset path of the active scene', Scene.cs:86) but never defines 'active' (15_bindings.md:145, :277), so which scene it means is a product choice. *Fix:* Options: (a) Unity semantics: the active scene is the first loaded entry (the last REPLACE load; additive loads do not change it), i.e. `path_of(loaded.front())`, with the comment updated and the test expectation changed to sceneA. (b) Keep back() and document ActivePath as 'the most recently loaded scene' in Scene.cs:86, csharp-api.md:118 and 15_bindings.md:277. (c) Have scene_manager track an explicit active scene with a setter (scene module, larger). Recommendation: (a), a one-line change in bindings that matches what Unity users expect from 'active scene'.

### audio (1)

- **F319** MINOR Group gain is hierarchical but group pause is not (`engine/src/audio/group_table.cpp:171`). Still present. group_table::set_paused flips one bit (group_table.cpp:171-182) while set_gain walks the subtree (:154-169), and the mixer tests only the voice's own group (mixer.cpp:268). As a result, even set_group_paused(MASTER_GROUP) leaves voices in child groups playing. Design 12_audio.md says nothing about whether group pause is hierarchical (3.3, :312), and the header text 'every voice of the group' (audio_engine.h:385) reads either way. *Fix:* Option A, recommended: hierarchical pause, like gain and like the usual bus model. Add effectivePaused to each group_table slot (own flag OR the parent's effectivePaused), recomputed in set_paused, create, remove and reparenting with the existing recompute_subtree walk. set_paused returns a group_change_list. set_group_paused, create_group (instead of the hard-coded false at audio_engine.cpp:1862-1866) and remove_group push SET_GROUP_PAUSED with the effective flag for every changed group. The mixer is unchanged. group_info gains effectivePaused. Option B: keep pause per group, document it on set_group_paused and in the docs, and expose group_info::effectivePaused = paused. Users then pause children explicitly; set_group_paused(MASTER_GROUP) should then be documented as pausing only master-level voices, with set_paused() as the whole-mixer pause. A is about 50 lines inside audio. B is docs only.

### ci (1)

- **F130** MINOR AGENTS.md and three other documents point at an add-component skill that does not exist (`AGENTS.md:22`). Still present: AGENTS.md:22, docs/handbook/repository-layout.md:36 and docs/handbook/contributing.md:125 name .claude/skills/add-component/SKILL.md, which is not tracked (git ls-files .claude lists five skills; the local directory is empty). docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:495 and docs/design/00_brief.md:53 make the skill a wave-5 deliverable, so the choice is whether to deliver it now or mark the references as planned. *Fix:* Options: (A) write .claude/skills/add-component/SKILL.md now (ci area only; contributing.md:125 already defines its content: the CONJURE_COMPONENT (conjure/ecs/component.h) / CONJURE_REFLECT_STRUCT (conjure/core/reflect/reflect.h) pair, registration, the C# mirror and the required test), which makes all references true; (B) mark it as planned (wave 5) in AGENTS.md:22, repository-layout.md:36 and contributing.md:125 (multi: ci + docs). Optionally extend tools/check_docs.py to verify backticked repo paths in AGENTS.md and the handbook (tools area, separate change). Recommendation: A, because AGENTS.md is the single source of truth and the wave-6 demos will add components; the skill must be written from the real component macros and checked against an existing component.

### ecs (1)

- **F636** MINOR Read-only counters are non-const, so const queries, views and schedulers cannot be inspected (`engine/include/conjure/ecs/query.h:94`). The observation is accurate, but the non-const signatures are deliberate. query::chunk_count/entity_count/matches call refresh(), which appends to mMatches (query.cpp:38-62, 93-103), and scheduler::stages may run build_stages, which allocates and can fail (scheduler.cpp:235-247). No caller in the repository needs a const variant (no const_cast on query/view/scheduler anywhere). Making them const with mutable caches would make concurrent const use of one query a data race, so this is an API policy choice, not a defect. *Fix:* Options: (a) mark mMatches and mSeenArchetypeCount (and the scheduler phase cache) mutable and make the accessors const; this breaks the const = safe-to-share-across-threads expectation. (b) Add const overloads that never refresh or rebuild: `std::span<const stage_info> scheduler::stages(system_phase) const` returns the last built stages (empty when dirty), and query::entity_count() const counts the cached matches only, documented as possibly stale. (c) Keep as is and document in query.h/scheduler.h that the counters refresh lazily and are therefore non-const. Recommendation: (c) for query/view and, only if an editor panel actually needs it, (b) for scheduler::stages.

### engine (1)

- **F077** MAJOR A module system's unknown read/write component names are dropped silently, shrinking its access set (`engine/src/engine/module_consumers/ecs_module_consumer.cpp:59`). Still present. to_system_desc drops names the registry does not know (ecs_module_consumer.cpp:52-63), and validate() only rejects an empty access set (:140-143). Neither design 01_ecs.md 4.7 nor 07_modules.md 9 says what an unresolved name means. Modules support optional dependencies (module_manifest.h:41), and a system may legitimately name a component of an optional dependency that is absent; rejecting outright would make such a module fail to load. *Fix:* Option A (recommended): reject in validate(). For each record->reads[i] / writes[i], resolve with mRegistry->find(name). When it is invalid and the name equals none of core::string_id(std::string_view(c->name)) over extensions_of<ecs::component_type_desc>(set), return conflict(module, std::format("system '{}' names component '{}', which is not registered", record->name, name.to_string())). Document in docs/handbook/modules/engine.md that a module system may only name components that are registered when its module attaches: its own, the engine's, or a hard dependency's. A stale mask is a silent data race, and the Debug-only undeclared-write assert (chunk_view.cpp:20, 81) does not cover reads. Option B: log a WARN per unresolved name at attach and keep loading. This adds no behaviour change and surfaces typos, but the race remains. Option C: reject unresolved writes and warn on unresolved reads.

### managed (1)

- **F676** MINOR AudioSource.Exists throws instead of returning false when the audio components are not registered (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/AudioSource.cs:24`). Still present: AudioSource.cs:24 (and Rigidbody.cs:25, CharacterController.cs:24) call Entity.HasComponent, which uses ComponentType<T>.RequireId (Entity.cs:133-137, ComponentType.cs:34-43). That throws InvalidOperationException when the native type is unregistered, which happens when CONJURE_WITH_AUDIO/PHYSICS is off (application.cpp:426-431, audio_subsystem.cpp:77). Design 15 section 6 covers this case only partly: layout drift is InvalidOperationException, a compiled-out subsystem is NotSupportedException, and B11 only says predicates do not throw for dead entities. Whether Exists answers false or throws is the owner's call, and only a build with the subsystem compiled out can test it. *Fix:* Options: (A) Exists answers false when the component type is unknown: `ComponentType<X>.Id != 0 && Entity.HasComponent<X>()` in the three facades, while Entity.HasComponent<T> keeps throwing so mirror drift stays loud; (B) throw NotSupportedException from Exists when the id is 0, following the 'compiled out' row of 15 section 6; (C) keep the current behaviour and document it. Recommendation: A, together with the ComponentType caching of F449 so a 0 id stays cheap. Add a sentence to csharp-api.md that Exists is false in builds without the subsystem. The regression test needs a CONJURE_WITH_AUDIO=OFF (or physics OFF) CI leg, or a test seam that leaves the component unregistered.

### scene (1)

- **F635** MINOR scene's transform and lifetime functions are asymmetric with their siblings (`engine/include/conjure/scene/scene.h:406`). The asymmetries exist (scene.h:396 vs 401/406, 285/291, prefab.h:204), but each signature is exactly what 13_scene.md specifies (lines 557-558, 576, 686). The destroy/destroy_now split is deliberate (S6: queued vs immediate cascade). Renaming them, reordering add_node or changing the return type of local_transform_of is a public API change: bindings, editor commands, runtime and tests would all have to follow. *Fix:* Option (a): do nothing and record the naming as intentional. Option (b), non-breaking: add an overload 'core::result<void> set_local_transform(ecs::entity target, const math::transform &value)' that forwards local_transform::from_transform(value), so set_local_transform(e, *local_transform_of(e)) compiles. Option (c), breaking: rename destroy/destroy_now to destroy_entity/destroy_entity_now, return std::optional<local_transform> from local_transform_of, and reorder add_node(name, parentIndex). Every caller in bindings, editor, runtime and docs then has to change. Recommendation: (b) only. Keep destroy/destroy_now, which pairs with entity_handle::destroy and the design, and keep add_node as it is.

## Fixed

### editor (106) (commit `fa83689`)

- **F083** BLOCKER --ai-stdio: engine logs are written to stdout and corrupt the NDJSON protocol stream (`engine/src/editor/core/protocol/protocol_server.cpp:60`). Added editor_options::stdout_carries_protocol(): true for --ai-stdio, --exec without --out, and --export. When it is true, main.cpp turns off the console sink and adds a callback_log_sink that writes format_log_line output to stderr. In-process tests are unaffected. Updated HELP_TEXT, ai-protocol.md, headless-and-commands.md and the handbook.
- **F085** BLOCKER The default script.create template ('Behaviour') does not compile (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_script.cpp:94`). BEHAVIOUR_TEMPLATE's OnUpdate now does `Vector3 step = Transform.Forward * (Speed * deltaTime); Transform.Translate(step);`. It no longer assigns Transform.Position (CS1612), and the no-op `* 0.0f` is gone. OnCreate, the attributes and EMPTY_TEMPLATE are unchanged (commands_script.cpp).
- **F106** BLOCKER A scene made with File > New Scene can never be saved from the UI (`engine/src/editor/ui/editor_workspace.cpp:57`). File > Save Scene and Ctrl+S now call editor_workspace::request_save_scene: scene.save when the scene has a path, otherwise a Save Scene As modal. There is also a new File > Save Scene As... item. The modal sends scene.save_as through save_scene_as(), shows the error text on failure, and supports Enter, Save, Cancel and Escape. New public API: request_save_scene, request_save_scene_as, save_scene_as, save_as_pending, save_as_path, save_as_error. The Ctrl+S block was removed from toolbar_panel.cpp.
- **F132** BLOCKER --ai-stdio and --exec write log lines to stdout, corrupting the NDJSON protocol stream (`engine/src/core/foundation/log.cpp:207`). Same defect as F083 and fixed by the same change. Unlike F132's plan, stdout_carries_protocol also covers --export, as F083 planned, because run_export writes its response to stdout.
- **F188** BLOCKER Any non-UTF-8 string in a protocol reply aborts the editor (FATAL assertion); a project folder with one accented letter is enough (`engine/src/editor/core/protocol/protocol_message.cpp:150`). protocol_message.cpp now serialises through serialise_value. It uses core::json::to_string with no deep copy. When that refuses invalid UTF-8, a sanitised copy is written instead, with keys and strings repaired via to_utf32/from_utf32 (U+FFFD). errorMessage goes through valid_utf8. This covers response results and details, event data and request args. One sentence added under 'The wire' in ai-protocol.md.
- **F029** MAJOR `conjure_editor --export <dir>` produces a folder with no runtime, shared library, nethost or shaders, and reports a runtimePath that does not exist (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_project.cpp:192`). Added editor_options::exportDistribution (--distribution <dir>, needs --export, help line). editor_application::run_export forwards it as distributionDirectory. project.export now adds a warning that the runtime executable, library and shaders were not copied when no distribution is given. Deviation from the plan: the warning is skipped when the client passed copyRuntime explicitly. Documented in docs/api/editor/index.md, headless-and-commands.md and the handbook.
- **F037** MAJOR conjure_editor has no UTF-8 code-page manifest and takes narrow argv, unlike conjure_runtime (`editor/CMakeLists.txt:20`). conjure_editor now reads its arguments as UTF-8. The new editor/src/editor_arguments.h declares utf8_arguments(argc, argv). The Windows backend uses CommandLineToArgvW(GetCommandLineW()) plus core::str::from_utf16 and falls back to the narrow argv; the POSIX backend copies argv. The new editor/src/conjure_editor.manifest sets activeCodePage UTF-8. editor/CMakeLists.txt picks the backend per platform and adds the manifest and shell32 on Windows, the same way the runtime does. main.cpp uses utf8_arguments.
- **F079** MAJOR conjure_editor exit codes contradict their documentation in three ways (`editor/src/main.cpp:43`). make_engine_desc now returns engine_error::BAD_ARGUMENTS "unknown option(s): ..." when the engine leaves unrecognisedArguments. main.cpp has a new startup_exit_code helper. It returns 3 for BAD_ARGUMENTS. For INVALID_CONFIG it returns 3 unless the merge without engine flags also fails with INVALID_CONFIG, which returns 2 (the runtime's rule). Everything else returns 2. On 3, main prints a --help hint. Exit code 4 (device lost) is now documented in the main.cpp header comment, HELP_TEXT, ai-protocol.md and the handbook.
- **F086** MAJOR An atomic editor.batch is not rolled back while playing (and inside undo/redo) (`engine/src/editor/core/command_dispatcher.cpp:513`). Reordered command_dispatcher::record_outcome so a running batch always collects its children's inverses, even while playing or while the batch runs as an undo/redo step. Only after that does it check mSuppressRecording and the frozen state. run_batch pushes the collected entry only when mSuppressRecording == 0. The frozen arm in run_batch is now reachable and reports one UNRECORDED_PLAY_EDIT per batch.
- **F092** MAJOR <project>/.conjure/ai_port is written only for the start-up project (`engine/src/editor/core/protocol/protocol_server.cpp:45`). Added protocol_server::set_project_root(root). It removes the current port file and, while TCP is bound, writes <root>/.conjure/ai_port; an empty root only removes it. start() and stop() now go through the new private helpers write_port_file and remove_port_file. editor_application::apply_pending_project_open calls set_project_root after the engine restart. Documented in ai-protocol.md and the handbook.
- **F094** MAJOR --ai-token does not gate the event stream: unauthenticated clients receive every default event (`engine/src/editor/core/protocol/protocol_server.cpp:298`). protocol_server::deliver_event returns early for an unauthenticated client, so with --ai-token no event (events.dropped and client.connected included) reaches a client before a valid session.hello. Updated the 'Require a token' row in ai-protocol.md and the handbook.
- **F095** MAJOR console_buffer returns records whose text was overwritten after the text ring wraps a second time (`engine/src/editor/core/console_buffer.cpp:149`). In console_buffer::push_locked, a text-ring wrap now first drops every held entry at or past the old head (the previous lap's leftovers). The overlap loop now drops the oldest entry while its textOffset is in [offset, newHead). Each record takes at least one byte of the ring, so empty records get distinct offsets and cannot stop the eviction early. No header change.
- **F096** MAJOR Undo of a bulk create is quadratic: 10,000 entities take ~11 s (create takes 0.16 s) (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/command_support.cpp:258`). outermost() now uses an unordered_set of the requested entities plus a seen set, and walks parent_of upwards at most MAX_HIERARCHY_DEPTH steps. Same output and order as before, at O(n x depth). Added the overload restore_inverse(scene, root, position); the 2-argument form forwards to it. entity_destroy_many gets each root's position in one pass (sibling_index, or a scene.roots() map), stable-sorts, and builds each inverse at that position, so no per-root roots() scan is left. Undo of create_many with 10,000 entities: the existing 10,000-entity case now takes about 1.0 s in total. The sweep measured about 11 s for that undo alone.
- **F097** MAJOR Generated Game.csproj globs obj/**: every rebuild warns CS2002 and a second configuration breaks the build with CS0579 (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_script.cpp:60`). CSPROJ_TEMPLATE's compile item is now `<Compile Include="**/*.cs" Exclude="bin/**;obj/**" />`. ensure_csproj still writes only a missing file, so an existing generated Game.csproj keeps the old line. No migration; mention this in the commit message.
- **F101** MAJOR A module whose manifest name is "editor" removes every built-in command when it unloads (`engine/src/editor/core/editor_module_consumer.cpp:164`). editor_module_consumer::validate refuses a module named 'editor' (REGISTRATION_CONFLICT; BUILTIN_OWNER constant). detach() never calls remove_owned_by for that owner. describe_module (commands_modules.cpp) counts commands only for a LOADED module. Documented in headless-and-commands.md.
- **F102** MAJOR An unknown command with a long name freezes the editor: didYouMean runs Levenshtein over the whole name (`engine/src/editor/core/command_registry.cpp:173`). closest_names returns no suggestions for a name over 128 bytes (MAX_SUGGESTION_NAME_LENGTH). command_dispatcher has a new echoed_name helper: it quotes at most 128 bytes, cut back to a UTF-8 code-point boundary, followed by "...". It is used in the unknown-command response and in run_batch's unknown-child error. Documented in ai-protocol.md, headless-and-commands.md, the handbook and the closest_names summary.
- **F108** MAJOR Pressing Delete while renaming an entity destroys the selected entities (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/hierarchy_panel.cpp:108`). The hierarchy's Delete key only fires when no rename is in progress, io.WantTextInput is false and the rows child has focus. It uses IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Delete, false), so holding the key does not repeat. The same guard covers the new panel-level F2.
- **F109** MAJOR Hierarchy rows keep the old name and enabled state after a rename or enable/disable (`engine/src/editor/ui/hierarchy_cache.cpp:36`). New hierarchy_cache::refresh_labels(scene, first, last) noexcept re-reads names and the DISABLED flag for rows already built. It skips entities that no longer exist, clamps the range and never rebuilds. hierarchy_panel calls it for each clipper range. update() invalidates the cache when context.revision() changes while a filter is active.
- **F110** MAJOR The Console shows the oldest 5,000 records, so new log lines stop appearing once 5,000 match (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/console_panel.cpp:76`). Added console_query::newest. In that mode console_buffer::read walks back from the newest entry to sinceSeq, then reverses, so callers still read oldest first. console_panel sets newest = true and exposes newest_seq_shown(). Documented in headless-and-commands.md.
- **F112** MAJOR Dragging a selection that contains a parent and its child moves the child twice (`engine/src/editor/ui/gizmo_controller.cpp:145`). gizmo_controller::gather_selection skips entities that have a selected ancestor (new has_selected_ancestor helper walks parent_of and checks the selection by guid). The centroid and pivot now cover top-level selected entities only. The gizmo_controller.h file comment documents this.
- **F113** MAJOR Rotation fields round-trip quaternion -> Euler -> quaternion every frame, so dragging pitch past +-90 degrees flips or sticks (`engine/src/editor/ui/property_drawer.cpp:259`). draw_quat caches the displayed Euler degrees and the quaternion they produced in ImGui state storage, keyed under PushID(label). It re-derives angles from the stored quaternion only when that quaternion differs from the cache (abs(dot) < 1 - 1e-6). An edit stores the new degrees and quaternion, so yaw can pass 90 degrees without flipping.
- **F114** MAJOR Editing any unranged u64 field hits undefined behaviour: std::clamp with hi < lo on x64 (assert in Debug, 2^63 written otherwise) (`engine/src/editor/ui/property_drawer.cpp:116`). draw_integer was replaced by draw_integer_as<i64\|u64> with a saturate_to<Integer>(f64) helper. Bounds and the current value are clamped in the field's own domain, so no out-of-range f64-to-integer cast is left. install_defaults uses draw_signed<i8..i64> and draw_unsigned<u8..u64> with std::numeric_limits ranges.
- **F115** MAJOR Ctrl+Z, Ctrl+Y, Ctrl+S, F5 and Shift+F5 stop working when the Toolbar panel is closed, collapsed or an unselected dock tab (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/toolbar_panel.cpp:24`). Ctrl+Z, Ctrl+Y, Ctrl+S, F5 and Shift+F5 moved from toolbar_panel::draw_contents into a private editor_workspace::handle_global_shortcuts, called from draw() right after draw_menu_bar. Every shortcut is submitted every frame. Ctrl+S goes through request_save_scene (F106). The hierarchy's focus-gated Delete stays where it was.
- **F138** MAJOR Asset paths with '..' escape the assets root; asset.restore overwrote project.json and left files behind on failure (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_asset.cpp:56`). New helpers in commands_asset.cpp. contained_relative_path() normalises the relative part with path_utils::normalize and refuses '..', '../x', an absolute path or an empty path (INVALID_ARGS). checked_asset_path() is normalise_asset_path + split_asset_path + that check, rebuilt with make_asset_path so a '#sub' part is kept. It is used in asset_import (the in-project candidate and the copy-in destination, checked before anything is read or written), asset_create_folder, asset_delete's folder branch, asset_create_material and asset_restore. file_path_for runs the same check before joining the mount root. Lookups (resolve_asset) keep normalise_asset_path. A dot segment that stays inside the mount is folded ('props/../kept' -> assets://kept). Behaviour change: a relative asset.import source that leaves assets:// ('../x') is now INVALID_ARGS instead of being tried as a cwd-relative copy-in.
- **F147** MAJOR Delete key while renaming an entity destroys the selection (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/hierarchy_panel.cpp:108`): same as F108.
- **F155** MAJOR prefab.create accepts '../' and writes outside the assets root, registering an asset named assets://../x (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_prefab.cpp:29`). prefab_create now calls check_prefab_path() right after normalise_prefab_path, before find_by_path and any write. It refuses a path that does not split as an asset path, an empty relative part, a backslash, an absolute path, or any '.' or '..' segment, with the planned INVALID_ARGS message (commands_prefab.cpp).
- **F156** MAJOR history.undo inside a client's open transaction undoes an entry from before the transaction and loses it from redo (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_history.cpp:68`). Added undo_stack::has_open_transaction(). history.undo, history.redo and history.clear answer INVALID_REQUEST while any client's transaction is open. Documented in headless-and-commands.md and the README.
- **F157** MAJOR editor.quit inside an --exec file neither stops the run nor sets the exit code (`engine/src/editor/core/headless_runner.cpp:193`). run_requests stops after a request when application.quit_requested(); a non-zero editor.quit exit code becomes summary.exitCode, default 0 still reports 1 after an earlier --keep-going failure. Header comment, run_requests summary, ai-protocol.md and handbook updated.
- **F158** MAJOR --ai-stdio editor keeps running forever after stdin reaches EOF (`engine/src/editor/core/editor_application.cpp:148`). protocol_server now sets mStdioClosed when a STDIO transport reports its client closed, exposes it as stdio_closed(), and resets it in stop(). editor_application::run calls request_quit(0) after a frame when the session is headless with --ai-stdio, has no TCP server, and stdio_closed() is true. Documented in HELP_TEXT, ai-protocol.md, headless-and-commands.md and the handbook.
- **F175** MAJOR --exec bounds asynchronous commands by 1,000,000 frames, not by --timeout-seconds, so slow async commands fail with TIMEOUT (`engine/src/editor/core/headless_runner.cpp:135`). Removed MAX_FRAMES_PER_REQUEST from headless_runner.h. The async pump is now bounded in wall time on the engine clock: the dispatcher timeout times two, plus 5 s, and engine() is re-fetched every frame. If the runner gives up, or an engine frame fails, it answers TIMEOUT/INTERNAL and ends the run even with --keep-going, because the ticket may still be pending.
- **F524** MAJOR The inspector addresses components by the last name segment, which can be ambiguous or hit an alias of another type (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/inspector_panel.cpp:123`). The inspector sends the registered type name for component.remove and component.set, and uses it for the coalesce key; the short name is kept for the header only. In resolve_path (commands_component.cpp), an exact registered leading run (qualified_component) is tried before the alias/short/suffix reading. Documented in headless-and-commands.md.
- **F344** MINOR The editor's documented exit codes do not match what it returns on a startup failure (`engine/src/editor/core/editor_options.cpp:33`). Fixed by the F079 change: EXIT_STARTUP_FAILURE = 2 in editor/src/main.cpp replaces INIT_FAILURE (1) for a failed initialise().
- **F471** MINOR The editor reads narrow argv without a UTF-8 manifest; the runtime was fixed for this, the editor was not (`editor/src/main.cpp:60`): same as F037.
- **F474** MINOR editor.quit inside --exec neither stops the run nor sets the exit code (`engine/src/editor/core/editor_application.cpp:330`): same as F157.
- **F475** MINOR --exec rejects command files with a UTF-8 BOM (what Windows PowerShell 5.1 writes) (`engine/src/editor/core/headless_runner.cpp:203`). parse_command_file strips a leading UTF-8 BOM before both the array and NDJSON branches; doc comment, ai-protocol.md and handbook mention it.
- **F476** MINOR ${project} expands to the start-up project for the whole --exec run (`engine/src/editor/core/headless_runner.cpp:111`). ${project} is expanded per request from context.project() (the context survives restarts via rebind) instead of a root captured before the loop; ai-protocol.md and run_requests summary updated.
- **F477** MINOR --ai-token makes session.hello fail for --exec files and other in-process clients while their other commands run (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_session.cpp:20`). session_hello checks the token only for transport clients, i.e. those known to protocol_server::client(id). --exec files, editor.exec_file and other in-process clients are trusted. Documented in ai-protocol.md.
- **F478** MINOR --timeout-seconds 0 is accepted and makes every asynchronous command time out immediately (`engine/src/editor/core/editor_options.cpp:103`). --timeout-seconds now rejects 0 as well as negative/non-finite values ('is not a positive number'); help text says '> 0', header doc updated.
- **F480** MINOR script.list reports MSBuild's generated obj/ files as scripts (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_script.cpp:433`). New script_sources(root) uses an explicit recursive_directory_iterator with an error_code and increment(error), and disable_recursion_pending() for top-level bin/ and obj/. script.list and scripts_root_empty both use it, so MSBuild output is neither listed nor keeps Game.csproj alive. A nested Sub/obj directory is still scanned as source.
- **F481** MINOR editor.exec_file: private client leaks an open transaction, keeps running after its issuer disconnects, and carries dead members (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_editor.cpp:94`). exec_file_task stops and releases its client once its own reply is no longer valid (cancelled or timed out). release_client() calls dispatcher().cancel_client(privateClient), which commits a transaction the file left open and drops its pending requests. Removed the write-only mWaitingFrames and the dead clientId constructor parameter. README updated.
- **F482** MINOR input.inject: a tap of several controls releases only the last one, and a late validation error leaves earlier events injected (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_input.cpp:133`). input_inject now parses then injects. Every argument is turned into a std::vector<platform::input_event> plus a releases vector, and any INVALID_ARGS returns before anything is injected. All events are injected afterwards. tap_release_task holds and injects every release, so a tap of a key and a mouse button releases both. read_vec3_2d errors are now prefixed with args.position / args.scroll. The summary says so.
- **F483** MINOR view.set_camera applies part of its arguments before failing (`engine/src/editor/core/editor_camera.cpp:220`). editor_camera::apply_json now validates every member into locals (position, target, fov, orthographic, height, near, far) and writes the camera only after all checks pass, so a failed view.set_camera leaves the camera unchanged (D4).
- **F484** MINOR `parent: null` is accepted by entity.set_parent but rejected by entity.create / create_many / restore (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_entity.cpp:646`). Added OPTIONAL_ENTITY_REF_SCHEMA ({type:[string,null], description: 'guid or hierarchy path; null = scene root'}) to command_support.h. entity.create, entity.create_many, entity.restore and entity.set_parent use it for 'parent'. Regenerated docs/generated/editor_protocol.json.
- **F485** MINOR Schema 'integer' accepts 2.0 but the handlers' u32_or rejects it (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/command_support.cpp:73`). u32_or accepts any number and relies on as_i64(-1) and the u32 bound, so 2.0 reads as 2 while 2.5, -1, 1e20 and 4294967296 are still refused. The physics layer reader in commands_physics.cpp accepts whole reals the same way. The schema validator is unchanged.
- **F486** MINOR INVALID_REQUEST answers carry id null even when the request had a usable id (`engine/src/editor/core/protocol/protocol_server.cpp:133`). Added usable_id_of(line) to the anonymous namespace. An INVALID_REQUEST answer now echoes an integer or string id when the line was a JSON object. PARSE_ERROR and MESSAGE_TOO_LARGE keep id null. ai-protocol.md error table updated.
- **F487** MINOR entity.get reports an unset tag as "#0000000000000000" (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_entity.cpp:555`). entity.get now reports tag "" when tag_of() is the null id. entity.find maps {tag:""} to the null id, so it finds untagged entities instead of matching the hash of "".
- **F488** MINOR selection primary() is the lowest guid, not the entity the user selected first or last (`engine/src/editor/core/selection_set.cpp:108`): same as F657.
- **F489** MINOR Picking includes disabled entities and maxDistance is not applied to mesh hits (`engine/src/editor/core/picker.cpp:248`). gather_pick_candidates skips entities that are not scene::is_active. pick() also drops a narrow-phase hit beyond context.maxDistance. picker.h comments and headless-and-commands.md updated.
- **F491** MINOR profiler.frame_stats: gpuMs is always null and `systems` holds phases, not systems (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_profiler.cpp:81`). The per-phase array is now 'phases' (entries unchanged). 'systems' lists every scheduler system from engine_context().scheduler->last_timings(phase) as {name, phase, ms}. gpuMs is the sum of render_stats.passTimings[].gpuMilliseconds when the renderer has any, otherwise null. Updated the result schema (phases), the summary and the file header comment, and regenerated editor_protocol.json.
- **F496** MINOR Registering the catalogue is quadratic: the schema hash is rebuilt after every add (`engine/src/editor/core/command_registry.cpp:107`). command_registry computes the schema hash lazily. New members: mutable mSchemaHash and mSchemaHashStale. add, remove and remove_owned_by now only mark the hash stale. schema_hash() is now out of line, const and allocating (no longer noexcept); it recomputes when stale. recompute_hash() is const, describe_all writes schema_hash(), and the constructor is = default. Registering the catalogue is linear again. headless-and-commands.md now names schema_hash().
- **F499** MINOR Forwarding log events scans the whole console ring under the logging mutex every frame that logged (`engine/src/editor/core/console_buffer.cpp:80`). console_buffer::read now computes how many entries to skip from sinceSeq (firstSeq = mNextSeq - mEntryCount, because sequence numbers in the ring are contiguous), instead of walking every held entry under the mutex. The seq <= sinceSeq check stays as a guard.
- **F502** MINOR Linux: the listening and client sockets are inherited by child processes (dotnet, cmake) (`engine/src/editor/core/net/socket_posix.cpp:73`). socket_posix.cpp creates sockets with SOCK_CLOEXEC where available. accept uses accept4(SOCK_CLOEXEC) under CONJURE_PLATFORM_LINUX, and set_close_on_exec (fcntl FD_CLOEXEC) runs after socket/accept/connect for macOS. socket.h documents it. This code is POSIX-only, so it was neither compiled nor run on this Windows host.
- **F503** MINOR Windows: a port in an excluded range (WSAEACCES) aborts the fallback search (`engine/src/editor/core/net/socket_win32.cpp:23`). New net::is_port_unavailable_error(int) declared in socket.h: true for WSAEADDRINUSE and WSAEACCES on Windows, and for EADDRINUSE on POSIX. Both socket_error functions use it to return ALREADY_EXISTS, so the fallback port search moves past reserved or excluded ports. Comment on listen_loopback updated.
- **F505** MINOR Unchecked write errors (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_script.cpp:163`). script write_text now close()s the file and returns IO_ERROR on failure. When the output stream fails, run_requests logs an ERROR, counts the request that ran, stops, and exits with RUNTIME_FAILURE (2). register_scene_file logs a WARN when the scene import fails or reports failed sources. Exit code 2 documented in ai-protocol.md, the handbook and the headless_runner.h comment.
- **F506** MINOR base64_decode accepts malformed input (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/command_support.cpp:454`). base64_decode now counts symbols ('=' included) and padding. It refuses a third '=', a data character after '=', a length that is not a multiple of 4, and non-zero bits after the last byte. Whitespace is still skipped. Every base64_encode output and the existing round trips still decode.
- **F508** MINOR One undo/redo step bumps the revision and emits scene.changed twice (or N+1 times) (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_history.cpp:45`). Added command_dispatcher::mSuppressRevision, incremented in dispatch_inverses and dispatch_replays. Inverses and replays (including an editor.batch inverse and the async branch) neither bump the revision nor emit scene.changed. history.undo/redo do both once per step. The failure branch now emits one 'Undo X (failed)' / 'Redo X (failed)' scene.changed. README step 3 updated.
- **F511** MINOR script.compile: `configuration` is unvalidated and concurrent builds are not prevented (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_script.cpp:571`). script.compile checks configuration with is_safe_tool_argument (INVALID_ARGS). It answers BUSY while a compile is pending, tracked by a weak token in editor_context that compile_task owns. editor_context also records last_script_configuration on success (reset on rebind). script.reload defaults to bin/<configuration>/Game.dll and answers PATH_NOT_FOUND when the file is missing. A load failure of an existing file is IO_ERROR instead of COMPILE_FAILED. The script_reload.json fixture now expects PATH_NOT_FOUND. Documented in ai-protocol.md and headless-and-commands.md.
- **F512** MINOR Duplicated helpers across command files (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_prefab.cpp:73`). import_now, file_path_for and contained_relative_path moved into command_support.{h,cpp} using the asset wording, and both copies deleted. Prefab paths now get the same canonical file path the asset database registers. Added exec_summary::record(ok, index), used by run_requests and exec_file_task. Removed the dead '.' test in reflection_path.cpp. The run_batch frozen arm the plan called dead was kept: after F086 it is reachable and is the single per-batch UNRECORDED_PLAY_EDIT notice.
- **F514** MINOR view.pick uses a fixed 16:9 aspect unless the client passes one (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_view.cpp:142`). editor_context gains viewport_aspect() and set_viewport_aspect(), plus a DEFAULT_VIEWPORT_ASPECT of 16:9 constant; the setter ignores non-finite or non-positive values. viewport_panel reports its aspect every frame. view.pick defaults aspect to context.viewport_aspect(), and DEFAULT_PICK_ASPECT was removed. Documented.
- **F515** MINOR ImGuizmo::IsOver() is read on frames where the gizmo was not drawn, so a stale `over` flag can block picking (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/viewport_panel.cpp:54`). gizmo_controller has mOverHandle and is_over(). draw() clears the flag first and sets it from ImGuizmo::IsOver() after Manipulate. viewport_panel calls mGizmo.draw every frame and tests !mGizmo.is_over() instead of the stale ImGuizmo::IsOver(). The ImGuizmo include was dropped from viewport_panel.cpp.
- **F516** MINOR The `drop on empty area to reparent to the root` target can never be hit (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/hierarchy_panel.cpp:90`). The root drop target below the rows is now a Dummy that fills the remaining content region, at least one text line tall. Its id is still 0, so clicking the empty area still clears the selection.
- **F517** MINOR The list clipper is given the wrong row height, so long hierarchies scroll with a jumping scrollbar (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/hierarchy_panel.cpp:82`). clipper.Begin uses ImGui::GetFrameHeightWithSpacing(), the real row height. The same rowHeight drives the new scroll-to-row.
- **F518** MINOR Camera drags stop when the cursor leaves the panel, and pan speed ignores zoom (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/viewport_panel.cpp:45`). Added editor_camera::world_units_per_pixel(viewportHeight). viewport_panel handles camera input while the surface is hovered or active, so a drag continues outside the panel; picking still needs hover. Pan now uses world_units_per_pixel in place of the fixed PAN_UNITS_PER_PIXEL, which was removed. Documented.
- **F520** MINOR ImGui::Separator() after SameLine() draws a horizontal rule through the button row (Toolbar and Viewport toolbar) (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/toolbar_panel.cpp:83`). Added support::vertical_separator() (SeparatorEx with the Vertical flag) in ui_support.h/.cpp. It replaces the two ImGui::Separator() calls in toolbar_panel.cpp and the two in viewport_panel.cpp's toolbar.
- **F521** MINOR Inline rename: clicking away after typing neither commits nor cancels and focus is stolen back every frame; F2 renames the hovered row, not the selection (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/hierarchy_panel.cpp:152`). New public hierarchy_panel::begin_rename and is_renaming. The rename field takes focus once (mRenameFocusPending) instead of every frame. Deactivation after an edit commits (only when the name changed), and Escape or any other deactivation cancels. F2 is now a tree-level key that renames the primary selection when the rows child has focus. The context menu's Rename uses begin_rename, which also reveals the row and scrolls to it. The rename UI is in a new private draw_rename_field.
- **F522** MINOR Shift-click toggles one row like Ctrl-click (no range select) and a viewport pick never reveals the entity in the tree (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/hierarchy_panel.cpp:173`). Shift-click selects the visible range from mAnchor (set by every plain or Ctrl click) through select_range, and adds to the selection when Ctrl is also held. The clicked row is named last, so it becomes the primary. update() reveals a primary selected outside the tree and scrolls to its row via mScrollTarget/scroll_to_target. mSelectedHere is cleared on every update. Note: selection_set::primary() is the last-named guid in this tree, not the first as the rationale says, so the Ctrl-click test uses a Ctrl-click that removes a non-primary row.
- **F523** MINOR Gizmo snapping is unreachable: set_snap has no caller outside its test (`engine/src/editor/ui/gizmo_controller.cpp:72`). Snap defaults are now 0.5 units, 15 degrees and 0.1. Added getters snap_enabled, snap_translate, snap_rotate_degrees and snap_scale, and set_snap now has a summary. The viewport toolbar has a Snap checkbox and one clamped step DragFloat for the current operation (universal uses the translate step), in a new private draw_snap_controls(). It calls set_snap with each step at least 0.001.
- **F526** MINOR Integer fields with only one bound become unusable sliders; i32 casts overflow for large u32 bounds; float min/max are ignored unless both are set (`engine/src/editor/ui/property_drawer.cpp:88`). Fixed in the same rewrite as F114. Integers get a SliderScalar only with both bounds inside ImGui's half-range limits, otherwise an AlwaysClamp DragScalar. There are no i32 casts. draw_number treats each bound on its own: a slider only with both, otherwise an AlwaysClamp DragFloat with open ends (bounds saturated to the f32 range). f64 values are clamped after InputDouble. range_of was removed.
- **F528** MINOR Fixed widget labels collide when a component has two transform or two mat4 fields (`engine/src/editor/ui/property_drawer.cpp:312`). PushID(label)/PopID now scopes the fixed child labels of draw_transform, the matrix rows of draw_mat4 and the flag checkboxes of draw_enum. Visible labels are unchanged.
- **F529** MINOR Dead popup code in draw_add_component and a constant readOnly flag (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/inspector_panel.cpp:178`). Removed the dead BeginPopup/EndPopup branch from draw_add_component (a single 'if (Button) { OpenPopup; clear filter }'). Removed the constant readOnly local and pass false directly, keeping the play-mode comment. No change in behaviour.
- **F532** MINOR AI Session panel: no automatic session.hello, so with --ai-token the first typed command gets UNAUTHORISED and disconnects; focus is lost after every Enter (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/ai_session_panel.cpp:111`). ensure_connected sends session.hello (id 'ai_session_panel.hello', clientName 'AI Session panel') with the server's token when one is set. It is not counted in sent_count, and the transcript only shows '> session.hello (token hidden)'. After Enter the request field takes focus back with SetKeyboardFocusHere(-1).
- **F533** MINOR Export state lives in a process-wide static slot and leaks its event client and response sink when the dialog dies mid-export (`engine/src/editor/ui/panels/export_dialog.cpp:36`). The function-static state slot is gone. export_dialog owns a std::shared_ptr<export_state> (a private nested struct, defined in the .cpp) and the editor_context it registered with. A private release_callbacks() unregisters the response sink and the event client; both update() on completion and the destructor call it. profiler_panel's static history is now the member mHistory.
- **F534** MINOR Mistyped flags are accepted silently, and --help does not list the engine flags (`engine/src/editor/core/editor_options.cpp:118`). The rejection half was already in place from F079: editor_application::make_engine_desc (editor_application.cpp:77-92) refuses unknown options with BAD_ARGUMENTS and exit 3, covered by test_editor_application.cpp:108 and test_editor_process.cpp:150. So I did not duplicate it in parse_editor_options. The remaining defect was that --help did not list the engine flags. editor_options_help() now returns the editor text followed by engine::command_line_help(). It returns std::string instead of a noexcept string_view, and main.cpp prints it unchanged in both places. Updated the header comments, the help line and headless-and-commands.md, and renamed the test 'unknown tokens are forwarded' to 'engine flags are forwarded to the engine'.
- **F535** MINOR Wiki and comments describe UI that does not exist (`docs/api/editor/workspace-ui.md:42`). workspace-ui.md: commands vs UI state wording, Window menu -> View menu, and the viewport row (ordinary window, input surface). viewport_panel.h header comment now says it is an ordinary window with an input surface that does not show the scene yet, citing 'code sweep 2026-11-12 B10' (the report's id for the viewport blocker, the plan's F104). The ui_support.h comment no longer promises a status line. The toolbar comment (step 6) was superseded by F115 and now says the shortcuts live in editor_workspace::draw.
- **F541** MINOR WIN32_GUI on conjure_editor is a no-op (`editor/CMakeLists.txt:21`). Removed WIN32_GUI from conjure_add_executable(conjure_editor). Added a header comment explaining why the editor keeps the console subsystem in every configuration it is built in.
- **F542** MINOR Missing direct includes in the UI sources (`engine/src/editor/ui/editor_workspace.cpp:36`). Added missing direct includes: <array> (and <format>, <misc/cpp/imgui_stdlib.h>, undo_stack.h for the new code) in editor_workspace.cpp; <algorithm>, <optional>, <string> and <utility> in viewport_panel.cpp; <string> and <vector> in history_panel.cpp.
- **F588** MINOR project.export reports a runtimePath that does not exist and ignores its 'config' argument (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_project.cpp:238`): same as F760.
- **F590** MINOR view.set_camera with position equal to target is accepted and silently retargets the camera (`engine/src/editor/core/editor_camera.cpp:248`). apply_json refuses position/target closer than MIN_DISTANCE (negated >= also rejects NaN) with INVALID_ARGS before anything is written; set_look_at's fallback stays for UI callers.
- **F592** MINOR Entity names may contain '/', which makes the entity unaddressable by path (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_entity.cpp:104`): same as F648.
- **F599** MINOR asset.create_material baseColor elements are not validated (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_asset.cpp:454`). asset_create_material reads each baseColor element with as_f32(quiet_NaN) and refuses one that is not finite (INVALID_ARGS 'expected finite numbers within the f32 range'), starting from vec4(0,0,0,1). f32_or, read_vec3 and read_rotation in command_support.cpp read each number once with a NaN fallback and test that value, so 1e39 is refused instead of silently becoming 0. Added <limits> and <cmath> includes.
- **F600** MINOR asset.import copy-in does not check the read (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_asset.cpp:234`). The copy-in read is now read_source_file(). It answers ASSET_NOT_FOUND unless the source is a regular file, IO_ERROR when file_size fails, and IO_ERROR when a sized read returns fewer bytes (gcount check). The old rdbuf/stringstream read is gone.
- **F616** MINOR Hierarchy expand state is keyed by entity index without generation (`engine/src/editor/ui/hierarchy_cache.cpp:56`): same as F737.
- **F643** MINOR entity.bounds / view.focus on a parent ignore the subtree when nothing has render bounds (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_entity.cpp:629`). Added subtree_box subtree_bounds_or_boxes(scene, root) to command_support (a struct result instead of the plan's bool& out-parameter). It returns the render bounds when the subtree has any, otherwise the union of a 1 m box around every member's world position. entity.bounds uses it, and so does view.focus in the entity, entities, selection and whole-scene branches; the [-1,1] default is kept for an empty scene. Updated the entity.bounds summary and regenerated editor_protocol.json.
- **F645** MINOR exec failure log numbers requests by 0-based index, not by id or 1-based position (`engine/src/editor/core/headless_runner.cpp:170`). The exec failure WARN now reads "exec: request #<1-based> (id <id>) '<cmd>' failed: ..."; firstFailedIndex stays 0-based.
- **F646** MINOR entity.set_parent answers parent:"" for a root while entity.get answers parent:null (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_entity.cpp:457`). Cleanup pass: entity.set_parent answers parent null for a root and selection answers primary null when empty; schemas, fixtures and the protocol document regenerated.
- **F648** MINOR Entity names that cannot be addressed are accepted: '/', empty, duplicates from duplicate (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_entity.cpp:1`). Added support::check_entity_name, which refuses an empty name or one containing '/'. It applies to entity.create (args.name), entity.create_many (args.nameFormat, checked before the template is created), prefab.instantiate (args.name) and entity.rename. It also applies to the name component in component.set/set_many and in entity.create components.name. Added command_dispatcher::replaying() (true while undo/redo or a batch rollback dispatches). entity.rename and the name-component routes skip the check while replaying, and set_many's own rollback skips it too, so undo restores legacy names. Duplicate names stay allowed. Documented in headless-and-commands.md.
- **F653** MINOR view.screenshot inconsistencies: camera by path refused, misleading NOT_AVAILABLE text under --offscreen (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_view.cpp:1`). screenshot_service::parse_request accepts any non-empty camera and refuses only "". view.screenshot resolves any value other than editor/game as an entity by guid, path or name. It answers COMPONENT_NOT_FOUND when that entity has no camera component, and hands the service its guid. Under --offscreen without a device the NOT_AVAILABLE text says to run with a window. headless_editor_options.offscreen was not added: an --offscreen headless start-up enables the renderer on the null device, which needs the shader index next to the executable, and that is absent in build/mod_editor/bin. The offscreen case instead builds an editor_context marked offscreen over a bare headless engine::application.
- **F657** MINOR Selection order and primary ignore the order given (`engine/src/editor/core/selection_set.cpp:1`). selection_set now tracks mPrimary, the last non-null guid named by set() or add(). A change of primary alone also emits on_changed. remove() and rebuild() fall back to the lowest remaining guid through a new private refresh_primary(); clear() resets it; primary() returns it. play_controller::start adds the primary at the end of mSelectionBackup, so play.stop restores it. The guid list stays sorted. Header summaries and headless-and-commands.md describe the rule.
- **F702** MINOR script.reload is asserted as 'ok or COMPILE_FAILED' in both the unit test and the catalogue fixture, so nothing about it is pinned (`tests/editor/test_commands_script.cpp:230`). Pinned the script.reload outcomes, test-only. F511 changed the code in the same step, so the pinned missing-assembly answer is now PATH_NOT_FOUND rather than the plan's COMPILE_FAILED. An existing file reloads ok twice, and the second reloadCount is the first + 1. The script_reload.json fixture uses a single expect (PATH_NOT_FOUND) without expectAnyOf or allowErrors.
- **F703** MINOR 28 catalogue fixtures assert only ok:true, and the undo round trip never checks that the command changed anything (`tests/editor/test_command_catalogue.cpp:176`). Cleanup pass: the fixture runner checks that every mutating fixture changes the scene or project before undo restores it; fixtures fixed to actually change something; expect.result subsets added.
- **F704** MINOR editor_snapshot.worldHash is a hash of the same serialised text stored next to it; the comparison adds nothing and one branch is unreachable (`tests/editor/editor_test_support.cpp:214`). Removed editor_snapshot::worldHash, its capture, and the unreachable worldHash branch of describe_difference. headless_editor::world_hash stays for its other users. Updated the snapshot summary and the test_command_catalogue.cpp header comment.
- **F705** MINOR project_file_snapshot ignores project.json and everything outside assets/ and scripts/ (`tests/editor/editor_test_support.cpp:241`). project_file_snapshot walks the whole project root but skips .conjure/. It normalises project.json through engine::project::parse and to_json, so helper-written and project::save text compare by meaning, and it REQUIREs that the listing succeeds. The header comment is updated. The catalogue round trip, including project.set_settings, passes.
- **F714** MINOR Test titles promise behaviour the bodies do not exercise (entity.find tag/layer, entity.bounds sprite) (`tests/editor/test_commands_entity.cpp:377`). Test-only. The entity.find case now adds conjure.scene.tag {value:enemy} and conjure.scene.layer {index:3} through component.add and checks {tag:enemy}, {layer:3}, {name:Cell, layer:3} and {layer:32} INVALID_ARGS.
