The bounds case is renamed to 'entity.bounds - a bare entity reports a unit box around its position'. Separate production gap, not handled here: nothing writes scene::local_bounds, so fromRenderBounds is always false. That belongs to renderer/engine (04_editor.md:982).
- **F715** MINOR input.inject tests never look at the injected input (`tests/editor/test_commands_input.cpp:40`). Test-only. The tap case now pumps one frame and checks snapshot().is_key_down(SPACE). After the release task finishes it pumps one more frame and checks the key is up. The UTF-8 case pumps after each inject and compares snapshot().text() with {0x68,0xE9,0x6C,0x6C,0x6F} and {0x65E5,0x672C}.
- **F727** MINOR Frame tasks of disconnected or timed-out clients are never removed (`engine/src/editor/core/editor_context.cpp:268`). wait_frames_task::tick returns true at once when its reply is no longer valid (client cancelled or request timed out).
- **F731** MINOR history.undo and history.redo bump the revision twice and emit two scene.changed events per step (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_history.cpp:45`): same as F508.
- **F735** MINOR A client beyond the 8-connection limit is dropped without any protocol message (`engine/src/editor/core/protocol/tcp_transport.cpp:178`). tcp_transport::accept_pending sends one best-effort BUSY line with id null ('the editor serves at most 8 clients') to a client beyond MAX_CLIENTS before the handle closes; documented in ai-protocol.md and the handbook protocol section.
- **F737** MINOR Hierarchy expansion state is keyed by entity index only and is never reset, so it leaks onto unrelated entities (`engine/src/editor/ui/hierarchy_cache.cpp:50`). hierarchy_cache expansion is now std::unordered_set<core::uuid> keyed by scene guid. set_expanded and is_expanded take the scene, and null or guid-less entities are ignored. Insert or erase marks the cache dirty only on a real change. rebuild looks up the guid only for rows with children. All callers in hierarchy_panel.cpp and the two test files were updated, and workspace-ui.md was updated.
- **F738** MINOR A scene file that is too deep is reported as INVALID_ARGS (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_scene.cpp:77`). scene_open remaps a load failure: classify_error's INVALID_ARGS becomes IO_ERROR, other codes pass through. The message is "cannot load '<path>': <error.to_string()>", so the scene error name is kept. A comment says the path was valid and the file's content is at fault.
- **F744** MINOR Script path confinement is a case-sensitive lexical prefix test: rejects valid Windows paths, and symlinks/junctions escape it (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_script.cpp:127`). script_file now uses weakly_canonical on both the scripts root and the requested path, then checks containment component by component on lexically_relative: refused if empty, '.', absolute or starting with '..'. It returns root.lexically_normal()/relative, so relative_script_path still works. This resolves symlinks and junctions and the on-disk drive and directory case. The .cs check stays lower-case only, with the message "scripts are .cs files (lower-case extension)".
- **F754** MINOR normalise_scene_path does not normalise: scene.open/save_as return and keep paths no asset has, and the .scene.json suffix test is case-sensitive (`engine/src/editor/core/editor_context.cpp:193`). normalise_scene_path canonicalises the path: it splits the scheme, runs path_utils::normalize, strips leading separators and matches the .scene.json/.json suffix case-insensitively. It answers "" for a path that leaves its mount ('..', '../x', and also a drive-letter path). scene.new (non-blank path), scene.open and scene.save_as return INVALID_ARGS "args.path: '...' is not a scene path inside a mount". Header doc and wiki updated.
- **F755** MINOR asset.import of an absolute path that already lies inside the project's assets directory copies the file to the assets root as a duplicate asset (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_asset.cpp:222`). New in_project_asset_path() in asset_import. An absolute source is mapped with weakly_canonical against project()->assets_directory() and a component-wise lexically_relative. If the file exists as assets://<relative>, the existing in-project flow applies (copied false, skipRecording). Otherwise the copy-in branch runs unchanged.
- **F757** MINOR Script path checks are case-sensitive on a case-insensitive platform (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_script.cpp:133`): same as F744.
- **F760** MINOR project.export ignores its `config` argument and reports a runtimePath that does not exist (`engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_project.cpp:203`). project_export takes runtimePath from report.copiedFiles: the first top-level entry whose path_utils::stem is 'conjure_runtime', joined to the output directory. Otherwise runtimePath is null. The ANY_OBJECT result schema is replaced by one declaring directory, runtimePath [string,null], bytes, durationMs, assetCount, steps and warnings, with additionalProperties:true. Summary updated, editor_protocol.json regenerated. Whether an unbootable export should fail is left to F139/F029.
- **F782** MINOR conjure_editor gets no $ORIGIN / @loader_path run path, unlike conjure_runtime (`editor/CMakeLists.txt:28`). editor/CMakeLists.txt now gives conjure_editor the runtime's run-path block: BUILD_RPATH/INSTALL_RPATH $ORIGIN and BUILD_RPATH_USE_ORIGIN, or @loader_path on APPLE. A comment says libconjure_shared sits next to the executable in bin/.
- **F783** MINOR Only the runtime gets the UTF-8 active-code-page manifest; the editor takes narrow argv without one (`runtime/CMakeLists.txt:36`). Fixed by the F037 change: the activeCodePage UTF-8 manifest is embedded in conjure_editor (the .rc holds only GLFW_ICON, so no RT_MANIFEST clash). The handbook's editor page now says the executable reads UTF-8 arguments and embeds the manifest, as conjure_runtime does.

### core (41) (commit `6b86359`)

- **F000** MAJOR Moved-from slot_map keeps its free-list head and corrupts memory on the next emplace (`engine/include/conjure/core/containers/slot_map.h:216`). slot_map.h: the move constructor and move assignment are now written out instead of defaulted. They take the vectors and reset the source's free-list head to INVALID_INDEX and its free and retired counts to 0, then clear the source vectors. A self-move is a no-op. The class summary and the new summaries say a moved-from map is empty (no slots) and reusable. Container README and wiki updated.
- **F001** MAJOR Moved-from bit_vector keeps mSize while its words are gone: out-of-bounds access on reuse (`engine/include/conjure/core/bit_vector.h:122`). Cleanup pass: move operations written out of line in bit_vector.cpp; a moved-from vector is empty and reusable.
- **F002** MAJOR flat_map with a transparent comparator cannot erase by mutable iterator (does not compile) (`engine/include/conjure/core/containers/flat_map.h:330`). flat_map.h: the heterogeneous erase(const KeyLike &) now also requires that KeyLike converts to neither const_iterator nor iterator (the C++23 associative rule). A mutable iterator therefore reaches erase(const_iterator). Added a summary to that overload. Container README and wiki TIP updated.
- **F004** MAJOR libc++ (macOS) file write times jitter between two stats of an unchanged file (`engine/src/core/vfs/vfs_internal.cpp:103`). vfs_internal.cpp file_time_to_nanoseconds: the libc++ branch now uses file_clock::to_sys(time). libc++'s file clock counts from the Unix epoch, so this is exact and reads no clock. The other branch keeps clock_cast. The type aliases moved into the branches, so -Wunused-local-typedef cannot fire. vfs README documents it.
- **F189** MAJOR One asset nested deeper than the classic Windows path limit makes the whole project unopenable (enumerate fails); record 09 section 4 item 15i is understated (`engine/src/core/vfs/native_file_system.cpp:278`). Added internal::always_extended_native_path (Windows: always the absolute extended form, with the existing literal-prefix normalisation; POSIX: the path unchanged). extend_native_path now calls it at or above 248 characters. native_file_system::enumerate walks from the extended root, including lexically_relative, and remove_all removes through it. vfs README and handbook updated.
- **F234** MAJOR Windows: .bat/.cmd executables are run through cmd.exe with MSVCRT quoting (argument injection) (`engine/src/core/system/process_windows.cpp:198`). validate_process_desc now rejects a NUL in the executable, in any argument (reported by index) and in any environment name or value (reported by entry index) with INVALID_ARGUMENT. In process_windows.cpp, is_batch_file takes the file name after the last separator, strips trailing dots and spaces, and compares the extension with bat or cmd ignoring ASCII case. process::start refuses such files with INVALID_ARGUMENT and a message that suggests starting cmd.exe explicitly. Documented in the process.h file comment and the executable, arguments and environment fields, the system README, docs/api/core/system.md (WARNING box) and the handbook.
- **F003** MINOR small_vector::insert of an input-iterator range recurses forever on standard libraries without P2520R0 (Apple clang 15 / libc++ 16, older libstdc++) (`engine/include/conjure/core/containers/small_vector.h:235`). small_vector.h: the single-pass branch of insert(position, first, last) no longer re-dispatches through std::move_iterator<T *>. It collects the range into a temporary small_vector, calls open_gap(index, count) and move-constructs the collected elements into the gap. A comment explains the pre-P2520 recursion. Container README notes that single-pass ranges are collected and moved in.
- **F203** MINOR small_vector::resize(count, value) and assign(count, value) read a dangling reference when value aliases an element (`engine/include/conjure/core/containers/small_vector.h:351`). small_vector.h: resize(count, value) copies 'value' into a local before reserve() when count > capacity, then fills from that copy (re-entering resize, which no longer reallocates). On the non-reallocating path nothing moves, so it still reads 'value' directly. assign(count, value) copies 'value' before clear(). Both summaries now say 'value' may refer to an element of this vector. engine/src/core/containers/README.md and the WARNING in docs/api/core/containers.md list push_back, emplace_back, single-element insert, emplace, resize(count, value) and assign(count, value) as alias-safe, and say the range overloads of insert and assign are not.
- **F204** MINOR radix sort asserts only two of the six possible overlaps although the header says all are asserted (`engine/src/core/strings/radix_sort.cpp:123`). radix_sort.cpp: spans_overlap now takes two spans of possibly different element types and compares bytes. sort_impl also asserts on values vs scratchValues, keys vs values, keys vs scratchValues, values vs scratchKeys and scratchKeys vs scratchValues. It keeps the assert and leaves the input untouched; the assert message now reads 'radix sort spans overlap each other'. radix_sort.h (file comment and the four-span summary), the wiki warning in strings-and-identifiers.md and the strings README now say that no two of the spans may overlap.
- **F205** MINOR error::category() documents 0 for a default-constructed error; it returns the core category id (`engine/include/conjure/core/error.h:117`). error.h: the category() summary now says a default-constructed error carries the core category (error_category_traits<error_code>::ID) with code NONE, and that callers should test for 'no error' with is(error_code::NONE), not category() == 0. Comment only. The behaviour is pinned by error.cpp:55-59 and test_error.cpp.
- **F206** MINOR error.h uses std::convertible_to without including <concepts> (`engine/include/conjure/core/error.h:70`). error.h: added #include <concepts> (alphabetically before <format>) for std::convertible_to.
- **F210** MINOR README claims callback_log_sink synchronises itself; it does not (`engine/src/core/foundation/README.md:37`). engine/src/core/foundation/README.md threading line now says: file_log_sink locks internally, console_log_sink relies on the C runtime's per-call stream locking, and a callback_log_sink callable is invoked concurrently from every logging thread and must be thread-safe itself.
- **F211** MINOR README says CONJURE_UNREACHABLE() is 'fatal everywhere'; in Distribution it is an optimiser hint (undefined behaviour) (`engine/src/core/foundation/README.md:17`). engine/src/core/foundation/README.md: CONJURE_UNREACHABLE() is now described as asserting then aborting in Debug and Development, even when the handler continues, and as a compiler hint in Distribution, where reaching it is undefined behaviour (matches assert.h:108-123). The testing paragraph now says platform_detect.h, types.h and api.h are macro/alias-only, non_copyable.h holds two trivial base classes, all four are in tools/style_exceptions.json, and build_info.h is generated into the build tree so the test-mirror check does not apply. The follow-ups for other owners are in notes.
- **F215** MINOR reserve(0) and the (0) constructor allocate an 8-bucket table although capacity() is documented as 0 before the first insertion (`engine/include/conjure/core/containers/flat_hash_map.h:245`). flat_hash_map.h: detail::hash_table::reserve returns early for count == 0. The sized and initializer-list constructors of flat_hash_map and flat_hash_set go through it, so reserve(0), (0) and {} no longer allocate an 8-bucket table. The summaries of hash_table::reserve, flat_hash_map::reserve, flat_hash_set::reserve (new summary) and both sized constructors now say this. Container README and the wiki reserve row updated.
- **F216** MINOR trim / trim_left / trim_right return a null default view, not a view into text, for all-trimmed input (`engine/include/conjure/core/string_utils.h:113`). string_utils.h: when nothing remains, trim and trim_left return text.substr(text.size()) (an empty view at the end of text) and trim_right returns text.substr(0, 0) (an empty view at the start). They are still constexpr and noexcept. The summaries were extended to say so.
- **F217** MINOR parse_double accepts spellings the contract excludes ('infinity', 'nan(abc)') (`engine/src/core/strings/string_utils.cpp:214`). string_utils.cpp parse_double: after the hexadecimal check, it strips one '-' (a '+' is already stripped by strip_plus). If the remaining text starts with i/I/n/N, anything other than iequals "inf" or "nan" is rejected before both the from_chars and strtod paths. The header contract is unchanged. The strings README note and the wiki parser paragraph now say only inf/nan (any case, optional sign) parse as non-finite.
- **F218** MINOR The <summary> for result<T> is attached to namespace detail; the class itself is undocumented (`engine/include/conjure/core/result.h:15`). result.h: the 'Either a value or an error' <summary> moved, text unchanged, from above namespace detail to directly above template <typename ValueType> class [[nodiscard]] result.
- **F220** MINOR rect::from_position_size mis-documents negative sizes, and rect::closest_point lacks the invalid-rectangle guard aabb has (`engine/include/conjure/core/math/rect.h:157`). rect.h: closest_point returns the point unchanged for an invalid rectangle, like aabb. Its summary says so, and the from_position_size summary now describes the actual negative-extent behaviour with the (10,10), (-4,2) example. The math README and the wiki rectangle section were updated.
- **F221** MINOR Platform #if in a non-backend source file (`engine/src/core/foundation/log.cpp:130`). New internal header engine/src/core/foundation/local_time.h declares detail::local_time_of(std::time_t) noexcept. local_time_windows.cpp wraps localtime_s and local_time_posix.cpp wraps localtime_r. foundation/CMakeLists.txt now builds a source list like core/system's and picks the backend with if(WIN32), with a comment explaining why. log.cpp lost its #if CONJURE_PLATFORM_WINDOWS block; format_log_line calls detail::local_time_of(to_time_t(timestamp)) and keeps the Apple libc++ comment. The foundation README lists the backend files.
- **F222** MINOR pool_allocator rejects types its blocks are in fact aligned for, and alignment() under-reports (`engine/src/core/memory/pool_allocator.cpp:206`). pool_allocator.cpp: after the invalid-alignment fallback, the constructor raises mAlignment to max(mAlignment, alignof(free_block)) and uses it as the stride alignment. Block size and slab layout are unchanged; grow() now also requests that alignment for the slab. fits() and alignment() therefore report the real guarantee. The pool_allocator.h constructor and alignment() summaries say the alignment is raised to at least alignof(void *).
- **F224** MINOR Unused include <charconv> in uuid.cpp (`engine/src/core/foundation/uuid.cpp:4`). uuid.cpp: removed the unused #include <charconv>. Nothing in uuid.cpp or uuid.h uses from_chars, to_chars or chars_format.
- **F226** MINOR Documented stats identity ignores the caller's own parallel_for range (`engine/include/conjure/core/jobs/job_system.h:51`). job_system.cpp parallel_for runs the caller's range 0 through run_range_inline, so it is counted in executed and inlineExecutions. The documented identity stolen + helped + inlineExecutions == executed now holds for a plain multi-range parallel_for.
- **F227** MINOR Thread-name truncation is documented wrongly (macOS too, and not on a code point boundary) (`engine/include/conjure/core/jobs/dedicated_thread.h:24`). The POSIX prepare_thread_name now steps back over UTF-8 continuation bytes, so the 15-byte cut never splits a code point; comment updated. dedicated_thread.h now says the name is truncated on a code point boundary to 15 bytes on Linux and macOS and 63 UTF-16 units on Windows.
- **F228** MINOR Comment claims invalid UTF-8 converts to length 0; it does not (`engine/src/core/jobs/thread_name.cpp:21`). Comments only. thread_name.cpp wide_length now says invalid UTF-8 counts as U+FFFD (flags 0), so 0 means an empty prefix or a failed call. The thread_name.h summary now describes both platforms: Windows cuts to 63 UTF-16 units on a code point boundary with U+FFFD substitution; POSIX cuts to 15 bytes on a code point boundary and copies bytes unchanged. Unlike the plan, it does not say POSIX may split a character, because F227 fixed that.
- **F229** MINOR Queue drains throw away the queue's capacity every time (main-thread queue and event_bus) (`engine/src/core/jobs/main_thread.cpp:91`). drain_main_thread_queue and event_bus::dispatch_queued record the count, clear the batch outside the lock, then swap the emptied buffer back if the queue is still empty. This is safe when dispatch_queued is re-entered. time README and handbook updated.
- **F231** MINOR unit_vector3 breaks the header's 'same values on every platform' promise (`engine/include/conjure/core/random.h:211`). Docs only. The random.h file comment now limits the cross-platform promise to uniform and chance. unit_vector3 takes the same two engine draws everywhere, but its x and y use std::cos/std::sin and can differ in the last bit; z is exact. Notes added to the free and member unit_vector3 summaries, docs/api/core/time-and-events.md (intro sentence and table row), handbook 1.2, and the time README random.h row (golden-test tolerance exception).
- **F236** MINOR A child whose status is lost (ECHILD) is reported as exit code 0 (`engine/src/core/system/process_posix.cpp:491`). process_posix.cpp: added mStatusLost, written before mExited under mExitMutex. A new record_lost_exit() sets it, records the exit and logs a WARN; both ECHILD branches call it. exit_code() returns KILLED_EXIT_CODE when the status was lost. process.h (exitCode and KILLED_EXIT_CODE docs), docs/api/core/system.md and the README 'POSIX reaping' bullet updated.
- **F237** MINOR Linux children inherit every non-CLOEXEC descriptor of the engine (Windows and macOS restrict inheritance) (`engine/src/core/system/process_posix.cpp:606`). process_posix.cpp: on Linux with glibc 2.34 or later, a posix_spawn_file_actions_addclosefrom_np(STDERR_FILENO + 1) action is added after the dup2 actions and the macOS block. If it fails, the file actions are destroyed and an errno_error is returned. Documented in the README inheritance bullet (including the older-libc limitation), docs/api/core/system.md and the handbook.
- **F238** MINOR Bare-name dlopen failures containing 'not found' are all classified NOT_FOUND (`engine/src/core/system/dynamic_library_posix.cpp:48`). New pure helper detail::classify_bare_name_load_failure(requested, loaderMessage) in system_internal.h/.cpp. 'symbol not found', 'undefined symbol' and 'library not loaded' give BACKEND_FAILURE. Without 'no such file' or 'image not found' the result is BACKEND_FAILURE. A glibc ': cannot open shared object file' message that names an object other than the requested one gives BACKEND_FAILURE. Otherwise NOT_FOUND. dynamic_library_posix.cpp uses it for bare names; comment updated and the unused <algorithm>/<cctype> includes dropped. README dynamic_library bullet updated.
- **F239** MINOR image(width, height) can throw std::bad_alloc for dimensions the class accepts (`engine/src/core/system/image.cpp:72`). image.h adds MAX_PIXEL_BYTES = MAX_ENCODED_BYTES and extends the constructor summary. The constructor computes the u64 pixelBytes, asserts withinLimits (both axes and the byte cap) and returns the empty image when the cap is exceeded instead of letting resize throw. docs/api/core/system.md WARNING box and constant row updated.
- **F240** MINOR parent("C:/a") returns the drive-relative "C:" and the test pins it (`engine/src/core/vfs/path_utils.cpp:189`). path_utils.cpp: strip_trailing_separators keeps the separator of a 3-character drive root, and parent() returns trimmed.substr(0, 3) when the last separator sits at index 2 after a drive prefix. Results: "C:/a" -> "C:/", "C:/" -> "C:/", "C:\\a" -> "C:\\". path_utils.h summary, docs/api/core/files.md table and vfs README updated.
- **F242** MINOR Atomic replace falls back to MoveFileExW only for two error codes (`engine/src/core/vfs/native_platform_windows.cpp:271`). New internal::rename_falls_back_to_move(u32) in native_platform.h. On Windows it is true for ERROR_INVALID_FUNCTION, ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER and ERROR_NOT_SUPPORTED; on POSIX it is always false. replace_file uses it, and the comments on rename_posix and the fallback mention ERROR_INVALID_FUNCTION (SMB/NAS redirectors, FAT/exFAT drivers). ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED is deliberately excluded. vfs README and handbook updated.
- **F244** MINOR Cross-backend rename breaks the file_system::rename contract (error codes, parent rule, atomicity) (`engine/src/core/vfs/mount_table.cpp:212`). Cleanup pass: cross-mount rename refuses a read-only source, a directory destination and a missing parent, resolves nested mounts on both sides, and undoes a new copy when the source cannot be removed.
- **F245** MINOR parse_file rejects a UTF-8 BOM while vfs::read_text_file strips it (`engine/src/core/json/document.cpp:422`). document::parse_file skips one leading UTF-8 BOM before calling parse(); parse() stays strict. Documented in the document.h parse_file summary, the json README Parsing section and the docs/api/core/json.md 'Files and strictness' section.
- **F248** MINOR has_same_layout ignores element kinds, array counts and nested type ids (`engine/src/core/reflect/type_info.cpp:229`). type_info::has_same_layout now requires equal size, alignment and field count. Field by field it compares name, kind, offset and size; arrayCount and elementKind for arrays; and the type id when value_kind() is STRUCT or ENUM (scalar ids are not compared). layout_hash() is unchanged. type_info.h summary, reflect README and handbook updated.
- **F253** MINOR Console sink test cannot fail (`tests/core/foundation/test_log.cpp:293`). New internal header engine/src/core/foundation/console_output.h, registered in the foundation CMakeLists. It declares detail::console_stream_for(log_level) noexcept (stdout below WARN, stderr from WARN up) and detail::write_console_line(FILE *, const log_message &, bool useColour). Both are implemented in log.cpp next to colour_for. console_log_sink::write now just calls them, with no public API change. Foundation README documents the seam and the test.
- **F614** MINOR unit_vector3 uses libm sin/cos although the header promises identical values on every platform (`engine/include/conjure/core/random.h:211`): same as F231.
- **F620** MINOR math::ray::from_viewport and math::frustum::from_view_projection assume forward Z while the renderer's matrices are reverse Z (`engine/include/conjure/core/math/ray.h:78`). Docs only, no API change. ray.h from_viewport says it assumes forward depth (near plane at 0, far at 1, as math::perspective builds) and to use renderer::ray_from_viewport with the renderer's reverse-Z matrices. frustum.h: the struct summary says forward 0..1 depth. from_view_projection notes that with a reverse-Z matrix NEAR_PLANE and FAR_PLANE swap and renderer::frustum_from_clip corrects them (verified against camera.cpp:67-76). corners() notes the far corners come first under reverse-Z. The math README conventions and docs/api/core/math.md (a NOTE after the conventions table, a NOTE in Frustums, the from_viewport bullet) point to renderer::clip_from_view, frustum_from_clip and ray_from_viewport, linking ../renderer/rendering-a-frame.md.
- **F710** MINOR Process tests depend on short real-time windows (`tests/core/system/test_process.cpp:424`). process_child gained a 'wait-for <path>' mode: it polls every 5 ms, exits 0 when the marker appears and exits 4 after 60 s. It is documented in the helper header and the README Testing list. The is_running case now starts wait-for and adds a releaser thread that creates the marker only after the poller has seen the child alive. The concurrent-pipes case now uses wait-for with a marker that is never created, plus a 30 s watchdog that kills the slow child. The fastSeconds < 1.5 check was dropped in favour of CHECK_FALSE(watchdogFired) and CHECK(slow still running).
- **F711** MINOR Clock checks that cannot fail (`tests/core/time/test_clock.cpp:230`). Test-only change. The past-deadline subcase adds CHECK(after - before < 1.0). The default stopwatch case sleeps until default_clock() + 2 ms and checks 0.001 <= elapsed < 5.0. The time README Testing note now names this case as wall-clock dependent too.
- **F720** MINOR Sleep-based ordering in job tests can pass without exercising the path they name (`tests/core/jobs/test_job_system.cpp:673`). Test-only change. "job_system - jobs scheduled by a running job during stop still execute" no longer sleeps. The outer job spins until stopEntered. A dedicated_thread prober schedules one probe at a time and polls it with is_complete() rather than wait(); its first inline probe proves stop() cleared accepting-jobs. The case then checks REQUIRE(stopEntered), ran == 2 and queueFullFallbacks == 0. The wait_idle case keeps its sleep with a comment saying it is a best-effort negative check and that blockerFinishedWhenInnerReturned is the deterministic assertion.

### renderer (32) (commit `76bd7a0`)

- **F045** MAJOR Split-sum BRDF LUT is baked with the roughness axis flipped relative to how forward_pbr samples it (`engine/shaders/ibl_brdf_lut.hlsl:37`). ibl_brdf_lut.hlsl now bakes roughness = max(uv.y, CONJURE_MIN_ROUGHNESS); the header comment states that texture v is roughness, with row 0 smooth, matching forward_pbr's float2(N.V, roughness) lookup.
- **F046** MAJOR Blended-only view with an environment tests against an uncleared depth buffer (`engine/src/renderer/passes/depth_prepass.cpp:28`). depth_prepass::setup now creates the depth buffer only when mShared.graph.depth is still INVALID_GRAPH_RESOURCE, so the skybox's depth buffer (already created and cleared) is reused in a blended-only view with an environment. This is the same pattern the skybox, sprite and debug passes use. Header comment updated.
- **F050** MAJOR HDRI texels above 65504 become +inf in the baked cubemaps and turn into NaN in bloom / tonemap (`engine/src/renderer/import/ibl_baker.cpp:360`). ibl_baker.cpp pack_cubemap now packs each radiance channel through pack_radiance: NaN becomes 0, and values are clamped to [0, 65504], so +inf becomes 65504. ENVIRONMENT_IMPORTER_VERSION is bumped from 1 to 2 so cached .cenv files are re-baked. The bake_environment summary and the wiki page document the clamp.
- **F052** MAJOR An invalid camera in scene data asserts inside render(), contradicting 'a bad scene never aborts a frame' (`engine/src/renderer/camera.cpp:27`). renderer.cpp gains usable_camera (valid_camera's rules, plus finite near/far/orthographic height) and sanitised_camera. sanitised_camera returns a usable camera unchanged; otherwise it clamps or resets only the fields the projection uses: near clamped to [1e-4, 1e6], far set to near + 999.9, fov clamped to [0.01, pi-0.01], orthographic height reset to its default. prepare_view uses the sanitised camera for clip_from_view, the build_region far plane, build_cascades and the view constants. It WARNs once per view through the new renderer_state::invalidCameraViews bitmask, and a valid camera clears the bit. Change from the plan: sanitising runs only for an unusable camera, so a valid camera such as near 1e-5 is never altered and never warns, whatever its unused fields hold.
- **F053** MAJOR glTF import never validates the document: out-of-range buffer views are read out of bounds and a node cycle recurses forever (`engine/src/renderer/import/gltf_importer.cpp:760`). import_bytes calls cgltf_validate right after resolve_buffers and fails with PARSE_FAILURE 'invalid document: <reason>'. image_bytes checks the buffer view range itself before copying, written to avoid overflow. The recursive import_node is replaced by import_tree, which walks the node tree with an explicit stack and keeps the same pre-order. Deviation from the plan: no 1024-level cap with truncation and a warning. The explicit stack removes the stack-overflow risk without dropping nodes. A scene that lists a cycled node was already rejected by cgltf_parse (one parent per node, scene roots must be parentless; cgltf.h:6618-6649), so cycles are only reachable when there is no scene, and validation now catches them.
- **F141** MAJOR COLOR_0 accessor with fewer than three components is read out of bounds (`engine/src/renderer/import/gltf_importer.cpp:435`). import_mesh fails with PARSE_FAILURE when COLOR_0 has neither 3 nor 4 components ('mesh ... COLOR_0 must be VEC3 or VEC4, not N components'). Before, a SCALAR or VEC2 accessor was read out of bounds. Documented in gltf_importer.h and the wiki page.
- **F401** MAJOR Tangents are transformed with the normal matrix (`engine/shaders/forward_pbr.hlsl:28`). gpu_types.hlsli adds transform_direction(instance, direction), which uses the world rows and is commented as being for surface directions such as tangents. forward_pbr.hlsl transforms the vertex tangent with it instead of transform_normal.
- **F375** MINOR renderer_desc::atlasResolution is clamped for the texture but not for the allocator, and a non-power-of-two value silently drops every local shadow (`engine/src/renderer/renderer.cpp:178`). renderer::create computes std::bit_floor(std::clamp(desc.atlasResolution, MIN_ATLAS_RESOLUTION, MAX_ATLAS_RESOLUTION)). When that differs from the input it WARNs ('atlasResolution X is not a power of two in [256, 16384]; using Y'). The result goes to both the allocator and frame_resources::create, so the uv rects and the texel size agree. desc() still reports the raw value. The shadow_atlas_allocator constructor bit_floors its edge, and its header comment says so. MIN/MAX_ATLAS_RESOLUTION live in frame_resources.h, and frame_resources.cpp's clamp uses them. The renderer.h comment and the rendering-a-frame.md renderer_desc paragraph are updated.
- **F378** MINOR Draw batching keys on the slot index only: a stale id and a live id sharing an index merge into one batch (`engine/src/renderer/renderer.cpp:615`). build_region keys draw items with meshLive / materialLive, found by comparing the resolved reference with fallback_mesh() / fallback_material(). Any id that draws the fallback (stale, unknown or pending) uses MAX_DRAW_KEY_INDEX, so a stale id never batches with the live id that reuses its slot. A comment explains why.
- **F379** MINOR Unreachable fallback for invalid bounds in build_region (`engine/src/renderer/renderer.cpp:603`). The dead invalid-bounds fallback in build_region is replaced by source.worldBounds.center(). A comment says cull_instances rejects invalid bounds, and a CONJURE_ASSERT guards the invariant.
- **F380** MINOR A failed GPU build of an upload is silent and later reported as 'not uploaded yet' (`engine/src/renderer/gpu_resource_cache.cpp:758`). upload_report gains 'failed'. In flush_uploads, every build that fails (mesh, texture, material, environment) now goes through report_build_failure: it increments report.failed and logs ERROR "<kind> '<name>' (asset <uuid>) could not be created on the device; drawing the fallback". completed still counts the record, because completed drives the byte budget. The texture branch now rebinds dependent materials even when the build failed, so they drop the buried texture for the fallback. The four resolve DEBUG lines now read 'is not ready (upload pending or failed)'. The header comments are updated to match.
- **F381** MINOR Transient pool buckets are never erased and trust a 64-bit hash; same_storage() is dead code (`engine/src/renderer/transient_pool.cpp:108`). pooled_texture / pooled_buffer store their graph_*_desc. acquire / acquire_buffer skip an entry unless entry.desc.same_storage(desc), which confirms a 64-bit hash match, and record desc on creation. end_frame walks each map with explicit iterators and erases a bucket once it is empty. A bucket_count() accessor is added, and the header comment is updated (buckets erased when emptied; hash match confirmed with same_storage).
- **F382** MINOR read_back returns a blank image as success when mapping the staging texture fails (`engine/src/renderer/renderer.cpp:1472`). read_back returns rhi_error::DEVICE_LOST when mapStagingTexture returns nullptr on a lost device, and RESOURCE_CREATION_FAILED ('the staging texture could not be mapped') otherwise. It no longer returns a blank image as success. The decode loop and unmap are now unconditional. The renderer.h read_back summary and the rendering-a-frame.md read-back paragraph are updated.
- **F383** MINOR A skipped frame keeps the ImDrawData pointer for the next render() (`engine/src/renderer/renderer.cpp:1316`). render() now takes the pointer right after the render-thread assert, ImDrawData *const imguiDrawData = std::exchange(state.imguiDrawData, nullptr), so every exit drops it: device lost, a skipped acquire, or a drawn frame. The editor block uses the local, non-editor builds get CONJURE_UNUSED in an #else, and the trailing reset is removed.
- **F387** MINOR GTAO steps use one uv step for both axes, so the sample radius is stretched by the aspect ratio (`engine/shaders/ssao_gtao.hlsl:70`). ssao_gtao.hlsl: stepUv is now float2 (radiusPixels / steps / float2(halfSize)) and horizon_cos takes a float2 stepUv, so the search radius is the same pixel distance on both axes.
- **F389** MINOR Image dimensions are checked only after the full decode (`engine/src/renderer/import/image_loader.cpp:52`). image_loader.cpp: decode_rgba8 and decode_hdr_rgb now call check_header_size, which uses stbi_info_from_memory to fail with INVALID_ARGUMENT '<caller>: WxH exceeds the 16384 texel limit' before any decode. A header stb cannot read falls through to the decode, which reports the failure. The post-decode check stays as a backstop through the shared exceeds_texel_limit helper.
- **F393** MINOR .cmat parsing silently ignores unknown keys, including the glTF spellings (`engine/src/renderer/assets/material_asset.cpp:127`). material_asset.cpp: report_unknown_keys runs after a successful parse in material_importer::import. It warns 'unknown key ... is ignored' for top-level keys outside MATERIAL_KEYS (the 14 keys to_json writes) and 'unknown texture key ...' for textures members outside TEXTURE_KEYS. The glTF spellings get a hint: baseColorFactor points to baseColourFactor, baseColor to baseColour. parse_json and the loader are untouched and no aliases were added. Documented in the wiki .cmat paragraph.
- **F394** MINOR Two different 'grey fallback' environments (`engine/src/renderer/gpu_resource_cache.cpp:140`). environment_asset.h adds FALLBACK_ENVIRONMENT_RADIANCE = 0.25f. gpu_resource_cache.cpp create_fallbacks and environment_loader::create_fallback_typed both use uniform(vec3(FALLBACK_ENVIRONMENT_RADIANCE)). The loader previously used 0.5. The header and the wiki document the constant.
- **F396** MINOR Duplicated fullscreen vertex shader and unused colour helpers in the shaders (`engine/shaders/tonemap.hlsl:20`). New engine/shaders/shared/fullscreen.hlsli (include guard, fullscreen_output, fullscreen_vs). tonemap, skybox (skybox_output renamed to fullscreen_output), blit and ibl_brdf_lut include it and keep vs_main as a one-line wrapper, so the SHADERS list and the pipeline and shader asset code are unchanged. The new header is added to DEPENDENCIES in engine/shaders/CMakeLists.txt. The unused srgb_to_linear and linear_to_srgb are deleted from colour.hlsli and its header comment is updated. fullscreen_triangle.hlsl is untouched.
- **F397** MINOR Magic numbers duplicated across files (BRDF LUT size, draw-key sentinel) (`engine/src/renderer/renderer.cpp:259`). BRDF_LUT_SIZE moved from frame_resources.cpp's anonymous namespace into frame_resources.h (inline constexpr, documented). The BRDF LUT bake viewport in renderer.cpp uses it, and the 0x3FFFFFu draw-key literals are replaced by MAX_DRAW_KEY_INDEX as part of F378.
- **F398** MINOR Every debug_draw facade call heap-allocates a temporary buffer under the mutex (`engine/src/renderer/debug_draw.cpp:186`). debug_draw::line/box/sphere/axes with durationSeconds <= 0 now write straight into mImmediate under the mutex, with no temporary buffer. Timed shapes build their buffer outside the lock and move it into a timed_entry through a new private add_timed(debug_draw_buffer&&, f32). add_timed takes the lock only for push_back, and submit() uses it for its timed path. debug_draw_buffer::append now adds other.mDropped, so the facade's drops reach the frame buffer.
- **F399** MINOR read_file_bytes reads char by char into a string, copies again, and formats errors with path.string() (`engine/src/renderer/import/file_bytes.h:32`). file_bytes.h adds detail::path_utf8, which builds the text from u8string() and so never depends on the code page. read_file_bytes now uses it in every message. It also reads the whole file at once (file_size, one stream.read, then a gcount check giving IO_FAILURE on a short read), and an empty file returns an empty vector. texture_asset::load_from_file names the asset with path_utf8(filename) and gltf_importer::import the model with path_utf8(stem). The directory reader also builds URIs as UTF-8 paths.
- **F402** MINOR render_stats::passNames holds 192 names but 8 views can execute more passes; executed()/count() then under-report (`engine/include/conjure/renderer/renderer.h:89`). limits.h adds MAX_PASSES_PER_VIEW = MAX_SHADOW_CASCADES + (2*BLOOM_MIP_COUNT - 1) + 16 = 31, documented with the 16 single passes and marked CPU-only, not mirrored in gpu_types.hlsli. The file header notes the exception. renderer.h includes limits.h and sizes passNames as MAX_VIEWS * MAX_PASSES_PER_VIEW (248). A comment above render_view_graph says new passes must be counted. The render_graph.cpp guard stays as a backstop. rendering-a-frame.md mentions the capacity.
- **F404** MINOR Spot shadow field of view is not sanitised like the light record is (`engine/src/renderer/renderer.cpp:778`). The new spot_outer_cone(light) clamps outerConeRadians to [1e-3, pi/2]. Both make_gpu_light and the spot shadow-tile fov in prepare_frame use it, so the light record and its shadow projection share one cone. A NaN cone is also replaced by the light default, which goes slightly beyond the plan so NaN user data never reaches clip_from_view either.
- **F467** MINOR GPU upload failures are discarded and counted as uploads (`engine/src/engine/subsystems/renderer_subsystem.cpp:382`). Same defect and same fix as F380: one report_build_failure path counts upload_report::failed and logs the refused build. Deviation from this item's plan: it logs ERROR, not WARN, to match F380 and the transient pool's existing ERROR for a refused texture. The engine-side calls are untouched; enqueue cannot fail.
- **F601** MINOR Standalone gltf_importer::import follows buffer/image URIs outside the model's directory (`engine/src/renderer/import/gltf_importer.cpp:719`). directory_reader builds the path from the URI as UTF-8 and lexically normalises it. An empty path, one with a root name or root directory, or one starting with '..' fails with INVALID_ARGUMENT "gltf: '<uri>' points outside the model's directory". resolve_buffers and image_bytes wrap that as NOT_FOUND carrying the message. Documented in gltf_importer.h (import) and renderer-assets.md.
- **F618** MINOR debug_draw facade and debug_draw_buffer take the 4th positional argument with different meanings (`engine/include/conjure/renderer/debug_draw.h:162`). debug_draw.h adds the documented enum class debug_depth : u8 { TESTED, OVERLAY }. Every bool depthTested parameter is now debug_depth depth = debug_depth::TESTED, in the same positions, on debug_draw_buffer::line/box/sphere/frustum/axes/grid and debug_draw::line/box/sphere/axes. push_line selects the list by enum, and append passes TESTED/OVERLAY. The test call sites in test_debug_draw.cpp and test_renderer_null_frame.cpp:564 are updated. Callers outside the renderer (bindings entries_diagnostics.cpp) use defaults and still compile; conjure_bindings was built to confirm. rendering-a-frame.md documents debug_depth with an example.
- **F713** MINOR Header fuzz loops assert only on the failure branch; a corrupted blob that parses is accepted unchecked (`tests/renderer/test_mesh_asset.cpp:349`). Test-only hardening of the three header-fuzz loops. Mesh: offsets 4-19 (version and counts) must fail; any parse that succeeds must keep the vertex, index and submesh counts. Texture: every header byte before the first mip's pixels must fail; a success must keep width, height, mip count, format and gpu_byte_size. Environment: the name length and the 16-byte sky cubemap header must fail; a success must keep gpu_byte_size and both cubemaps consistent. Offsets are computed from the blob layout. No parser gap showed up.
- **F728** MINOR transient_pool never erases empty buckets, so every distinct transient description ever used is walked every frame (`engine/src/renderer/transient_pool.cpp:169`): same as F381.
- **F745** MINOR model read_index truncates 64-bit JSON integers to u32 (`engine/src/renderer/assets/model_asset.cpp:37`). model_asset.cpp read_index reads with as_i64(INT64_MAX), so values beyond i64 land on the error path. Negative values still mean INVALID_INDEX. A value >= INVALID_INDEX (0xFFFFFFFF) fails with INVALID_ARGUMENT '<key> is out of range'. The node materials loop also uses as_i64(INT64_MAX), so over-range values fail the existing range check. <limits> is added. Documented in renderer-assets.md.
- **F746** MINOR ctex/cenv parsers accept mip chains longer than the size allows, and ctex does not tie format to colour space (`engine/src/renderer/assets/texture_asset.cpp:269`). texture_asset::parse_blob rejects an RGBA8 format that disagrees with format_for(colourSpace), and a mip count above mip_count_for(base width, height), which also rules out repeated 1x1 mips. environment read_cubemap rejects mipCount > bit_width(size) (<bit> added). Non-power-of-two sizes keep their full chain. The header comment and the wiki page are updated.
- **F747** MINOR cmesh parser does not validate floats: NaN/Inf or inverted bounds and positions are accepted (`engine/src/renderer/assets/mesh_asset.cpp:422`). mesh_asset.cpp adds is_finite, all_finite and is_valid_bounds (finite, min <= max on every axis). parse_blob rejects non-finite positions, and non-finite or inverted submesh and mesh bounds, with INVALID_ASSET. from_geometry rejects a non-finite position with INVALID_ARGUMENT, so glTF NaNs fail at import. Documented in renderer-assets.md.

### managed (23) (commit `884c51a`)

- **F066** MAJOR _pendingStart keeps destroyed instances alive, so a hot reload leaks the old load context (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Runtime/ScriptRuntime.cs:139`). ScriptRuntime.UnloadGameAssembly now prunes destroyed instances (Slot < 0) from _pendingStart before AssemblyLoader.UnloadPrevious(). A young instance destroyed by a reload therefore no longer keeps the retired load context reachable. The comment at DestroyInstances now explains the pruning.
- **F068** MAJOR OnLateUpdate can run before OnStart (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Runtime/ScriptRuntime.cs:158`). ScriptRuntime.RunPhase skips instances whose OnStart has not run yet (!instance.Started), so a script created by the UPDATE flush gets no OnLateUpdate before its OnStart. This matches native (STARTED is set only at FIXED_UPDATE/UPDATE). The RunPhase summary is updated, and the csharp-api.md lifecycle text now says what happens to a script created during OnUpdate.
- **F070** MAJOR ChunkView.Get<T>(column) performs no type or size check: a wrong T corrupts chunk memory (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/Dod/ChunkView.cs:32`): same as F161.
- **F161** MAJOR ChunkView.Get<T>(column) never checks that T is the component the column holds (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/Dod/ChunkView.cs:32`). ComponentQuery.Begin keeps the up-to-8 requested ids in an [InlineArray(8)] ColumnIds (declared in ChunkView.cs, so no new source file) and passes them to ChunkView. ChunkView.Get<T> (and so GetReadOnly) throws InvalidOperationException when ComponentType<T>.Id differs from that column's id. The cost is one compare per Get and nothing per row. The steady-state allocation test still passes. Summaries and csharp-api.md are updated.
- **F662** MAJOR [EcsComponent] structs with an enum, AssetRef or nested-struct field pass the managed check and are then refused by native (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Runtime/LayoutHash.cs:112`). New LayoutHash.DynamicSchemaJson(type, layout, out refusal). It pairs the fields with the Describe entries, writes an enum field as its underlying integer type name, and for an AssetRef or nested-struct field returns null with a refusal naming the field. SchemaJson and DynamicSchemaJson share a private WriteSchema. ScriptCatalog.RegisterComponents uses it and logs a WARN skip on refusal. [NativeComponent] mirrors keep KindEnum and FullName for their hash.
- **F162** MINOR AssetRef.Path throws NotSupportedException in an exported game although its contract says it returns "" without a database (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/AssetRef.cs:130`). Comment fix only: the AssetRef.Path summary now says it returns "" for an unknown id and throws NotSupportedException where there is no asset database (cooked or exported project, 15_bindings.md 6), so it is for tooling and logs. csharp-api.md says the same.
- **F435** MINOR Enum fields with a 64-bit (or unsigned 32-bit) underlying type are silently truncated (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Runtime/FieldBlob.cs:451`): same as F671.
- **F437** MINOR An empty LayerMask means 'every layer', so 'no layers' cannot be expressed (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/Physics.cs:259`). The 7 Physics query masks are now `LayerMask? mask = null`. MakeRay and MaskBits take LayerMask?: null means every layer, and an explicit mask passes through, so LayerMask.None hits nothing. LayerMask.All and LayerMask.None have summaries, and the Physics class summary and csharp-api.md are updated.
- **F446** MINOR Template script teleports its entity to the origin on start (`managed/Conjure.Templates/Game/Player.cs:33`). The template Player.Spawn is renamed StartOffset, and OnStart now does `transform.Position += StartOffset` with the copy-to-local idiom kept, so a placed entity stays where the scene put it.
- **F448** MINOR ComponentQuery.Begin failures surface as a bare numeric status (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/Dod/ComponentQuery.cs:66`). ComponentQuery.Begin maps the negated script_error codes 13, 14, 16 and 17 (WRONG_THREAD, NOT_IN_PHASE, NOT_INITIALIZED, NOT_SUPPORTED) to readable InvalidOperationException messages through a private BeginFailure, with a comment pointing at scripting_error.h. The NOT_IN_PHASE message lists where Begin is allowed. The Begin summary and the csharp-api.md 'Data-oriented scopes' section are updated.
- **F449** MINOR An unresolved ComponentType<T> re-runs reflection and a native lookup on every access (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/Dod/ComponentType.cs:25`). ComponentType<T> now caches what it reflects once per type: mirror or EcsComponent, the name hash, size and layout hash, or a layout-invalid flag after logging the [NativeComponent] error once. Resolve keeps the cheap native lookup, the never-cache-0 rule and the generation logic unchanged. The class summary is updated.
- **F630** MINOR User-facing C# types live in Conjure.Interop next to the marshalling records (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Interop/Records.cs:70`). ForceMode moved to Api/Rigidbody.cs, CursorMode and ButtonAction to Api/Input.Bindings.cs, AssetLoadPriority to Api/AssetRef.cs, and TransformData (with its StructLayout) to Api/Transform.cs. All are now in namespace Conjure with summaries. CastShape stays in Interop and gained a summary. The generator output and every Interop, Runtime and Dod user resolve unchanged. BindingProbes now uses Conjure.CursorMode.Hidden.
- **F660** MINOR AssetRef.IsLoaded / State / Version are documented as never throwing but throw on a type mismatch or without an asset manager (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/AssetRef.cs:76`). Comment fix only: the AssetRef.IsLoaded summary now states it throws ConjureNativeException (AssetTypeMismatch) on a type mismatch and NotSupportedException without an asset manager, like State and Version (15_bindings.md 2.5; B11 covers only predicates on dead entities). The State summary is extended the same way, and csharp-api.md documents it.
- **F663** MINOR A user field named Pad<digits> in an [EcsComponent] is silently dropped from the schema (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Runtime/LayoutHash.cs:264`). New LayoutHash.SkipsPadding(type) (true only for [NativeComponent]). Describe and AssetRefBinder.Reflect drop Pad<digits> fields only for mirrors, so an [EcsComponent] keeps them in its schema and alignment. Summaries are reworded with escaped &lt;digits&gt;, which also fixes their CS1570. Every shipped mirror hash is unchanged (all mirrors with Pad fields are [NativeComponent]).
- **F670** MINOR ABI record checks compare sizes only, so reordered same-size fields pass (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Interop/Records.cs:332`). Test-only fix. BindingRecordSizeProbe gains an Offsets string: 'Record.Field=offset;' for every field of all 18 records (the 15 listed plus GamepadState, InputBlock and FrameBlock), using Marshal.OffsetOf<T> with string literals. The native case builds the same string from offsetof on native_api.h, mapping PadN to pad+N and skipping frame_block.padding. The comment on the size case now points at the offset case.
- **F671** MINOR Enum defaults: 64-bit JSON versus 32-bit blob, and an OverflowException for ulong enums aborts class registration (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Runtime/JsonText.cs:113`). ScriptClassInfo.TryClassifyScalar refuses long- and ulong-backed enums, so the existing INFO log at ScriptCatalog skips them, lists of them included. JsonText writes an enum default as the int32 the blob carries (unchecked((int)Convert.ToInt64)), so a uint 0xFFFFFFFF is -1 in both, and the OverflowException that aborted the whole assembly load cannot happen anymore.
- **F672** MINOR NaN and infinite defaults are silently rewritten to 0 (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Runtime/JsonText.cs:84`). JsonText threads a clamped flag through Value, Append, AppendScalar and Number. NaN becomes 0, and an infinity becomes the largest finite double, or float.MaxValue written through double so it parses back to exactly FLT_MAX. FieldAccessor.DefaultJson(defaults, out clamped) is added. MetaJson writes min and max only when finite. ScriptCatalog logs an INFO naming the field when a default was clamped. csharp-api.md is updated.
- **F675** MINOR AudioSource.Volume/Pitch/Loop cost three to four native calls and an asset bind per assignment (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/AudioSource.cs:68`). AudioSource Volume, Pitch and Loop now write through a private WriteFields. When the clip read back is null or already bound, it calls component_set directly with no asset_ref_bind. When native holds an id with an unbound slot it falls back to Entity.SetComponent so the clip is still bound. Clip and Data keep the R25 bind. The facade summary and csharp-api.md document the cost.
- **F677** MINOR Color has Equals but no == / != operators, unlike every other value type of the SDK (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/Color.cs:10`). Color gains operator == and != (via Equals), with summaries.
- **F678** MINOR Conjure.Managed ships without its XML documentation, so IDEs show none of the /// summaries (`managed/Directory.Build.props:12`). Conjure.Managed.csproj sets GenerateDocumentationFile true with NoWarn CS1591, and Conjure.Managed.xml now ships in bin/managed. Fixed the CS1570 comments: LayoutHash Pad<digits> (via F663) and the ScriptClassInfo "<DeclaringType>.<name>" line. The build reports 0 warnings, so there are no other CS15xx. managed/CMakeLists.txt adds the CTest entry conjure_managed_xml_docs.sdk (label managed). It also lists Conjure.Managed/Conjure.Managed.csproj in the SDK project's SOURCES so editing that csproj rebuilds the SDK; before, only Bootstrap's csproj and the .cs files were stamp inputs.
- **F679** MINOR FrameStats.PhaseMs takes a bare index with no managed name for the ten engine phases (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/Diagnostics.cs:34`). Diagnostics.cs adds a public EnginePhase : byte enum (FrameBegin through FrameEnd, in ecs::system_phase order) and FrameStats.PhaseMs(EnginePhase). The int overload's summary points at EnginePhase, and csharp-api.md documents PhaseMs(EnginePhase) and lists EnginePhase in the summary table.
- **F680** MINOR WrongThreadProbe has a conditional whose two branches are identical (`managed/Conjure.TestScripts/Probes.cs:25`). WrongThreadProbe now does `_ = entity.IsAlive;` with a comment that it throws off the main thread, then `return "none";`, instead of an identical-branch conditional.
- **F681** MINOR AssetRefBinder says a mismatch leaves the slot null but never clears it (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Interop/AssetRefBinder.cs:90`). AssetRefBinder<T>.Bind now sets *slot = null after any failed bind (mismatch, NOT_SUPPORTED, INVALID_ARGUMENT), so a stale pointer read back with GetComponent cannot land bound. This matches its summary and the bindings README. The once-per-field mismatch warning is unchanged.

### ecs (22) (commit `b3943cd`)

- **F006** MAJOR System views stamp world.version() instead of the system's run version, so a system sees its own previous writes (`engine/src/ecs/query.cpp:124`). query::chunk and query::visit_chunks now stamp through a helper that returns context->run_version() when a system_context is bound and world.version() otherwise (query.cpp includes system.h). The for_each_chunk, chunk and bind_context summaries in query.h are updated. The change-detection section of the API docs, the handbook and the README now say that system views stamp the run version.
- **F007** MAJOR Re-registering a dynamic (C#) component with changed fields but the same size/alignment silently keeps the old field description (`engine/src/ecs/component_registry.cpp:76`). Added same_fields() to component_registry.cpp. It compares name, type, kind, offset and size of every field, and same_layout() calls it when both infos are DYNAMIC. Re-registering a dynamic name with different fields at the same size and alignment is now NAME_CONFLICT, which sends scripting's hot reload to its redefine_dynamic branch. C++ and module registrations are unchanged. The register_dynamic summary and the docs are updated.
- **F009** MAJOR Ids of a failed or discarded deferred instantiate go back to the allocator without a generation bump: handles already returned to the caller alias the next entities (`engine/src/ecs/command_buffer.cpp:229`). command_buffer::clear() now releases each pending instantiate id with world::release_reserved (bumped generation) instead of allocator return_unused. One deviation from the plan, needed for correctness: release_reserved did not keep the record table consistent for an index past mRecords. A table grown later would start that index at generation 1 while the free list held 2, which breaks validate() and is_reserved(). release_reserved now calls ensure_records(target.index) first; this is a no-op for the existing flush callers. The clear() and destructor summaries, the API docs, the handbook and the README now document that this is owner-thread work.
- **F268** MAJOR A preserved unresolved component and a real component of the same name are written as duplicate keys; the file then fails to load (`engine/src/ecs/serialization.cpp:515`). write_archive_json now skips a preserved unresolved entry whose name resolves to a non-transient component that the entry's mask holds, so the live component is written once in its place. The read side stays strict. The serialization.h summary, the archives docs NOTE, the handbook and the README are updated.
- **F010** MINOR A tag (empty type) in a view pack compiles but indexes an empty span: assert in Debug, undefined behaviour in Distribution (`engine/include/conjure/ecs/view.h:88`). view.h: added detail::HAS_COLUMN<PackType> and a static_assert in the private section of class view, so a tag in the pack (plain or maybe<Tag>) no longer compiles. chunk_view.h: get() and get_optional() are constrained with requires(!is_empty_v<remove_cv_t<T>>), and their summaries are updated. The view.h header comment, the API docs, the handbook and the README are updated. I grepped engine, editor, runtime, tests and samples for any tag in a pack or a get<Tag>; there are none, and conjure_scene, conjure_scripting and conjure_physics still build.
- **F256** MINOR world.get<const T>(e) on a non-const world still stamps the column (spurious change) (`engine/include/conjure/ecs/world.h:320`). The non-const world::get<T> passes !std::is_const_v<T> as forWrite, so get<const T> on a non-const world never stamps. The summary and the entities-and-components docs (table row and TIP) are updated.
- **F260** MINOR Unknown after/before names are logged on every add_system and every rebuild, for every system of the phase, not 'one DEBUG log each' (`engine/src/ecs/scheduler.cpp:84`). scheduler::resolve_edges now takes 'const system_record *logFor' instead of 'bool logUnknown'. Only that system's unknown after/before names are logged. add_system passes &record and build_stages passes nullptr, so finalise() and lazy rebuilds never log. The comment, the add_system summary, the API docs, the handbook and the README are updated.
- **F261** MINOR Dead members and functions: mEmptyArchetype, world::stamp_chunk, observer_table::clear/count, the reflected remapEntities hook chain, query_desc::writes/optional (`engine/include/conjure/ecs/world.h:181`). (a) mEmptyArchetype is now read. archetype_for() returns it for an empty mask once the constructor has set it. This covers create_entity and create_entities as the plan asked, plus flush and instantiate placement, with identical semantics and fewer lines than duplicating the code. (b) world::stamp_chunk is deleted (declaration and definition). (c) observer_table::count() and clear() are deleted. (d) No change: remap_typed_component and make_default_serializer are live. (e) The query_desc summary is rewritten: optional and writes only record what the pack declares, stamping follows span constness, and write validation uses the bound system's system_desc. The query API docs are updated as well.
- **F262** MINOR world_hash depends on row order inside an archetype (creation/destruction history), which the documentation does not list (`engine/include/conjure/ecs/world_hash.h:29`). world_hash.h gains a row-order bullet in the 'depends on' list and its summary now reads 'same entities in the same row order, same bytes'. The same qualification is added to the world_hash section of command-buffers-and-observers.md, the handbook section 3.5 and the README.
- **F263** MINOR Every component event scans every observer slot of the world (`engine/src/ecs/observer_table.cpp:91`). observer_table keeps std::array<component_mask, EVENT_COUNT> mObserved and std::array<u32, EVENT_COUNT*MAX_COMPONENTS> mObserverCounts. These are arrays rather than the plan's std::vector, so a world does not pay an extra allocation. EVENT_COUNT is derived from component_event::REMOVED. add_component_observer and remove() maintain them, and fire_component returns early per call when the (event, id) bit is clear. The README and the handbook are updated.
- **F264** MINOR entity_remap::remap is a linear search per reference; capture of N entities with hook-remapped references is O(N^2) (`engine/include/conjure/ecs/entity.h:179`). entity_remap gains a private mIndex (sorted (to_u64, local index) pairs) and a public index_targets(). index_targets skips null targets, uses stable_sort, and keeps the first local index of a duplicate key. remap() binary-searches when the index is non-empty. set(), resize() and clear() drop the index. <algorithm> is included. entity_archive::capture indexes its table. archives-and-serialization.md, the README and the handbook are updated.
- **F265** MINOR Archives are fully re-validated on every instantiate, twice (three times through command_buffer paths) (`engine/src/ecs/world_archive.cpp:53`). Removed the redundant archive->validate() from command_buffer::push_instantiate and kept the registry identity check. The private declaration in command_buffer.h now carries a comment saying the caller validates. Each deferred instantiate now validates exactly once. The README and the handbook note this.
- **F266** MINOR instantiate_batch with a command buffer of another world is not rejected (`engine/src/ecs/world_archive.cpp:61`). world::instantiate_batch now asserts and returns INVALID_ARGUMENT when the command buffer targets another world. The check sits after the registry check and before validate, so nothing is reserved. The instantiate and instantiate_batch summaries, archives-and-serialization.md, the handbook and the README are updated.
- **F270** MINOR write_archive_json leaves the JSON writer unbalanced on a component write failure (`engine/src/ecs/serialization.cpp:527`). Removed the four end_* calls on the unregistered-id path of write_archive_json, so both failure paths leave the writer as it was at the failure. The write_archive_json summary and the archives docs row say the writer then holds partial, unbalanced output that must be reset or discarded. The handbook and the README are updated.
- **F271** MINOR remove followed by add of a component the entity already holds is a no-op: value not reset, no REMOVED/ADDED observers (`engine/src/ecs/world_structural.cpp:649`). Documentation-only fix as planned. The command_buffer::add_raw summary now states that an empty payload records no value: a held component keeps its value and no observer fires, and passing the default bytes resets it. The add_raw and remove-then-add rows in command-buffers-and-observers.md, the handbook and the README are updated. No production behaviour change.
- **F272** MINOR world::add<T> can return success with a null pointer for a non-tag component (`engine/include/conjure/ecs/world.h:277`). Documentation fix as planned. The world::add<T> and add_raw summaries document a nullptr result when an ADDED observer's deferred work removed the component or destroyed the entity. The entities-and-components table row and untyped-access paragraph, a NOTE in command-buffers-and-observers.md, the handbook and the README are updated.
- **F273** MINOR flush drops the whole buffer with an empty report when the record table cannot grow (`engine/src/ecs/world_structural.cpp:540`). flush_internal now sets report.skipped = commands.command_count() before commands.clear() when ensure_records fails. The command-buffers docs and the handbook gain the matching row.
- **F274** MINOR Size/TAG mismatch is reported as BAD_ALIGNMENT (`engine/src/ecs/component_registry.cpp:412`). validate_layout now returns core::error(INVALID_ARGUMENT, "component '<name>': …") for a TAG with non-zero size and for size 0 without TAG. The register_described summary, the entities docs error list, the handbook failure table and the README are updated.
- **F275** MINOR 'Debug-only' access validation still does its work in Distribution (`engine/src/ecs/system.cpp:49`). system_context::view() wraps only validate_access in #if CONJURE_ASSERTS_ENABLED; bind_context stays unconditional. chunk_view::column_data wraps the declared-write check in the same guard and keeps the stamp unconditional. The summary, the API docs, the handbook and the README are updated.
- **F277** MINOR Two test cases consist of static_asserts plus CHECK(true) (`tests/ecs/test_component.cpp:68`). Replaced CHECK(true) with runtime checks and kept the static_asserts. test_component.cpp checks the has_flag values; test_system_phase.cpp walks a constexpr std::array of the 10 phases and checks SYSTEM_PHASE_COUNT and COUNT.
- **F617** MINOR world_hash allocates a JSON writer and a string per non-trivial component per entity (`engine/src/ecs/world_hash.cpp:72`). world_hash uses one std::optional<core::json::writer> scratch per call. It is built on the first non-trivial component, then reset per component, and the hashed bytes come from text(). One deviation from the plan: the plan declared the writer eagerly, which would break the existing 'allocation free for trivial worlds' gate under MSVC debug iterators; the optional keeps trivial worlds allocation-free. world_hash.h gains a bullet for an unserialisable component and its summary is updated. The command-buffers docs, the handbook and the README are updated.
- **F770** MINOR Header usage comment passes a string literal where string_id's constructor is explicit (`engine/include/conjure/ecs/system.h:28`). The usage comment in system.h now passes core::string_id("game.integrate") and wraps the call in CONJURE_TRY. engine/src/ecs/README.md uses core::string_id("game.integrate").

### assets (21) (commit `d175fee`)

- **F016** MAJOR A source whose import keeps failing is re-imported, re-logged and re-written to disk every frame (`engine/src/assets/hot_reload.cpp:65`). hot_reload_controller::update() takes the CACHE_MISS failed ids once, adds only those whose record is OK to dirty (has_ok_record via status_of), and after the import queues only those whose record is still OK. An IMPORT_FAILED source now waits for a change to itself, its .meta or a side file, so it is no longer re-imported, ERROR-logged and rewritten every frame. hot_reload.h summary, handbook section 8 and the database-and-import.md Hot reload section are updated.
- **F017** MAJOR scan() overrides a legitimately new uuid with the old record's uuid, leaving memory and the .meta on disk disagreeing (`engine/src/assets/asset_database.cpp:372`). scan() builds scannedPaths and tracks metaCreated. A moved_here() lambda is true when the uuid belongs to a known primary at another path that is MISSING_SOURCE, whose source is gone, or whose old meta no longer declares the uuid; only that last case reads the meta. The uuid guard now skips metas this scan created and moved-here uuids. A created meta takes old = nullptr, so the new asset inherits nothing and counts in recordsAdded. A hand edit to an unknown uuid, or to the uuid of a live asset, is still refused. Handbook 3.1, database-and-import.md and the module README are updated.
- **F018** MAJOR A sub-asset the importer stopped producing makes its source stale again after every scan (`engine/src/assets/asset_database.cpp:457`). scan() claims each sub uuid before creating its record. It skips a sub that has no previous record when the primary was imported and the cache has no blob for it. apply_import_results removes the blobs of dropped sub-assets (WARN on failure). write_meta skips such subs in the same way. A primary that was never imported still gets every sub-asset.
- **F019** MAJOR load_blocking TIMEOUT on a dependency strands the dependent in FINALISING for ever (flush() spins) (`engine/src/assets/asset_manager.cpp:756`). In state::load_blocking_slot, the dependency-TIMEOUT branch puts the slot back into 'finalising' when it is still awaitingFinalise and not already listed. Each recursion level restores the slot it erased, so the async pipeline finalises it and flush() no longer spins. The load_blocking summary in asset_manager.h, referencing-and-loading.md and handbook 5.2 are updated.
- **F020** MAJOR Cook stops importing when the closure changes membership without changing size (`engine/src/assets/asset_cooker.cpp:135`). asset_cooker::cook drops the closure-size 'grew' test and loops until toImport is empty (importedThisCook bounds it). Member failures ('record vanished', 'record is <status>') are collected per pass in memberFailures and appended after the loop, so only the final closure's members fail the cook. Import failures still come first, so dedup keeps the import message. The comment, handbook section 9, the module README and cooking-and-packs.md are updated.
- **F026** MAJOR The source is read twice per import: the blob can record the hash of bytes it was not built from (`engine/src/assets/asset_database.cpp:706`). Added the private asset_database::hash_source_bytes(primaryBytes, sideFiles). hash_source now reads the file and delegates to it. importOne hashes the bytes it handed the importer, so the blob header never records the hash of bytes it was not built from. Side files are still read twice; closing that window needs import_context to record side-file hashes, a public API change.
- **F150** MAJOR assets::make_asset_error and assets::make_asset_path are not exported (`engine/include/conjure/assets/assets_error.h:51`). Added CONJURE_API to make_asset_error (assets_error.h) and make_asset_path (asset_database.h). Verified that assets_error.cpp.obj and asset_database.cpp.obj now carry /EXPORT directives for both symbols; tools/check_exports.py reports 0 findings. I did not run dumpbin on conjure_shared.dll: that target builds every module and is outside my target.
- **F310** MAJOR A side file that is missing at import time is not recorded, so the source is not re-imported when it appears (`engine/src/assets/asset_importer.cpp:93`). import_context::read_side_file records the side path (deduplicated, sorted) before the read, so a missing side file is recorded too; the IO_ERROR return is unchanged. In apply_import_results, the failure branch merges job.sideFiles into the primary's sideFiles (union, sorted, unique; never replaced). The asset_importer.h comments, database-and-import.md and handbook 3.3 are updated.
- **F312** MAJOR create_asset / move_asset / add_file accept paths that leave the mount (`engine/src/assets/asset_database.cpp:987`). Added stays_in_mount() and leaves_mount() helpers to asset_database.cpp. add_file, create_asset and move_asset normalise the relative path right after the mount lookup and return INVALID_ARGUMENT before any record lookup or file-system access when the path is empty, '.', '..', starts with '../' or is absolute. The asset_database.h summaries, database-and-import.md and handbook 3.3 / 11 are updated.
- **F313** MAJOR One unwritable .meta aborts the whole scan; create_asset leaves an orphan source when the meta write fails (`engine/src/assets/asset_database.cpp:348`). scan(): a failed fresh-meta write logs WARN and falls into the shared META_INVALID path (transient id, invalidMetas); the next scan retries. A failed importer-pinning write writes a copy, logs WARN and leaves the record NO_IMPORTER. create_asset removes the source when its meta write fails (WARN if that removal also fails) and returns the meta error. The asset_database.h summaries, database-and-import.md and handbook 3.1 / 11 are updated.
- **F184** MINOR 16-byte .conjure/cache/db.bin makes editor and runtime commit ~5.3 GB at startup (reserve driven by an unvalidated count) (`engine/src/assets/database_snapshot.cpp:92`). parse_database_snapshot reserves std::min(recordCount, in.remaining() / MIN_RECORD_BYTES), with MIN_RECORD_BYTES = 113 and the derivation in a comment, and includes <algorithm>. The truncation checks still reject the file. Handbook 3.4 is updated.
- **F300** MINOR Every newly added source (and every import that rewrites its .meta) is imported twice (`engine/src/assets/hot_reload.cpp:133`). collect_dirty skips a non-REMOVED .meta change when meta_needs_import() is false. That helper is an anonymous-namespace function in hot_reload.cpp. It returns true when the meta is unreadable, the record is missing or not OK, the importer or canonical settings hash differs from the record's, or the owner is in stale_ids(). The metas that add_file and an import write therefore cause no second import, and an inspector write_meta still re-imports. The hot_reload.h summary, handbook section 8 and the API page are updated.
- **F302** MINOR hot reload scans every slot twice per frame (`engine/src/assets/hot_reload.cpp:65`). Added slot_data::inFailedList and state::failedSlots. fail() lists a slot once when it enters FAILED. failed_ids() drops the slots that left FAILED (clearing their flag), sorts the list into slot order and answers from it, keeping its signature. hot_reload_controller now calls failed_ids once per update instead of twice. Handbook 5.2 is updated.
- **F303** MINOR A fallback asked for before its type is registered stays null for ever (`engine/src/assets/asset_manager.cpp:884`). state::fallback_payload checks find_loader before inserting into fallbackAttempted, so a request made before registration records nothing. A create_fallback failure is still logged once per registration. Docs are updated (handbook 6, referencing-and-loading.md).
- **F304** MINOR A subscriber removed during event delivery is still called for the rest of the batch (`engine/src/assets/asset_manager.cpp:567`). fire_events keeps the listener copy, which keeps a running callback alive, and calls a listener only while is_subscribed(id) holds on the live list. unsubscribe() in asset_manager.h has a summary. The Events section of referencing-and-loading.md and handbook 5.2 state the semantics.
- **F305** MINOR A failed reload corrupts the slot's bookkeeping: payloadBytes, dependencyIds and lastError (`engine/src/assets/asset_manager.cpp:313`). Added slot_data::pendingPayloadBytes. accept_stage writes it and publish commits it to payloadBytes. A restore_published_dependencies() helper rebuilds dependencyIds from dependencyHandles in fail()'s reload branch and in discard_in_flight's reload branch. collect() resets lastError. The bytesResident comment and the last_error summary in asset_manager.h, referencing-and-loading.md and handbook 6 are updated.
- **F306** MINOR asset_load_batch::is_complete() flips back to false while a loaded member hot-reloads (`engine/include/conjure/assets/asset_types.h:78`). An anonymous-namespace is_settled() helper (unbound, LOADED, RELOADING or FAILED) now drives asset_load_batch::is_complete and progress. loaded_count counts LOADED and RELOADING. assets::is_terminal and asset_handle::is_terminal are unchanged, so dependency gating still waits for a reloading dependency. The header docs and referencing-and-loading.md (example comment plus a sentence) are updated.
- **F309** MINOR Type-mismatch 'log once' state is a process-wide static that grows without bound and outlives the manager (`engine/src/assets/asset_ref.cpp:38`). Added detail::forget_type_mismatches() (CONJURE_API, summary) to asset_ref.h and asset_ref.cpp; it clears the set under the mutex. ~asset_manager calls it after shutdown(). The report_type_mismatch doc reads 'until the asset_manager is destroyed'. referencing-and-loading.md says 'logs once per asset_manager'.
- **F316** MINOR Every load copies the payload twice (`engine/src/assets/cooked_blob.cpp:168`). cooked_blob.cpp has a parse_blob_prefix(span, outPayloadOffset) helper holding all the validation. The span overload copies the payload. The new CONJURE_API parse_cooked_blob(std::vector<std::byte> &&) shifts the prefix out in place and moves the buffer into payload. asset_cache::read calls it with std::move(bytes).value(). cooked_blob.h has a summary and cooking-and-packs.md a row; handbook section 4 is updated.
- **F585** MINOR A failing watcher pass logs the same WARN every interval forever (`engine/src/assets/file_watcher.cpp:103`). polling_file_watcher has a new member, mLastPassFailure (guarded by mMutex). The pass lambda logs the WARN only when the failure message differs from the last one. A successful pass clears it and logs INFO 'file watcher pass succeeded again'. database-and-import.md and handbook sections 8 and 11 are updated.
- **F768** MINOR Assets handbook says the design's integration tests have not landed; they exist (`docs/handbook/modules/assets.md:319`). The last sentence of docs/handbook/modules/assets.md section 13 now says the integration suites landed with wave 4 (tests/integration/test_assets_renderer_headless.cpp and test_assets_scene.cpp exist and are listed in tests/integration/CMakeLists.txt:44-45). It also says bench_asset_ref_get, bench_asset_load and bench_pack_lookup are still owed: tests/benchmarks has no assets scenario.

### engine (21) (commit `05301cd`)

- **F032** MAJOR The editor never enables shadow copies, so a loaded native game module stays locked on Windows and module.build can never relink it (`engine/src/engine/subsystems/modules_subsystem.cpp:87`). modules_subsystem::startup now turns on shadowCopyBinaries for an EDITOR host that has a project, with shadowCopyDirectory = <project>/.conjure/module_cache (07 D9). The runtime still loads binaries in place. The step-15 header comment and the handbook table say so.
- **F064** MAJOR collect_shapes() is never called outside tests, so the shape cache only grows (`engine/src/physics/physics_world.cpp:525`): same as F176.
- **F074** MAJOR scripting_module_consumer keeps a dangling script_runtime pointer after scripting is disabled at step 17 (`engine/src/engine/subsystems/scripting_subsystem.cpp:281`). Added scripting_module_consumer::set_runtime(scripting::script_runtime*), inside the CONJURE_WITH_SCRIPTING block. scripting_subsystem::disable() and shutdown() now clear the consumer's runtime pointer before the runtime is destroyed, so a later managed reload is a no-op instead of a use-after-free.
