- **F076** MAJOR imgui_layer swallows key and mouse release events while ImGui captures input: stuck keys in engine input (`engine/src/engine/imgui_layer.cpp:287`). imgui_layer::on_event no longer consumes key RELEASE or mouse-button RELEASE events while ImGui has capture, so a key or button pressed before capture began cannot stay stuck down in platform::input. The imgui_layer.h summary is updated.
- **F152** MAJOR project.export into the project's own directory overwrites project.json with cooked:true and bricks the project for editing (`engine/src/engine/export_pipeline.cpp:161`). export_pipeline::run now rejects an output that is the project directory itself, or the assets root or any folder inside it, with BAD_ARGUMENTS before anything is written. The comparison uses std::filesystem::equivalent on the output and each existing ancestor, so a trailing separator, case or spelling difference still matches. <project>/export is still accepted. Updated the export.h run() summary, the IMPORTANT box in docs/api/engine/export-and-diagnostics.md, and the handbook.
- **F176** MAJOR physics_world::collect_shapes() is never called by the engine, so the shape cache only grows (`engine/src/physics/physics_world.cpp:525`). physics_subsystem subscribes to scene_manager::on_before_unload and sets a pending flag. Its new end_frame override then calls physics_world::collect_shapes() once, after the unload has destroyed the bodies. The subscription is reset in shutdown. Not covered (needs a physics-side decision): shape-cache growth inside a single scene.
- **F750** MAJOR Runtime on a source project exits 0 running the stale cached scene when the current scene file no longer imports (`engine/src/engine/export_pipeline.cpp:224`). load_startup_scene now checks the asset database: a runtime fails with STARTUP_SCENE_FAILED when the startup scene's record is IMPORT_FAILED, instead of running the stale cached copy. The editor still loads the cached copy. Updated the engine_error.h comment and the API page.
- **F460** MINOR Saved and exported project.json carries float noise; the comment claims the opposite (`engine/src/engine/project.cpp:327`). project.cpp number() now writes each float as the shortest decimal that reads back as the same f32, via std::format("{}") then core::str::parse_double. 0.1f is now saved as 0.1, not 0.10000000149011612. The misleading comment is replaced.
- **F462** MINOR Command-line edge cases: a flag is taken as another flag's value, --audio off wins regardless of order, headless silently ignores --rhi (`engine/src/engine/command_line.cpp:55`). take_value now treats a following token that starts with -- as a missing value (BAD_ARGUMENTS). When --audio is given more than once the last one wins, because off and a backend now clear each other. application_desc::from_options logs a WARN when a real --rhi backend is combined with --headless. Updated the command_line.h and application_desc.h summaries, the API page and the handbook.
- **F463** MINOR A forced frame delta above maxFrameDeltaSeconds validates and is then silently clamped (`engine/src/engine/engine_config.cpp:60`). engine_config::validate() now rejects forcedFrameDeltaSeconds > maxFrameDeltaSeconds. In headless mode, project::apply_to caps the forced delta at the project's maxFrameDeltaSeconds, so a project with a clamp below 1/60 still boots with the same effective delta. The validate() summary now lists every rule. running-the-engine.md is updated.
- **F464** MINOR set_time_scale(NaN) stores NaN (`engine/src/engine/application.cpp:292`). application::set_time_scale and begin_frame now store 0 for negative, NaN or infinite time scales. Before, std::max kept NaN and +inf. Updated the application.h and engine_time.h summaries and the API page.
- **F468** MINOR Misleading names in the frame statistics (`engine/include/conjure/engine/frame_stats.h:94`). Renamed frame_stats::total_dropped_fixed_steps() to total_frames_with_dropped_time() and mTotalDroppedFixedSteps to mTotalFramesWithDroppedTime. The debug overlay now prints 'cpu' as frameMilliseconds minus pacerSleepMilliseconds, next to the full 'frame' time. Updated frame-layers-and-time.md.
- **F469** MINOR Stale comments: unknown flags are not BAD_ARGUMENTS, step_frame(f64) does not replace the delta on frame 0 (`engine/include/conjure/engine/engine_error.h:26`). Comment and docs fixes only: the BAD_ARGUMENTS comment in engine_error.h (unknown flags are not errors); the step_frame(f64) summary in application.h and the run_frame summary in frame_loop.h (frame 0 always runs fixedDeltaSeconds); the merge order in the command_line.h header; and running-the-engine.md (step_frame on the first frame).
- **F593** MINOR project.json vec3 values are not checked for finiteness (read_f32 is) (`engine/src/engine/project.cpp:127`). read_vec3 now reads each component with a NaN fallback and returns PROJECT_INVALID for a value outside the f32 range, as read_f32 already did. Before, [0,-1e39,0] silently became 0.
- **F594** MINOR An unknown top-level key overwrites a same-named member of 'extensions' (`engine/src/engine/project.cpp:433`). An unknown top-level key that is also a member of extensions no longer overwrites that member. The extensions member is kept and a WARN names the key. Updated the API page and the handbook.
- **F595** MINOR Game module name is empty when the project path ends with a separator (`engine/src/engine/project.cpp:626`). project::parse drops a trailing separator from the root directory (a bare root such as C:/ is left alone). root_directory() and the game module are now named after the folder.
- **F609** MINOR Mesh extraction takes 1 + N locked hash lookups per mesh entity (record names sprites only) (`engine/src/engine/subsystems/renderer_subsystem.cpp:282`). The renderer_subsystem mesh extraction now remembers the last mesh and material lookups within one extract call. Consecutive entities that share a mesh or material skip the locked gpu_resource_cache lookup. The sprite loop memo (optional in the plan) was not done.
- **F658** MINOR Export copies shader dependency files (*.dxil.d) into the shipped folder (`engine/src/engine/export_pipeline.cpp:335`). The copy_runtime step now uses copy_shader_blobs. It walks shaders/ recursively with the non-throwing iterator, keeps relative paths, and copies only index.json, *.dxil and *.spv, so the .d depfiles are no longer shipped. copy_into lost its now-unused recursive parameter. Updated export-and-diagnostics.md.
- **F709** MINOR Wall-clock assertion: application construction must take under 5 ms (`tests/engine/test_application_headless.cpp:79`). The application_headless construction case no longer asserts a 5 ms wall-clock limit. It reports the time with MESSAGE and checks structurally that subsystems() is empty and the state is CREATED. The case is renamed. The plan's message said a benchmark asserts the budget, but none does, so the message says 'not asserted'.
- **F758** MINOR executable_directory() silently falls back to the current directory when the executable path exceeds MAX_PATH (`engine/src/engine/executable_path.cpp:22`). executable_directory() now enlarges its buffer when the path is truncated: on Windows, GetModuleFileNameW in a std::vector up to 32768 wide characters; on Linux, readlink up to 65536 bytes. Its current-directory fallback now logs a WARN. Updated the executable_path.h summary. The runtime duplicate is left to F466.
- **F777** MINOR Export publishes managed PDBs and the Bootstrap apphost into the shipped game (`engine/src/engine/export_pipeline.cpp:277`). New private engine/src/engine/export_commands.{h,cpp} with managed_export_commands(), registered in engine/src/engine/CMakeLists.txt; export step 3 now calls it. Debug symbols are stripped (-p:DebugType=none, -p:DebugSymbols=false) unless scripting.exportDebugSymbols is set. A framework-dependent publish adds -p:UseAppHost=false; a self-contained one keeps the apphost (NETSDK1067). Updated the publish_managed row of the docs table and the module README.

### scene (19) (commit `22d12f3`)

- **F039** MAJOR prefab::apply writes duplicate entry guids when two instance entities share a prefab_link guid (`engine/src/scene/prefab.cpp:380`). prefab::apply (prefab.cpp) keeps a std::unordered_set of link guids already used. The first entity in hierarchy pre-order keeps its link guid and later repeats get a fresh guid, which is mirrored into their prefab_link. scene_serializer::parse_prefab now also runs detail::check_unique_guids, so a file with repeated entry guids fails with DUPLICATE_GUID. Updated: prefab.h apply summary, scene_serializer.h parse_prefab summary, README, the API page (apply row and errors paragraph) and the handbook section 6.
- **F149** MAJOR scene::prefab::instantiate is not exported (missing CONJURE_API) (`engine/include/conjure/scene/prefab.h:105`). Added CONJURE_API to prefab::instantiate in prefab.h, matching every sibling declaration.
- **F357** MAJOR revert destroys the instance before it knows the re-instantiation can succeed (`engine/src/scene/prefab.cpp:346`). New check_reinstantiable in prefab.cpp's anonymous namespace. It runs archive->validate(), detail::check_prefab_roots (the F366 helper, so PREFAB_MULTIPLE_ROOTS or BAD_FORMAT), compute_archive_shape, and a depth test (baseDepth + max shape depth >= MAX_HIERARCHY_DEPTH gives HIERARCHY_TOO_DEEP). revert calls it before destroy_now, so these failures leave the instance untouched. Updated: prefab.h revert summary, README, API page revert row, handbook section 6.
- **F358** MAJOR unload and apply_document hold a record pointer/reference across event emissions that can reallocate mRecords (`engine/src/scene/scene_manager.cpp:119`). unload() keeps only the initial find_record check. After the event and the destroys it calls find_record(id) again, and only if the record is still there does it retire it and erase it from mLoadOrder. A re-entrant unload of the same id leaves nothing to do and does not remove a newer scene that reused the slot. apply_document reads the id before load_scene and afterwards uses mRecords[slot] for the failure path and the batch assignment. Comments note that handlers may load scenes and grow mRecords.
- **F347** MINOR transform_dirty is added to transformless entities and never removed (`engine/src/scene/scene_link_pass.cpp:257`). transform_dirty now goes only to entities that carry a local_transform. Four places changed. add_dirty tests the live mask for localTransform. add_transform adds the tag itself, using the mask it read, because its local_transform may still be in mScratch. deferred_set_parent requires localTransform before adding the tag. The link pass adds the tag inside the existing transform/companions block. The repair pass and set_parent go through add_dirty. A README deviation bullet records that 13_scene.md 3.2 step 5 says 'always'. Handbook section 3 is updated.
- **F350** MINOR create_entities with a null template bumps structure_version and emits on_structure_changed once per entity (`engine/src/scene/scene.cpp:165`). The body of create_entity moved into a private create_entity_unannounced, declared in scene.h under Tree bookkeeping and defined in scene.cpp's tree bookkeeping section. create_entity now calls it and then bump_structure(). The null-template branch of create_entities calls create_entity_unannounced in the loop and bumps once after the loop when count > 0. The create_entities summary says it is one structural operation.
- **F351** MINOR sibling_index documents a root position it does not return (`engine/include/conjure/scene/hierarchy.h:46`). Comment-only contract fix: the hierarchy.h sibling_index summary now says 0 for roots (their order is scene::roots()) and for dead entities. The 'Walking the tree' paragraph of the API page says the same.
- **F352** MINOR find_by_layer does not return entities without a layer component for layer 0 (`engine/include/conjure/scene/scene.h:510`). Comment-only contract fix: the scene.h find_by_layer summary now describes the column scan. Entities that never received a layer are not listed, although layer_of() reports 0 for them. The find_by_* row of the API page states the same. The code is unchanged.
- **F356** MINOR apply_hierarchy_changes gives away the repair queue's storage on every pass with work (`engine/src/scene/scene_link_pass.cpp:316`). Added scene::mRepairSpare. apply_hierarchy_changes clears it, swaps it with mRepairQueue, iterates it (waiting entries are pushed back into mRepairQueue) and clears it before the WARN and bump. Both vectors keep their capacity. scene::clear() also clears the spare.
- **F359** MINOR unload_all clears authored entities without on_before_unload (`engine/src/scene/scene_manager.cpp:150`). After the record loop, when mWorld->entity_count() > 0, unload_all collects the remaining roots and emits on_before_unload(SCENE_ID_AUTHORED, roots) before mScene.clear(). Updated: scene_manager.h unload_all and on_before_unload comments, README scene-manager paragraph, API page unload_all row and WARNING box, handbook section 7.
- **F360** MINOR scene_snapshot::structureVersion is captured and never used (`engine/include/conjure/scene/scene_snapshot.h:30`). Documentation-only: scene_snapshot::structureVersion is now documented as informational, because restore() cannot rewind the counter. Updated: the field comment, the scene_snapshot.h file header, the README Snapshots bullet, the API page Snapshots paragraph and handbook section 6.
- **F361** MINOR destroy_internal's unlink parameter is always true (`engine/src/scene/scene.cpp:1096`). Removed the destroy_internal(target, bool unlink) declaration and definition. Its body, without the dead `if (unlink)`, now lives in destroy_now, and destroy_pending calls destroy_now.
- **F364** MINOR Entities with a null guid stay unidentified under every guid policy (`engine/src/scene/scene_link_pass.cpp:133`). In instantiate_archive's guid loop, a guid component with a null value gets a fresh guid indexed in mGuids under REGENERATE_ON_CONFLICT and REGENERATE_ALL. It is not counted as a conflict, so there is no extra WARN. KEEP leaves it null, which snapshot restore relies on. Updated: the guid_policy comments in scene.h, the API page guid_policy paragraph, the README Instantiation bullet and handbook section 5.
- **F366** MINOR parse_prefab reports PREFAB_MULTIPLE_ROOTS for an empty archive and for a single root that is not entry 0 (`engine/src/scene/scene_serializer.cpp:173`). New detail::check_prefab_roots(archive, ids, what) in archive_tools.h/.cpp. It returns BAD_FORMAT for an empty archive, 'the only root is entry N' (PREFAB_MULTIPLE_ROOTS) for a single misplaced root, and 'found N roots' (PREFAB_MULTIPLE_ROOTS) otherwise. It replaces the three inline checks in parse_prefab, prefab::build_document and prefab::instantiate_many. Updated: the scene_error.h PREFAB_MULTIPLE_ROOTS comment, the scene_serializer.h parse_prefab summary, the API page errors paragraph, the README and the handbook.
- **F367** MINOR The unresolved_components id is a magic `component_id{0}` (`engine/src/scene/archive_tools.cpp:263`). Added detail::UNRESOLVED_COMPONENTS_ID (ecs::component_id{0}, with a summary pointing at the ECS contract) to archive_tools.h. It replaces the literal in count_unresolved and unresolved_names, and the `.value == 0` tests in prefab_builder::add_component_json, add_component_raw and add_tag.
- **F368** MINOR README statements about benchmarks and the allocation gate are out of date (`engine/src/scene/README.md:184`). README only. The bench bullet now lists the four real scenarios (scene.transform_propagation_10k, _10k_sparse, scene.snapshot_capture_restore_10k, scene.load_10k, with thresholds.json) and the design scenarios that are not written. The Testing paragraph describes both 10k allocation gates, including the scheduler phase gate. The hierarchy-storage bullet names job_system::parallel_for. Handbook section 2 had the same stale parallel_for_chunks wording and is fixed too.
- **F372** MINOR The guid helpers test hides a validate violation by destroying its raw entity (`tests/scene/test_scene.cpp:772`). Test-only item, as reduced in triage. It adds coverage of link_instantiated's kept-external-parent branch; 'scene - guid helpers' is left to F042 as planned.
- **F587** MINOR Transform propagation's five shared counters sit on one cache line written by every worker per chunk (`engine/include/conjure/scene/transform_system.h:54`). Added a private transform_system::chunk_tally. process_chunk takes a chunk_tally& and adds to it instead of doing four relaxed fetch_adds per chunk. The run_level body lambda keeps one tally per job range and does the four fetch_adds once at the end of the range. chunksSkipped stays in the serial collect step. Updated: the run_counters summary, the README threading paragraph and handbook section 8.
- **F598** MINOR create_entities without a template leaves earlier entities behind when a later one fails (`engine/src/scene/scene.cpp:165`). The null-template branch of create_entities no longer uses CONJURE_TRY_ASSIGN. When the creation at index i fails, it calls destroy_now on out[i-1] down to out[0], sets out[0..i) back to NULL_ENTITY and returns the error, so the call is atomic like the template path. The summary in scene.h and the README deviation now say so. Lands together with F350.

### scripting (19) (commit `1d8c2b7`)

- **F067** MAJOR Game assembly path is sent to managed as ANSI, not UTF-8, on Windows (`engine/src/scripting/script_runtime.cpp:942`): same as F169.
- **F168** MAJOR null_host_recorder grows without bound: every run_phase appends a string and a record for the life of the process (`engine/src/scripting/null_script_host.cpp:438`). null_host_recorder now has MAX_RECORDS = 65536 per list, dropped_count(), set_enabled()/is_enabled() and a private admits() check in every record_* that fills a list. The u32 counters are unchanged. clear() keeps the enabled flag. null_script_host::set_recording(bool) was added. Class docs and hosting-and-hot-reload.md are updated.
- **F169** MAJOR Paths are converted with path::string() (ANSI code page on Windows) but consumed as UTF-8: non-ASCII project paths break builds and assembly loads (`engine/src/scripting/managed_build.cpp:196`). New private header engine/src/scripting/scripting_internal.h (detail::path_to_utf8, and detail::utf8_to_path using an element-wise u8string copy), listed in CMakeLists.txt. It is used in managed_build.cpp (project path, -o value, error text), script_runtime.cpp (the ABI path), null_script_host.cpp (builds the path from UTF-8, fixture name as stem + ".classes.json", read_file and the error log) and hot_reload.cpp (logs). With the old code the new runtime test threw from path::string() on this machine: 'No mapping for the Unicode character exists in the target multi-byte code page'.
- **F430** MAJOR Entity name table: erased when only the script component is removed, never erased on other destroy paths (`engine/src/scripting/script_runtime.cpp:602`). attach_world registers mDestroyedObserver via world::observe_destroyed, before the script_component check so ecs-only worlds are covered too. It erases the entity's name and is removed in the detach block. The mEntityNames.erase calls in on_component_removed, destroy_all, request_entity_destroy and process_pending_destroys are gone. entity_name_count() was added. Header and wiki docs are updated.
- **F173** MINOR conjure_scripting_tests fails two cases when run as one process: a zombie load context left by an earlier case (`tests/scripting/test_script_hot_reload.cpp:140`). Both dotnet cases now record zombiesBefore = collect_garbage() right after the fixture is built and check collect_garbage() <= zombiesBefore. The runtime-local zombieAssemblies == 0 checks are kept.
- **F429** MINOR initialize() returns an error yet leaves the runtime READY when the initial assembly fails to load (`engine/src/scripting/script_runtime.cpp:139`). Documentation only, as planned. The initialize summary in script_runtime.h now says a host-start or table-validation failure leaves the runtime DISABLED, while a failed config.gameAssemblyPath load returns ASSEMBLY_LOAD_FAILED and leaves it READY without an assembly (03_scripting.md 6). The code is unchanged. scripts-and-components.md describes the same behaviour.
- **F434** MINOR Leaky-timer reload test cannot fail for the right reason (`tests/scripting/test_script_hot_reload.cpp:106`). The leaky-timer case now calls fixture.run_frame() after the Variant check and before the reload, with the comment from the plan. Verified by experiment: with Arm=false the frame brings zombieAssemblies to 0 and the '== 1' check fails, so the case now fails for the right reason. The experiment was reverted.
- **F439** MINOR Every GetScript<T>() allocates a std::vector on the native side (`engine/src/scripting/native_api_core.cpp:214`). entry_script_get_slots now reads the script_set in place (world.get<script_component> then the const store().find) instead of scripts_of(). Set order and counting past capacity are unchanged. Added includes for script_component.h and <utility>.
- **F440** MINOR dispatch_collision_events allocates a record vector on every fixed step with contacts (`engine/src/scripting/script_runtime.cpp:318`). Added the private member mCollisionRecords to script_runtime. dispatch_collision_events clears it and reserves into it instead of using a local vector. A comment notes the function is not re-entrant.
- **F441** MINOR load_game_assembly and reload_game_assembly are the same function; remove_script ignores its entity parameter (`engine/src/scripting/script_runtime.cpp:239`). remove_script now returns with a DEBUG log when no world is attached, the entity has no script_component, or its setHandle differs from id.setHandle. load and reload stay one function, as documented. The header summary is extended.
- **F442** MINOR Fault log text is wrong for OnCreate faults and absent when the slot is not mapped yet (`engine/src/scripting/script_runtime.cpp:663`). mFaultedBeforeMapped is now a vector of faulted_slot{slot, callback}. A new private const helper log_faulted() writes 'OnCreate of '<class>' (slot N) threw: every callback of the instance is disabled' for CREATE and keeps the 'three consecutive faults' text otherwise. It is called from the mapped path of mark_faulted and where process_pending_creates applies the deferred FAULTED flag. Docs for the FAULTED flag and mark_faulted are updated.
- **F443** MINOR Mirror-mismatch warnings are deduplicated forever (`engine/src/scripting/script_runtime.cpp:985`). mWarnedMirrorNames is now cleared in reload_internal step 5 (successful loads only), in shutdown() and in attach_world's detach block. The note_mirror_mismatch summary is updated.
- **F444** MINOR script_register_class outside a load opens a staging table that is never committed (`engine/src/scripting/native_api_core.cpp:145`). entry_script_register_class now logs a WARN and returns when staging is closed, instead of calling begin_staging(). stage_field already refuses a closed staging, so the field entry needed no change.
- **F454** MINOR A failed automatic reload is never retried (`engine/src/scripting/hot_reload.cpp:81`). hot_reload_controller has new private members WATCH_RETRY_LIMIT = 3, mWatchRetriesLeft and mWatchTriggered. request() clears mWatchTriggered. poll_assembly_file watches the newer of the .dll and .pdb stamps and arms the retry state when it requests. perform_reload re-queues a watched ASSEMBLY_LOAD_FAILED as PENDING with mRequestTime = -1 and a WARN, up to 3 times; otherwise it keeps the old WARN and clears the flag. The set_watch_interval_seconds doc, the wiki and the handbook are updated.
- **F455** MINOR Serialising a script_component without a live runtime silently writes an empty script list (`engine/src/scripting/script_component.cpp:306`). write_script_component_json writes the empty form only for INVALID_SET_HANDLE. A real set without a live runtime now returns INVALID_STATE ('its scripts would be lost'). The script_component_serializer doc, the wiki, the handbook and the README are updated.
- **F456** MINOR mapped_slot_count() scans the whole slot map; stats() calls it (`engine/src/scripting/script_component.cpp:208`). script_store keeps mMappedCount, updated in map_slot (when the cell was invalid), unmap_slot (when it was valid), remove_entry's re-pointing (when the cell was invalid) and clear(). mapped_slot_count() is now an inline accessor and the old scan is removed.
- **F699** MINOR is_slot_started is documented as a mutator (`engine/include/conjure/scripting/null_script_host.h:279`). Documentation only: the is_slot_started summary in null_script_host.h now describes the query (live and either started by FIXED_UPDATE/UPDATE or created ALREADY_STARTED). The code is unchanged.
- **F700** MINOR field_blob_writer::finish claims an overflowed blob is 'complete and valid'; it is not readable (`engine/include/conjure/scripting/script_fields.h:97`). Documentation only: the field_blob_writer::finish summary in script_fields.h now says an overflowed output is not a valid blob and the caller should retry with the returned size. Confirmed against script_fields.cpp: finish() patches the full field count and put() drops every piece from the first one that does not fit. The code is unchanged.
- **F701** MINOR commit_staging has no 'staging open' guard and wipes the active schema when called without begin_staging (`engine/src/scripting/script_class_registry.cpp:103`). commit_staging now asserts mStagingOpen and returns early, so the active table is untouched in Distribution too. Added the conjure/core/assert.h include and the header doc.

### bindings (18) (commit `b010448`)

- **F163** MAJOR entity_set_enabled inside a query scope: disable then re-enable leaves the entity disabled (`engine/src/bindings/entries_entity.cpp:133`). entries_entity.cpp entity_set_enabled: the live-state early-out moved below the deferred branch. Inside a scope every request is now queued as a SET_ENABLED fixup and applied in call order; scene::set_enabled already ignores an unchanged state. A locked world without a runtime still answers 0 for a request that changes nothing and STRUCTURE_LOCKED otherwise. entity_is_enabled reports the pre-flush state.
- **F167** MAJOR Raycast and shape-cast max_distance is not validated: float.PositiveInfinity produces NaN ray components (`engine/src/bindings/entries_physics.cpp:183`). entries_physics.cpp: new valid_distance() helper refuses a non-finite or negative max_distance with INVALID_ARGUMENT. It is called after unit_direction in physics_raycast, physics_raycast_all and physics_shape_cast (query->ray.max_distance). Zero is still allowed and misses. csharp-api.md Physics paragraph documents the rule and the absence of an infinite ray.
- **F432** MAJOR Wiki says Diagnostics.Draw.* reaches the debug overlay; in the engine it never does (`docs/api/bindings/csharp-api.md:126`). docs/api/bindings/csharp-api.md: the false 'Diagnostics.Draw.* reaches the overlay' sentence is replaced by a [!NOTE] box. It says Line/Box/Sphere are validated and counted but draw nothing in an application yet (09 section 4 item 15(e)), that a hand-built binding_context with debugDraw draws, and that Text never draws. I checked that engine_context still has no debug_draw.
- **F452** MINOR asset_ref_bind creates the permanent slot before the type check that refuses the bind (`engine/src/bindings/entries_asset.cpp:79`). entries_asset.cpp resolve(): the ASSET_TYPE_MISMATCH check now runs before the slot_policy::BIND block, so a refused asset_ref_bind no longer creates a permanent slot.
- **F453** MINOR set_linear/angular_velocity write the component mirror before physics accepts the request (`engine/src/bindings/entries_physics.cpp:358`). entries_physics.cpp physics_set_linear_velocity / physics_set_angular_velocity: the command is queued first and the rigid_body mirror is written only on success, so a refused request (non-finite vector, COMMAND_QUEUE_FULL) no longer lands through the push diff.
- **F562** MINOR Wiki lists an asset tag `BinaryAsset` that the C# SDK does not define (`docs/api/bindings/csharp-api.md:115`). docs/api/bindings/csharp-api.md: removed the nonexistent `BinaryAsset` from the asset-tag list. It now matches managed/Conjure.Managed/Api/AssetTypes.cs (nine tags) and 15_bindings.md 2.5.
- **F564** MINOR Bindings README and handbook still say the editor does not add the bindings subsystem (`engine/src/bindings/README.md:83`). engine/src/bindings/README.md 'Using it': it now says runtime/src/main.cpp adds the subsystem and the editor adds it in editor_application::start_engine (engine/src/editor/core/editor_application.cpp:232, checked). The stale 'Open requests' paragraph is deleted. Handbook bindings.md section 2 now names both executables, and the editor clause is removed from section 10.
- **F666** MINOR Wiki says a mismatched mirror makes the engine refuse the assembly at start-up; the check is lazy and only throws on first use (`docs/api/bindings/csharp-api.md:77`). csharp-api.md 'Components and mirrors': size and layout hash are checked on first use and again after re-initialisation (ComponentType<T>). A mismatch is logged and never cached; HasComponent/GetComponent/TryGetComponent/AddComponent/SetComponent/RemoveComponent/ComponentQuery.Begin then throw InvalidOperationException; the assembly still loads. index.md mental-model item 3 now says the check happens the first time a mirror is used. Checked against ComponentType.cs, Entity.cs and native_api_core.cpp.
- **F689** MINOR A deferred reparent or enable that the scene refuses is reported as success and only logged at DEBUG (`engine/src/bindings/native_api_fill.cpp:469`). native_api_fill.cpp apply_pending_fixups: a fixup the scene refuses now logs at WARN ('deferred scene fixup on entity {} refused by the scene: ... (the request already returned success)'); the dead-entity skip stays at DEBUG. entries_transform.cpp reparent(): a deferred self-parent is refused at the call with INVALID_ARGUMENT, with no cycle or depth checks at queue time. README, handbook section 4, the entry_support.h summary and the csharp-api.md DOD warning describe the behaviour.
- **F690** MINOR apply_pending_fixups moves the queue into a fresh local vector, so every scope with a fixup allocates and frees (`engine/src/bindings/native_api_fill.cpp:447`). native_api_fill.cpp: new file-scope sApplyingScratch vector. apply_pending_fixups clears it, swaps it with sPendingFixups, iterates it and clears it again (capacity kept), so neither queue loses its storage. Re-entrancy is unchanged: sApplyingFixups blocks nested applies and observers queue into the emptied sPendingFixups. I did not clear sApplyingScratch in clear_active_context, unlike the plan: it is always empty outside an apply, and clearing it from inside an apply would invalidate the loop. Added #include <utility>.
- **F691** MINOR asset_load starts the load before it knows a script_residency exists (`engine/src/bindings/entries_asset.cpp:219`). entries_asset.cpp asset_load: a null script_residency now returns NOT_SUPPORTED before manager()->load(), so no slot is bound and no IO is queued. hold() keeps its own null check.
- **F692** MINOR asset_ref_bind creates the permanent slot before the type check that can still fail (`engine/src/bindings/entries_asset.cpp:80`): same as F452.
- **F693** MINOR asset_state_of returns UNLOADED for an unknown uuid; the README says NOT_FOUND, and it skips the null-uuid check the sibling entries have (`engine/src/bindings/entries_asset.cpp:272`). entries_asset.cpp asset_state_of: a null uuid is refused with INVALID_ARGUMENT, like its siblings. An unknown uuid still answers UNLOADED, which an existing test pins on purpose. README (and handbook section 3) corrected: asset_state_of is the exception to NOT_FOUND for unknown uuids.
- **F694** MINOR An out-of-range index maps to different status codes (and C# exception types) across entries (`engine/src/bindings/entries_physics.cpp:601`). entries_physics.cpp physics_layers_collide: an unknown layer index is now NOT_FOUND, matching physics_layer_name and 15_bindings.md 2.2 ('NOT_FOUND // path, name, layer, module, guid'). scene_loaded_path and the module entries are unchanged. csharp-api.md and the handbook note the KeyNotFoundException.
- **F696** MINOR diagnostics_draw_box constructs a debug_draw_buffer (two std::vectors) per call and submit() copies it (`engine/src/bindings/entries_diagnostics.cpp:150`). entries_diagnostics.cpp diagnostics_draw_box: twelve edges now go into a function-local static renderer::debug_draw_buffer that is cleared on each call (capacity kept; the main-thread guard makes the static safe) and submitted. debug_draw::line now writes immediates directly, but twelve line() calls would take the mutex twelve times, or create twelve timed entries for a timed box, so the scratch is still the right bindings-local fix.
- **F697** MINOR from_status negates INT32_MIN: signed overflow (`engine/include/conjure/bindings/bindings_error.h:59`). bindings_error.h from_status: the negation is now `0u - static_cast<u32>(status)`, which is well-defined. INT32_MIN gives 0x80000000, which is out of range, so the result is INTERNAL. The summary mentions INT32_MIN.
- **F698** MINOR Wiki page says the context carries debug draw and that the runtime refuses to start without the subsystem; neither is true (`docs/api/bindings/native-table.md:26`). docs/api/bindings/native-table.md, three corrections. 'debug draw' is removed from the services the context carries, with a note that debugDraw stays null (09 section 4 item 15(e)). The 'runtime refuses to start' claim is replaced: the handshake checks only the core entries, NativeBridge.MissingEntryCount is logged at DEBUG, and facades throw NotSupportedException. The IMPORTANT box now says the subsystem's startup refuses null owned slots (TABLE_INCOMPLETE) and the handshake validates size, version, hash and the core entries. Checked against native_api.cpp:202-238, bindings_subsystem.cpp:95-107 and NativeBridge.cs:107-124.
- **F767** MINOR Handbook and bindings README claim the editor does not install the bindings subsystem; it does (`docs/handbook/modules/bindings.md:149`). Same change as F564 (both items target the same stale sentences): README 'Using it' names the editor's editor_application::start_engine, the README 'Open requests' paragraph is deleted, and handbook section 10 no longer claims editor/main.cpp lacks the subsystem; it now starts with the debug_draw item.

### physics (18) (commit `3057ebd`)

- **F055** BLOCKER KINEMATIC_MOVES_WITH_TRANSFORM bodies carry an interpolated_transform nobody ever writes, so they render at the world origin (`engine/src/physics/physics_world_sync.cpp:276`). push_hierarchy_rows now writes interpolated_transform for KINEMATIC_MOVES_WITH_TRANSFORM rows (previous = firstPull ? pose : current; current = pose). firstPull is only cleared once the component exists, so a component still waiting in a deferred buffer cannot cause interpolation from a stale pose. The push system already declares interpolated_transform as a write.
- **F056** MAJOR interpolated_transform is added default-constructed, so a new dynamic body or character renders at the origin until its first pull (`engine/src/physics/physics_world_bodies.cpp:267`). add_interpolated now queues add_raw with a payload of previous = current = compose_world_pose(target), so a new dynamic body or character no longer renders at the origin before its first pull.
- **F057** MAJOR A body that is STATIC at creation keeps an interpolated_transform queued while it was still DYNAMIC/pending (`engine/src/physics/physics_world_bodies.cpp:543`). create_body calls remove_interpolated for a STATIC motion and add_interpolated otherwise. A component queued while the entity was pending as the default DYNAMIC rigid_body (later switched to STATIC, or removed so the colliders make an implicit static body) is now taken off.
- **F060** MAJOR shape_build_result::pendingAsset is ignored: a body with one loaded and one unloaded collider is built without the latter and never repaired (`engine/src/physics/shape_builder.cpp:226`). build_shape now checks asset readiness before building or logging anything (convex: get() null; mesh on a non-DYNAMIC body: shape not ready). When an asset is missing it returns pendingAsset with a null shape. create_body keeps the entity pending (comment added). In apply_rebuilds, a body with a pending asset keeps its current shape and its SHAPE rebuild is queued again for the next push. The now-unreachable pendingAsset assignments in append_convex and append_mesh are plain returns with a comment.
- **F062** MAJOR rigid_body with mass 0 on a STATIC or KINEMATIC mesh_collider body hands Jolt zero mass properties (assert, infinite inverse mass) (`engine/src/physics/physics_world_bodies.cpp:498`). In create_body, a body with mass <= 0 that has a rigid_body and whose shape has no mass (a mesh) now uses CalculateInertia with 1 kg; the other cases are unchanged. Jolt's MotionProperties assert no longer fires for STATIC or KINEMATIC mesh bodies. The wiki row now says 'a shape without volume, a mesh, gets 1 kg'.
- **F140** MAJOR glTF importers never call cgltf_validate: an accessor larger than its buffer crashes the editor at startup (`engine/src/physics/collision_mesh_importer.cpp:256`): same as F422.
- **F407** MAJOR raycast_all truncates to the first hits the broad phase visits, not the nearest ones; the test cannot tell (`engine/src/physics/physics_world_queries.cpp:57`). span_ray_collector now keeps the out.size() nearest hits. Once the span is full, a nearer hit replaces the farthest one kept and UpdateEarlyOutFraction follows the farthest kept fraction (which only shrinks); ForceEarlyOut is gone. Updated: the raycast_all header summary, the wiki row and API summary, the README Queries bullet and the handbook.
- **F422** MAJOR glTF collision import trusts accessors: no cgltf_validate, no per-primitive triangle check, mirrored nodes flip winding (`engine/src/physics/collision_mesh_importer.cpp:257`). (0) cgltf_read_side_file rejects an external buffer shorter than the declared *size with COLLISION_MESH_PARSE_FAILED / cgltf_result_data_too_short. (1) parse_gltf calls cgltf_validate after cgltf_load_buffers. (2) append_node fails a triangle primitive whose index (or vertex) count is not a multiple of three, and appends corners in (0,2,1) order when det(mat3(world)) < 0, via a new append_triangle helper. (3) collision_mesh_importer::VERSION is now 2, shared with F423. make_ramp_glb in the test file now takes a ramp_layout (node transform, POSITION count, index ranges).
- **F409** MINOR STAY events can carry a zero point and normal taken from a REMOVED record (`engine/src/physics/physics_world_contacts.cpp:210`). collect_events now tracks firstLive (the first ADDED or PERSISTED record of a run). ENTER still uses firstAdded; STAY uses firstLive and falls back to firstAny only when no live record survived.
- **F411** MINOR Rebuilds that replace the body (rigid_body removed/added on a collider entity) leave one fixed step without any body (`engine/src/physics/physics_world_bodies.cpp:751`). push_transforms now calls apply_rebuilds before flush_pending_creates, with a comment, so a rebuild that replaces a body (for example a rigid_body removed so the colliders make an implicit static body) is recreated in the same push. Added a README deviation bullet: the design (3.4) runs the flush first. Done together with F057.
- **F415** MINOR detach() leaves interpolated_transform on every former body (`engine/src/physics/physics_world.cpp:319`). detach now: clears ownDeferred, queues removal of interpolated_transform for live CHARACTER and non-STATIC rows and for pending entities with a character_controller or non-STATIC rigid_body (new state::queue_interpolated_removals), destroys the bodies, then flushes ownDeferred if anything is queued (asserting instead if the ECS world is locked). Updated the header summary and both wiki detach rows.
- **F419** MINOR Absolute 1e-4 teleport epsilon causes spurious teleports of parented bodies far from the origin (`engine/src/physics/physics_world_sync.cpp:297`). Added state::TELEPORT_RELATIVE_EPSILON = 16 * FLT_EPSILON (physics_world_internal.h), a 4-argument pose_differs overload and max_abs_component in sync.cpp. Only the parented branch of push_hierarchy_rows uses positionEpsilon = max(TELEPORT_EPSILON, max(\|pose\|, \|lastPulled\|, \|local\|) * relative); root rows keep the absolute tolerance.
- **F420** MINOR A body that falls asleep keeps interpolated previous != current, so its render pose keeps blending between the last two poses (`engine/src/physics/physics_world_sync.cpp:508`). pull_chunk and pull_parented_rows now write previous = current when the row is not awake (firstPull \|\| !awakeNow), so a body that falls asleep holds a still render pose.
- **F423** MINOR Welding registers only the first vertex of a grid cell, so later duplicates of a second vertex in that cell are not welded (`engine/src/physics/collision_mesh_importer.cpp:495`). weld_vertices now uses headOfCell plus a nextInCell chain. Every welded vertex is registered in its cell, all 27 neighbour chains are walked, and the lowest index within reach wins, so the result stays deterministic and 'first occurrence wins'. VERSION bump is shared with F422.
- **F424** MINOR Write-only result flags and unused accessors; the job adapter has no unit test (`engine/src/physics/shape_builder.h:45`). Removed dead code: shape_build_result::mixedTriggers/invalidScale/unsupportedShape/createFailed and their assignments, cached_leaf's createFailed parameter, the out parameter of append_box/sphere/capsule, body_record::hasShape and its two writes, contact_collector::recorded_count, and jolt_job_system::executed_by_helpers/queued_count with their counters. Dropped push_changed_components' fixedDeltaTime parameter. The WARN logs are kept, and so is pendingAsset (used by F060). Reworded the shape_builder file comment. Added deviation notes to the README and handbook for the missing Jolt-free job-adapter unit test.
- **F425** MINOR Wall-clock assertion in a default unit test (`tests/physics/test_component_sync.cpp:308`). In 'component_sync - pull over 2000 sleeping bodies allocates nothing [bench]', the wall-clock CHECK(pullMicroseconds < 50000) is now an informative MESSAGE. The allocation gate is unchanged.
- **F426** MINOR Queued commands and ray queries accept non-finite input (`engine/src/physics/physics_world.cpp:440`). state::queue returns core INVALID_ARGUMENT, without queueing, for a non-finite vectorA, vectorB or rotation. This covers every command, set_gravity included; the is_finite helpers live in physics_world_internal.h. raycast and raycast_all return nothing for a non-finite origin or direction and clamp maxDistance to MAX_RAY_DISTANCE = 1e6 (infinity means unlimited, and hit.distance uses the clamped length). shape_cast returns nullopt for a non-finite start position, rotation or direction. Header comments and wiki updated.
- **F428** MINOR body_pose reads the row tables before checking busy; attach has no owner-thread check; the 'any thread' query contract omits structural changes (`engine/src/physics/physics_world.cpp:555`). body_pose checks busy before find_row. attach asserts and returns WRONG_THREAD off the owner thread before touching anything. The attach summary and the query comment in physics_world.h now state the structural-change constraint; the same sentence is in the wiki warning, README and handbook.
