### tools (14) (commit `08336e3`)

- **F121** MAJOR strip_comments treats C++14 digit separators as character literals, blanking real code from every code-based rule (real files affected today) (`tools/check_style.py:165`). check_style.strip_comments now treats C++14 digit separators as code (is_digit_separator), reads raw strings as one literal (raw_string_end, prefixes u8R/uR/UR/LR/R, delimiter up to 16 chars), ends ordinary literals at an unescaped newline (literal_end) and keeps every newline when blanking (blank_literal). Across the repo no file loses newlines any more (19 did before). The only newly visible finding is std::thread in tests/core/strings/test_string_id.cpp, so that file is now listed under forbidden_in_tests in tools/style_exceptions.json with the reason from the plan.
- **F122** MINOR Any prototype with a default argument starting with 0 is classified as pure virtual and never reported (`tools/check_exports.py:226`). check_exports: removed the '= default/= delete/= 0' substring test from is_out_of_line_method and excluded '=' from FUNCTION_PATTERN's trailing-return group. Added a comment explaining why pure, defaulted and deleted declarations never match. Default arguments no longer hide prototypes, and trailing-return pure/default/delete members are no longer reported. 305 public headers still give 0 findings.
- **F123** MINOR third-party-include is a deny-list: any third-party header not in the hard-coded list passes in a public header (`tools/check_style.py:362`). third-party-include is now an allow-list (is_allowed_public_include). A public header may include 'conjure/...' headers, bare C++ standard library names, and glm only inside conjure/core/math/glm_types.h. A quoted non-Conjure include, or any angle include containing '/' or '.', is reported. An angle-bracket conjure/ include is left to include-order, so it is not reported twice. THIRD_PARTY_INCLUDE_ROOTS and THIRD_PARTY_FLAT_HEADER_PREFIXES stay for include-order. Updated the docstring and build-pipeline.md (section 8 dependency step and the 4.3 warning). No new findings in the repo.
- **F125** MINOR Tests carrying CTest's DISABLED property pass the skip gate (`tools/ctest_check_skips.py:69`). ctest_check_skips: added DISABLED_REASON and a skipped_test.disabled field. parse_junit now also collects testcases with status="disabled" (no <skipped> child), and unexpected_skips always reports a disabled test, whatever its labels and tags. The docstring and the summary/hint messages are updated. Checked against real CTest: a DISABLED test is written as status="disabled" with no <skipped> child, and the parser returns it.
- **F334** MINOR build.py cannot drive the windows-vs preset (`tools/build.py:165`). build.py: added PRESETS_FILE and USER_PRESETS_FILE, load_presets(root) (reads CMakePresets.json plus CMakeUserPresets.json; malformed JSON raises ValueError naming the file), and resolve_presets(name, presets, need_build). resolve_presets follows `inherits` chains for configurePreset and configuration and finds the test preset of the same name. A configure-only preset is rejected when building and the error lists its build presets. main() turns a ValueError into parser.error, rejects --test without a test preset, uses the configure preset for configure, the binary dir and --clean, and adds --config / -C on the --build-dir path. The help text and docstring are updated. Checked: windows-vs → error listing windows-vs-debug and windows-vs-dev; an unknown preset → error; windows-vs-dev --test → no test preset.
- **F543** MINOR More annotation false negatives: final / ref-qualified / volatile members, static data members, prototypes after a semicolon-less macro, decltype(auto), and the shared tokenizer bug (`tools/check_exports.py:68`). check_exports: the FUNCTION_PATTERN qualifier group now accepts const, volatile, &, &&, noexcept(...), override and final, plus override/final after a trailing return. Leading lines that are a semicolon-less upper-case macro invocation are dropped (leading_macro_lines_length), and the function is reported on the prototype's own line. decltype(...) is replaced before the name lookup. STATIC_DATA_MEMBER marks classes with out-of-line static data members; inline and constexpr members are excluded, and the lookahead is written so extra spaces cannot bypass it. The tokenizer fix from F121 is copied into strip_comments_and_strings. 305 headers still give 0 findings.
- **F544** MINOR windows-macro misses anonymous/unnamed-typed enums and every constant that is not constexpr (`tools/check_style.py:122`). windows-macro: ENUM_PATTERN accepts anonymous and unnamed-typed enums (\benum\b with an optional name). CONSTANT_PATTERN matches constexpr\|const; a const name is reported only when it is ALL_CAPS, with a comment citing CONVENTIONS 1. The new DEFINE_PATTERN reports `#define <Windows macro name>`. Docstring updated. No new findings in the repo.
- **F547** MINOR tests-mirror-headers matches by file stem anywhere under the module, and does not cover the benchmark harness headers (`tools/check_style.py:508`). tests-mirror-headers now looks for test_<stem>.cpp specifically (rglob). When several exist, it uses the one in the header's own sub-directory, or reports 'several ...; keep the test in the header's sub-directory'. A test file counts only if a CMakeLists.txt in its directory or an ancestor up to the test root lists it, and a file claimed by a second header is reported. Benchmark harness headers are now covered: tests/benchmarks/include/conjure/bench/*.h → tests/benchmarks/tests, key bench/<name>.h. Added the conjure/core/math.h exception and updated the docstring. The real repo stays clean.
- **F548** MINOR A mistyped path argument crashes with a traceback (exit 1) instead of a usage error (exit 2) (`tools/check_style.py:191`). Added class usage_error. iter_source_files raises it for a missing path, a path outside --root, a non-source file, and paths that name no source. load_exceptions and exception_table raise it for malformed JSON, a non-object, or a table that does not map paths to strings. main() computes both tables and the file list inside one try and prints `check_style: <message>` to stderr with exit 2.
- **F550** MINOR --tool with a bare program name is reported as 'not found' although the help says it is searched on PATH (`tools/check_distribution_symbols.py:193`). Both find_tool functions (check_distribution_symbols.py, check_exports.py) now take which=shutil.which and resolve a bare --tool name on PATH; the kind is still derived from the stem. check_distribution_symbols.build_configuration returns (None, False) when --config disagrees with CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE of a single-config tree (annotated tuple[str \| None, bool]), and run_build_dir prints the planned message and returns EXIT_USAGE. scan_symbol_text skips 'Dump of file' header lines. The --tool help text, docstrings and the check_exports self-test case count are updated.
- **F554** MINOR Manifest defects: Sponza.gltf is saved as Sponza.glb, a placeholder entry points at a README, and optional/flag handling in the downloader is off (`tools/test_assets.json:24`). Removed the optional Sponza.glb entry (it downloaded a .gltf) and the disabled README placeholder from tools/test_assets.json. download() now removes the .part file on any failure and re-raises. main(argv=None) uses parse_args(argv), and --verify and --force are mutually exclusive (exit 2). tests/.assets_ready is written whenever every required asset is present and verified, and removed otherwise; the run exits 0 only when every required asset is present and nothing failed, so an optional failure exits 1 but keeps the stamp. While validating I found that the plan's 'return 0 only when there are no failures' would make --verify with a missing required asset exit 0, because a missing file is not recorded as a failure. The exit code therefore also requires required_present, and a regression case covers it. Docstring updated.
- **F557** MINOR Page-length rule is off by one and the link rule has false positives and negatives (`tools/check_docs.py:59`). check_docs: the line count is now len(text.splitlines()). Links are collected by link_targets(), which runs on text with fenced blocks (backtick or tilde fences, any indent, unterminated runs to the end) and inline code spans blanked. It reads the existing LINK_PATTERN plus REFERENCE_DEFINITION (footnotes excluded) and HTML_HREF, and each target goes through a dangling_target() helper. Docstring now says TODO/FIXME/XXX. In docs/handbook/documentation.md, the 4.2 'links' row is rewritten as planned, the 'length' row notes that a final newline does not count, and 2.2 says link syntax inside code is not a link. Compared with the old checker, the only targets that disappear across docs/api and docs/handbook are the four code examples in documentation.md; docs.check stays clean.
- **F559** MINOR Generator silently drops or invents table entries and mangles type names containing 'const' (`tools/generate_native_api.py:55`). generate_native_api: the ENTRY_PATTERN return group allows '*'. /* */ comments and preprocessor directive lines (with continuations) are blanked, keeping offsets and newlines; blanking directives also stops an #error line that mentions CONJURE_NATIVE_FN(...) from being parsed as an entry. The number of CONJURE_NATIVE_FN( invocations is compared with the parsed entries, and a mismatch raises SystemExit with the planned message. cs_type removes const only as a word. Docstring says 'four outputs' and describes the new rules. `--check` stays up to date (126 entries, hash 0x4B06362D38B412A6).
- **F788** MINOR Sponza entry saves a .gltf under a .glb name, and the audio entry points at a README (`tools/test_assets.json:22`): same as F554.

### rhi (13) (commit `a9f952c`)

- **F293** MAJOR std::filesystem::path::string() in error and log messages throws on Windows for paths outside the ANSI code page (`engine/src/rhi/shader_library.cpp:129`). Added display_path() to the anonymous namespace of shader_library.cpp. It formats a path through path::u8string(). It replaces all 12 path::string() calls in error and log messages (parse_index, read_blob, open, reload). Updated the README and the handbook 3.7 paragraph.
- **F288** MINOR D3D12 acquire() succeeds on a swapchain that has no back buffers after a failed resize (`engine/src/rhi/backends/d3d12/swapchain_d3d12.cpp:141`). In the D3D12 backend, acquire() and present() now return OUT_OF_DATE when the swapchain does not have every back buffer wrapped. This is the state after a resize fails in ResizeBuffers or while wrapping. The check is a private has_back_buffers() (mBackBuffers.size() == mDesc.bufferCount) in swapchain_d3d12.cpp/.h, and the error wording matches Vulkan's unusable_error(). Updated the acquire/present/resize docs in swapchain.h, the OUT_OF_DATE comment in rhi_error.h, the README D3D12 paragraph, the handbook section 4 table, failure table and tests table, and the API page's resize paragraph and TIP (OUT_OF_DATE is no longer described as Vulkan-only).
- **F289** MINOR A failure half-way through wrap_back_buffers leaves a partial buffer list, contradicting 'buffer_count() is 0 after a failed resize' (`engine/src/rhi/backends/d3d12/swapchain_d3d12.cpp:236`). In wrap_back_buffers(), release_back_buffers() is now called before both SWAPCHAIN_CREATION_FAILED returns (GetBuffer failure and an NVRHI wrap refusal). A failed wrap therefore always leaves buffer_count() == 0 as swapchain.h promises, never a partial list. The new has_back_buffers() check from F288 also covers a partial list.
- **F290** MINOR Vulkan acquire() can return SWAPCHAIN_CREATION_FAILED, which swapchain.h and the README reserve for create/resize (`engine/src/rhi/backends/vulkan/swapchain_vulkan.cpp:225`). In swapchain_vulkan.cpp, any vkAcquireNextImageKHR failure other than device loss and out-of-date/surface-lost now returns PRESENT_FAILED instead of SWAPCHAIN_CREATION_FAILED. Updated to match: the swapchain.h acquire doc, the PRESENT_FAILED comment in rhi_error.h, the README 'Present failures' paragraph, the Vulkan acquire cell in the handbook (anything else is PRESENT_FAILED) and the failure-table row (now 'present, acquire').
- **F292** MINOR device_type_rank's preferSoftware branch and CPU rank are unreachable (`engine/src/rhi/backends/vulkan/device_vulkan.cpp:62`). In device_vulkan.cpp, device_type_rank now takes only the device type. The unreachable preferSoftware branch is gone and the doc comment explains that select_physical_device already filters CPU and non-CPU devices. The call site is updated. Behaviour is unchanged.
- **F294** MINOR index_entry::sourceHash is parsed and stored but never read (`engine/src/rhi/shader_library.cpp:79`). Removed index_entry::sourceHash and its assignment from shader_library.cpp. A comment explains why the field is not read. Added a sentence to the README, the handbook 3.7 paragraph and the shaders.md API page saying shader_library ignores sourceHash because it serves the build's incremental rebuilds.
- **F295** MINOR get()/contains() build a heap std::string key per call, and contains() is noexcept (`engine/src/rhi/shader_library.cpp:306`). Dropped noexcept from shader_library::contains in shader_library.h and .cpp; the doc now says the call may allocate. Rewrote read_blob: it sizes the buffer with std::filesystem::file_size, returns IO_FAILURE for 'cannot open' and 'is empty', then does one stream.read and checks gcount. Removed the now-unused <iterator> include and added <cstdint>. The std::string lookup key stays, since lookups happen only at creation time.
- **F296** MINOR rhi to_string/parse conventions differ from platform's and from each other (`engine/src/rhi/backend.cpp:34`). to_string(graphics_backend) and to_string(shader_stage) now return "" for COUNT and other out-of-range values instead of "null" or "vertex". parse_graphics_backend now compares the trimmed view with core::str::iequals instead of building a lower-cased std::string. Updated the docs in backend.h (including the whitespace behaviour) and shader_library.h, the devices-and-swapchains.md backend paragraph and the shaders.md API summary. platform's parse_backend_kind is left alone, as the plan says.
- **F297** MINOR Two rhi test cases cannot fail at run time (CHECK(true)) and the 'mirrors platform' case does not compare with platform (`tests/rhi/test_native_window.cpp:49`). Renamed the case to 'native_window - kind is one byte, x11Window is 64 bits and the kind values follow 11_platform.md 2.4'. Rewrote its comment to say the engine converts platform::native_window_handle with a switch and a field-by-field copy (engine/src/engine/subsystems/rhi_subsystem.cpp, which I checked at :19-33 and :163-169), so the asserts pin rhi's own layout. CHECK(true) is kept per the repository pattern.
- **F683** MINOR writeBuffer/copyBuffer past the end of a null buffer are silently truncated and still counted as written (`engine/src/rhi/backends/null/null_command_list.cpp:145`). In null_command_list.cpp, writeBuffer and copyBuffer now range-check against BufferDesc::byteSize with an overflow-safe range_fits(). An out-of-range request reports an NVRHI Error through mDevice.messages() (a new private report_error) and stores nothing. writeBuffer goes through a new private store_buffer_write() that returns the bytes stored, so bufferWriteBytes counts only stored bytes while bufferWrites still counts every call. null_buffer::write keeps its clamp as a defensive guard (comment updated in null_resources.h). The bufferWrites/bufferWriteBytes comments in null_device_stats.h, the README, the handbook 3.4 table and the API 'Testing without a GPU' paragraph are updated.
- **F684** MINOR mapBuffer and mapStagingTexture ignore the CPU access mode; any buffer maps (`engine/src/rhi/backends/null/null_device.cpp:249`). In null_device.cpp, mapBuffer returns nullptr with an Error for a buffer created with CpuAccessMode::None, and mapStagingTexture does the same for a staging texture created with None. A different map mode or a second map is still legal, as on D3D12. createStagingTexture now also rejects depth == 0. README, handbook and API text are updated together with F683.
- **F685** MINOR State tracking claims to answer 'like a real backend' but ignores every implicit transition (`engine/src/rhi/backends/null/null_command_list.h:13`). Narrowed the contract in the null_command_list.h header comment, the README and the handbook 3.4 table. Only explicitly set or tracked states are reported, the implicit transitions of automatic barriers are not modelled, and an untracked resource reports initialState with keepInitialState, Unknown otherwise. Removed the dead member mAutomaticBarriers; setEnableAutomaticBarriers(bool) is now an empty override with an unnamed parameter.
- **F686** MINOR queryFormatSupport reports block-compressed formats as render targets and integer formats as filterable (`engine/src/rhi/backends/null/null_device.cpp:671`). null_device::queryFormatSupport now starts every format with Texture \| ShaderLoad and adds ShaderSample only for non-integer formats, before the depth early return, so D32 keeps ShaderSample. RenderTarget moved inside the !compressed block. Added a 'Format support' row to the handbook 3.4 table and a README sentence.

### audio (12) (commit `440f50d`)

- **F022** MAJOR A voice stopped while its group or the master is paused never ends and keeps its mixer slot (`engine/src/audio/mixer.cpp:268`). mixer::render_block (mixer.cpp:267-276) now ends a STOPPING voice of a paused group or under the master pause at once with STOPPED (no fade, not counted active), matching the existing PAUSED-voice STOP rule. Stopped, released and stolen voices under a pause now free their slot. Updated: stop() summary in audio_engine.h, playing-sounds.md, handbook 2.1/5 and README.
- **F023** MAJOR resume() before a startPaused voice gets its mixer slot is lost: the voice starts paused and stays paused (`engine/src/audio/audio_engine.cpp:560`). start_mixer_voice now sets play.startPaused = slot.userPaused (audio_engine.cpp:562). play() seeds userPaused from startPaused, so resume() or pause() on a PRIMING voice decides how it starts. Updated the resume() summary in audio_engine.h, playing-sounds.md, handbook 4.2 and README.
- **F183** MAJOR u64 overflow in the cooked clip header check: a crafted clip in a pack aborts the runtime (`engine/src/audio/clip_asset.cpp:279`). read_clip_blob_header now checks the PCM payload by division (clip_asset.cpp:278-294): dataBytes % (channels*4) == 0 and frameCount == dataBytes/(channels*4). A forged frameCount can no longer wrap the u64 product and reach the allocation in load_clip_blob. The error message names the payload frames and the declared count.
- **F317** MINOR select_device can fall back to the null backend without setting last_backend_error; an unknown device name 'succeeds' on the default device (`engine/src/audio/audio_engine.cpp:1247`). select_device now sets backendError to the restore failure before adopting the null backend (audio_engine.cpp:1257), as recover_device does. The unknown-name half is the designed behaviour (12_audio.md 3.9) and is now documented in the select_device summary, playing-sounds.md Devices, and handbook 9 plus the failure table.
- **F318** MINOR Voice, listener and group setters do not assert the main thread although the header says they are main-thread only (`engine/src/audio/audio_engine.cpp:1462`). Added CONJURE_ASSERT(core::jobs::is_main_thread(), "audio_engine::<name> is main-thread only") at the top of all 25 mutators, keeping their behaviour after the assert. Mutators: stop, stop_all, pause, resume, release, set_volume, set_pitch, set_pan, set_loop, set_spatial, set_priority, set_group, set_position, set_positions, set_attenuation, apply_params, apply_source, seek, set_listener, create_group, remove_group, set_group_gain, set_group_paused, set_master_gain, set_paused. Updated the audio_engine.h header comment, handbook 4.4 and failure table, README threading table and the playing-sounds.md warning.
- **F321** MINOR forceMono, sampleRate and normalize are silently ignored for streamed clips (`engine/src/audio/clip_asset.cpp:199`). audio_clip_importer::import now reads the cooked header and calls context.add_warning when forceMono, sampleRate or normalize is set and the clip was cooked STREAMED (clip_asset.cpp:379-391). The warning names the source path and how to get PCM. Updated the clip_import_settings and import() summaries in clip_asset.h, the clips-and-decoding.md note, handbook failure table and README.
- **F322** MINOR Loading a streamed Ogg clip decodes the whole stream once just to re-validate the header (`engine/src/audio/clip_asset.cpp:310`). load_clip_blob now checks a STREAMED payload with streaming_decoder::open(payload, header.frameCount), then info() and close(), instead of probe() (clip_asset.cpp:313-319). The header guarantees frameCount > 0, so a Vorbis stream is not decoded in full; the format comparison is unchanged. Updated the load_clip_blob summary in clip_asset.h, README, clips-and-decoding.md and the handbook failure table.
- **F323** MINOR clip blob size check can wrap; a forged frameCount reaches a huge allocation (`engine/src/audio/clip_asset.cpp:279`): same as F183.
- **F325** MINOR A failed streamed seek still moves the reported cursor to the requested frame (`engine/src/audio/audio_engine.cpp:780`). (1) streaming_pass sets slot.seekFrame = stream.decoder.position() after the reset, whether or not the seek landed, so the SEEK sent to the mixer and the reported cursor match the audio. The WARN now names the requested and actual frames. (2) seek() during WAIT_PRIME moves the phase back to WAIT_RESET; the mixer voice is still PAUSED there, so the ring is reset again for the new target. Updated the seek() summary in audio_engine.h, playing-sounds.md, handbook 7 and failure table, and README.
- **F326** MINOR Missing audio tests for behaviour that matters (`tests/audio/test_audio_engine.cpp:222`). No standalone change, as the plan says: the missing tests landed with their fixes (F022 engine and mixer stop/release/steal under a pause, F023 resume of a PRIMING startPaused voice, F317 select_device keep-previous and fall-back-to-null). The F024 (competing PRIMING voices) and F319 (parent group pause) tests belong with those fixes, which are not in this unit; adding them now would fail against current code.
- **F579** MINOR start() overwrites a LOST state published by the device thread with RUNNING (`engine/src/audio/miniaudio_backend.cpp:181`). miniaudio_backend::start now promotes STARTING to RUNNING with compare_exchange_strong (acq_rel) instead of an unconditional store (miniaudio_backend.cpp:181-184). A LOST that the notification callback publishes in that window now reaches recover_device. Documented in handbook 8 and README.
- **F638** MINOR play_params defaults to flat playback while audio_source defaults to spatial; volume vs gain naming (`engine/include/conjure/audio/voice.h:68`). Docs only: comments on play_params::spatial (voice.h) and audio_source::spatial (components.h) point at each other's different default. Added to playing-sounds.md: the spatial row note and the volume-vs-gain sentence below the play_params table. Added the reverse note to the audio_source table in docs/api/audio/index.md. No API or name change (design A11).

### ci (11) (commit `a234d9e`)

- **F030** MAJOR Nightly distribution-export (windows) cannot start the exported runtime: nethost.dll is never copied (`.github/workflows/nightly.yml:211`). nightly.yml 'Export and run' now copies build/<dist-preset>/bin/nethost.dll into build/export_out/ when it exists. That happens on Windows only, because MSVC scripting links the nethost import library (engine/src/scripting/CMakeLists.txt:38-56, 96-112) and --export never copies it (export_pipeline.cpp:335-349 copies it only on the copyRuntime path). The step comment now says what is copied and why.
- **F131** MINOR add-engine-module skill produces a module that fails docs.check and contradicts the real module layout (`.claude/skills/add-engine-module/SKILL.md:23`). add-engine-module skill rewritten in five places. (1) Layout: log_category.h is now the public engine/include/conjure/<name>/log_category.h using CONJURE_DECLARE_ENGINE_LOG_CATEGORY, with the .cpp defining it, plus tests/<name>/test_log_category.cpp, docs/handbook/modules/<name>.md and docs/api/<name>/ pages, modelled on the ecs files. (2) CMake sample: the header moved to PUBLIC_HEADERS and test_log_category.cpp is in the test SOURCES. The mirror rule now says log_category.h is included. (3) Step 5 now gives the CONJURE_API rule enforced by tools/check_exports.py (CTest exports.check_annotations, style-and-headers job). (4) New step 8 'Documentation': header-coverage exemptions, mkdocs nav and index registration, check_docs and mkdocs --strict. Build and test is renumbered to 9 and runs python tools/docs.py check. (5) Front-matter description now mentions documentation pages.
- **F538** MINOR Opening a project inside the repository dirties the working tree (layout ini, ai_port, generated .meta files) (`.gitignore:11`). .gitignore: '/.conjure/' and '**/.conjure/cache/' are replaced by '**/.conjure/'. A comment explains that <project>/.conjure holds the import cache, editor_layout.ini and ai_port (04_editor.md D12), and that .meta sidecars are deliberately not ignored. The old 'Dependency and import caches' heading now covers only /third_party/_deps/.
- **F552** MINOR The nightly benchmark job runs all scenarios in one process, where a SKIPPED scenario is invisible (`.github/workflows/nightly.yml:330`). nightly.yml 'Benchmarks' step: after the step summary is written, any 'status=SKIPPED' row in build/bench-<name>.txt raises ::error::, lists the rows and exits 1. The step comment cites D12 and explains why the runner's own exit 77 (all-skipped only) is not enough. No runner change was needed: every skip() call in tests/benchmarks is an unmet precondition, and the report goes to stdout through tee.
- **F563** MINOR build-and-test skill describes a CI leg that does not exist (`.claude/skills/build-and-test/SKILL.md:89`). The build-and-test skill's CI paragraph now lists the real ci.yml legs: windows-msvc-debug, windows-msvc-dev with the export smoke, linux-gcc-debug, linux-clang-sanitize (scripting off), macos-debug and style-and-headers. It says the Distribution build, the full nightly matrix, TSan, golden images and benchmarks run only in nightly.yml (job names given) and points to docs/handbook/ci.md.
- **F569** MINOR Root README neither links the wiki/handbook nor says how to run anything (`README.md:29`). README.md gains a '## Running' section. It covers the editor, a headless --export and running the export with conjure_runtime --project. Readers are told to copy tests/assets/projects/minimal into build/ first because of the .meta and .conjure/ writes. It also points to conjure_editor --help, docs/api/engine/running-the-engine.md and the editor-ai-control skill. The Documentation table gains docs/index.md, docs/api/index.md, docs/handbook/index.md and docs/handbook/getting-started.md.
- **F571** MINOR Review checklist allows plain enum for flag sets; CONVENTIONS forbids it (`.claude/skills/code-review-checklist/SKILL.md:37`). The code-review-checklist enum item now requires enum class with an explicit underlying type for every enumeration, flag sets included. Flag sets end in _flags, have NONE = 0 and use CONJURE_ENUM_FLAGS(type);. This matches docs/CONVENTIONS.md section 3. 'Walk the three sections' is now 'four sections'.
- **F577** MINOR AGENTS.md layout and configuration table drift (`AGENTS.md:58`). AGENTS.md changes: (1) the layout row is now 'samples/ sample projects (feature_test: wave 5)', using a continuation line to keep the columns aligned. (2) '(default)' is removed from Development, and a sentence now says tools/build.py defaults to the host Debug preset and a presetless configure falls back to Development. (3) The options list adds CONJURE_ENABLE_ASAN/UBSAN/TSAN, CONJURE_WARNINGS_AS_ERRORS, CONJURE_PLATFORM_GLFW, CONJURE_PHYSICS_CROSS_PLATFORM_DETERMINISTIC and CONJURE_TEST_GOLDEN_IMAGES.
- **F773** MINOR GitHub Actions are pinned to moving major tags, including a third-party action (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:87`). All 38 'uses:' references in ci.yml (11) and nightly.yml (27) are pinned to full commit SHAs with the version in a comment. Each SHA is the newest release inside the current major, resolved with git ls-remote; the annotated ilammy tag was peeled to its commit. ilammy/msvc-dev-cmd@0b201ec74fa43914dc39ae48a89fd1d8cb592756 # v1.13.0, actions/checkout@11d5960a326750d5838078e36cf38b85af677262 # v4.4.0, actions/setup-python@a26af69be951a213d495a4c3e4e4022e16d87065 # v5.6.0, actions/setup-dotnet@67a3573c9a986a3f9c594539f4ab511d57bb3ce9 # v4.3.1, actions/cache@0057852bfaa89a56745cba8c7296529d2fc39830 # v4.3.0, actions/upload-artifact@ea165f8d65b6e75b540449e92b4886f43607fa02 # v4.6.2. The with: blocks are unchanged.
- **F789** MINOR Protocol fuzz corpus depends on git's text auto-detection to keep its bytes (`.gitattributes:1`). .gitattributes gets a new commented line, 'tests/editor/data/protocol_corpus/** -text', so the fuzz corpus is never line-ending converted whatever git's auto-detection decides.
- **F791** MINOR .editorconfig has no section for the MSBuild project files (`.editorconfig:13`). .editorconfig has a new [*.{csproj,props,targets,manifest}] section with indent_size = 2, matching the two-space indentation of the four csproj files, managed/Directory.Build.props and runtime/src/conjure_runtime.manifest.

### modules (7) (commit `1371099`)

- **F335** MINOR discover() and clean_shadow_copy_cache() iterate directories with the throwing operator++ (`engine/src/modules/module_manager.cpp:130`). discover() (module_manager.cpp) and clean_shadow_copy_cache() (shadow_copy.cpp) now walk the directory with the non-throwing iterator.increment(failure), following native_file_system.cpp:288. In discover() the existing MANIFEST_NOT_FOUND 'cannot list module search directory' block moved after the loop, so a listing that fails at construction and one that fails halfway return the same error. Nothing is registered before that point, so the early return has no side effects. In clean_shadow_copy_cache() the trailing 'cannot list' warning still covers both cases. The README now says every directory walk is non-throwing.
- **F337** MINOR Every pending request on a FAULTED module nests its last error one level deeper (`engine/src/modules/module_manager.cpp:1075`). checked_record returns record.lastError unchanged when it already carries module_error::FAULTED, which is always the case for a record that detach_record faulted. It only wraps the error as a fallback when lastError holds another code. apply_one still logs the 'refused' warning but no longer overwrites lastError on a FAULTED record. Repeated load, unload and reload requests therefore no longer nest 'is faulted:' one level deeper each time, and the stored fault survives. The handbook (modules.md 3.3), the wiki (loading-and-managing.md) and the README are updated.
- **F338** MINOR Manifest path members (binary, managed, content) are not validated; '..' or an absolute path escapes the module root and the export directory (`engine/src/modules/module_manifest.cpp:242`). module_manifest.cpp gets two textual checks that treat '/' and '\' alike. is_base_name() rejects '/', '\', ':', '.' and '..'. stays_inside_root() rejects an empty entry, a leading separator, any ':' and any '..' segment. A non-empty 'binary' that fails is_base_name is MANIFEST_INVALID at /binary. read_string_array was renamed read_relative_paths and now rejects bad 'managed' and 'content' entries as MANIFEST_INVALID at /managed/<i> or /content/<i>. The header comment and the parse_module_manifest summary in module_manifest.h, the writing-a-module.md table plus a new paragraph, and the README are updated. No manifest in the repo (fixtures, samples, docs) is affected.
- **F341** MINOR load_module_manifest parses the file, re-serialises it and parses it again (`engine/src/modules/module_manifest.cpp:354`). The body of parse_module_manifest, from the root-object check to 'return manifest', moved unchanged apart from indentation into an anonymous-namespace parse_manifest_root(json::value root, std::string_view sourceName). parse_module_manifest parses the text and calls it. load_module_manifest calls it on the root of the document returned by parse_file, so the file is no longer re-serialised with to_string() and parsed a second time. The diff is large only because of the re-indent. The load_module_manifest summary in the header now says the file is parsed once.
- **F342** MINOR Unknown keys inside a dependency object are ignored without the warning that top-level unknown keys get (`engine/src/modules/module_manifest.cpp:106`). warn_unknown_keys was generalised to take the object, a span of known keys, the source and a JSON pointer. read_dependency calls it with KNOWN_DEPENDENCY_KEYS {name, version, optional} and its /dependencies/<i> pointer, and logs "{source}: unknown manifest key '{key}' at {pointer} ignored". The root call keeps its exact old message. The header comment, the wiki and the README are updated.
- **F343** MINOR Version grammar inconsistencies: ">= 1.0.0" is rejected while "^ 1.2.3" is accepted, and leading zeros parse (`engine/src/modules/module_version.cpp:152`). parse_component rejects a component longer than one character that starts with '0' (semver's no-leading-zero rule). The str::split(' ') loop in parse_version_range was replaced by a nextToken lambda that skips spaces and tabs. When '>=' or '<' stands alone, the version is taken from the next token, so '>= 1.0.0 < 2.0.0' and tab separators now parse, matching the existing '^ 1.2.3' leniency. Error texts and the sawLower/sawUpper checks are unchanged, and '>=' or '<' alone still fail. The module_version.h grammar comment and the parse_version summary are updated, and writing-a-module.md 'Versions and ranges' gets a paragraph on both rules.
- **F766** MINOR Modules handbook 'Open items' still lists the CONJURE_API-only export switch as waiting (`docs/handbook/modules/modules.md:325`). Deleted the stale 'Export-everything on MSVC ... waits for core to carry the annotations' bullet from docs/handbook/modules/modules.md section 10. The switch has landed: conjure_apply_export_settings in cmake/ConjureModule.cmake:94, and modules.md 7.2 lists exports.check_annotations and exports.count_shared_library. The platform_detect.h bullet stays.

### support (7) (commit `bc44d2f`)

- **F472** MINOR A test that cannot fail: headless_engine construction time (`tests/support/tests/engine/test_headless_engine.cpp:146`). The construction-time case only printed a MESSAGE. It is now renamed and also asserts seconds <= CONSTRUCTION_CEILING_SECONDS (0.25 s) through a CHECK_MESSAGE. That ceiling is 50x the 5 ms budget of 08_testing.md 8.2, and the comment says why the case does not check the budget itself. The CHECK_MESSAGE comes after the fixture scope, like the MESSAGE. Measured here: about 18 ms.
- **F566** MINOR Engine test-support README says conjure::editor_core does not exist yet (`tests/support/engine/README.md:79`). Docs only. tests/support/engine/README.md: the 'Protocol client transport' bullet now says editor_core exists (conjure/editor/protocol/loopback_transport.h). It says the connect_in_process (over editor::loopback_transport) and connect_tcp factories and the two design-11 cases are still owed, citing 09_cross_area_resolutions.md 4 item 15(e). It also says LOOPBACK_TCP is installed by tests/editor/editor_test_support.cpp, not by this library. The send_line sentence is unchanged. I checked every referenced fact against the tree.
- **F707** MINOR --conjure-update-golden never refreshes an existing reference, although 08_testing.md says references are regenerated with it (`tests/support/engine/src/golden_image.cpp:115`). golden_image::compare() now regenerates the reference in update mode when the reference is missing, unreadable (the load error is in the reason) or fails the comparison, size mismatch included. This goes through a file-local rewrite_reference() helper that sets the message, writes the reference, sets referenceWritten and passed, and logs a WARN. In that path no actual.png or diff.png is written. A passing comparison never rewrites. One deviation from the plan: save_image() now returns bool, and referenceWritten comes from that write itself. The plan used is_regular_file, which would report true for an existing reference even when the overwrite failed. Updated golden_image.h (file comment, compare() summary, referenceWritten comment) and README.md (table row, How to test).
- **F708** MINOR make_diff ignores the alpha channel that compare_images counts (`tests/support/engine/src/golden_image.cpp:225`). make_diff now computes the alpha delta per pixel and draws max(colour delta, alpha delta) x 8 (clamped) in each RGB channel, with output alpha kept at 255. An alpha-only failure now shows grey instead of an all-black diff. compare_images is unchanged. Updated the make_diff summary in golden_image.h.
- **F712** MINOR 'the DOTNET_RUNTIME precondition probe is installed' asserts nothing when .NET is present (`tests/support/tests/engine/test_headless_engine.cpp:648`). tests/support/tests/engine/CMakeLists.txt passes CONJURE_SUPPORT_ENGINE_TEST_MANAGED_DIR to conjure_support_engine_tests when conjure_managed_sdk exists. The test file falls back to "" when the define is missing. The probe case now computes its expectation on its own: Conjure.Managed.dll present AND scripting::is_runtime_available(). It then CHECKs precondition_holds(DOTNET_RUNTIME) == expected. On this machine the expectation is true. Without the library's probe installer, precondition_holds returns false (tests/support/src/test_environment.cpp:37-39), so the case would fail.
- **F716** MINOR input_script events scheduled for a frame that already ran are silently never delivered (`tests/support/engine/src/headless_engine.cpp:387`). input_script gains mFirstOpenFrame and mLateEventCount, close_frames_before(u64) noexcept (it never moves backwards) and late_event_count(). push() counts an event scheduled before the first open frame as late but still stores it, and clear() keeps both the count and the open frame. The file comment now says frames are absolute (schedule at frame_index() + n). headless_engine::step_frames(u32, f64) checks for new late events before its loop. If there are any, it reports them once with FAIL_CHECK (only the newly late ones), turns the memory check off, and records mLateEventsReported. After each ++mFrameIndex it closes the frames that ran. The step_frames summaries in headless_engine.h and the README (table row and the one-failure-per-failure bullet) are updated.
- **F786** MINOR The core-level test support headers are never header-checked (`tests/support/CMakeLists.txt:58`). conjure_add_test_support_library() now registers the nine core support headers in CONJURE_PUBLIC_HEADERS, the same way the engine library does. tests/support/CMakeLists.txt calls conjure_header_check(conjure_test_support INCLUDE_ROOT .../include) for the static flavour only, and the file comment says so. The new conjure_test_support_header_check target built all nine stubs (allocation_scope through test_environment) with warnings as errors and no missing includes, so no header needed changes.

### platform (5) (commit `21d917d`)

- **F280** MINOR Leaving fullscreen on a window created fullscreen restores it at the monitor origin with the title bar off screen (`engine/src/platform/glfw/glfw_window.cpp:229`). A window created fullscreen no longer goes back to the monitor origin (title bar off screen) when it leaves fullscreen. glfw_window keeps a private mWindowedPositionKnown flag. initialise() reads the position only for a windowed window, and for a fullscreen one it takes window_desc::position when given. set_position (while windowed) and set_fullscreen(true) mark the position known. When set_fullscreen(false) has no known position, it centres the restored size on the work area of the monitor the window is leaving, using a new private helper centred_on_work_area() that runs before glfwSetWindowMonitor. The null backend does the same: mWindowedPositionKnown = desc.position \|\| !desc.fullscreen, set_position marks it when windowed, and leaving fullscreen with it unknown calls centred_position(). Docs updated: window.h set_fullscreen summary, null_backend.h header comment, windows-and-events.md, handbook 3.4 and 6, README.
- **F282** MINOR Null monitor reports contentScale 1 even when the window was created with nullContentScale != 1 (`engine/src/platform/null_backend.cpp:66`). null_backend.cpp: the virtual monitor's contentScale now starts at mContentScale (desc.nullContentScale) instead of vec2(1), matching simulate_content_scale. Updated the null_backend.h header comment, handbook section 6, the windows-and-events.md monitors paragraph and the README.
- **F285** MINOR A gamepad ignored because all four slots were full is never picked up when a slot frees (`engine/src/platform/glfw/glfw_window.cpp:642`). glfw_window: on_joystick sets a new private flag mRescanJoysticks when a disconnect frees a slot. pump_events calls scan_joysticks() after the swap and patch_drop_spans, so the disconnect goes out in this batch and the waiting pad's connect in the next one. scan_joysticks also stops when a presence poll inside the scan raises another disconnect; the next pump scans again. This closes a gap in the plan: without it, a slot freed during the scan could go to a later joystick in the same scan, putting its disconnect and connect in one batch. Updated the on_joystick summary, handbook 3.3, input.md Gamepads and the README.
- **F286** MINOR format_action, format_key and format_named are three copies of the same function (`engine/src/platform/event.cpp:37`). event.cpp: removed format_action and format_key, which were copies of the template format_named. key_event, mouse_button_event and gamepad_button_event now use format_named for the key and action fields. Output is unchanged. The handbook section 1 table mentions the single fallback formatter.
- **F287** MINOR Layout pin of input_state is much weaker than its comment claims (`engine/src/platform/input_state.cpp:20`). Comments and docs only. They now say what is actually pinned: write_frame_block copies only the three key_bitset word arrays with memcpy and every other field one by one. Only the key_bitset size and alignment are ABI; the other asserts keep the snapshot compact and do not mirror conjure_input_block. Reworded input_state.cpp, input_state.h, handbook section 5 (plus the section 1 table row), the README main-types row and the input.md see-also line.

### build (3) (commit `5a5ca15`)

- **F036** MAJOR abi_identity.h generation breaks when the checkout path contains a non-ASCII character (`cmake/ConjureSharedLibrary.cmake:56`). I reproduced the bug first. A header list naming .../include/Jos<U+00E9>/probe.h made file(SHA256) fail on the truncated path '.../include/'. The fix is in cmake/ConjureSharedLibrary.cmake:58: the script-mode file(STRINGS) now passes ENCODING UTF-8, with the same comment the shader index script uses. For ASCII-only paths the strings are identical, so the hash in existing trees does not change.
- **F197** MAJOR MSVC Development builds compile engine code with no debug information (`cmake/ConjureConfigs.cmake:37`). cmake/ConjureConfigs.cmake:32-39 now sets CMAKE_MSVC_DEBUG_INFORMATION_FORMAT to $<$<CONFIG:Debug,Development>:ProgramDatabase>, guarded by if(NOT DEFINED) so a command-line value still wins. The file is included before project(), so the value reaches every target, fetched ones included. Distribution still gets no PDB, as the plan said.
- **F198** MINOR Managed SDK stamp does not depend on Conjure.Managed.csproj (`cmake/ConjureManaged.cmake:186`). The direct SDK case was already covered: the managed wave added Conjure.Managed/Conjure.Managed.csproj to the sdk SOURCES (managed/CMakeLists.txt:90). The function itself still ignored <ProjectReference>s for any other caller, so I made the general fix in cmake/ConjureManaged.cmake. A new function, conjure_managed_project_references(<csproj> <out>), parses the references; it handles backslashes and resolves paths with BASE_DIR. The CHECK_CLEAN script mode now uses it. conjure_add_managed_project walks the references transitively with a cycle guard. Every project file it visits is added to CMAKE_CONFIGURE_DEPENDS. A missing referenced project is a FATAL_ERROR. The referenced project files are now in the stamp DEPENDS, de-duplicated against SOURCES. I updated the header comment to match. This goes slightly past the plan, which only covered direct references: dotnet build compiles the whole reference graph.

### integration (3) (commit `8da0177`)

- **F127** MAJOR In Debug (enforcement off) a benchmark scenario that fails functionally still exits 0, so the benchmark.* CTest entries cannot fail (`tests/benchmarks/src/bench_runner.cpp:473`). Confirmed at bench_runner.cpp:473: exit_code() returned `enforceThresholds && anyFailed ? 1 : 0`, so a scenario that never measured or called fail() exited 0 in Debug and with --no-enforce. Added `bool scenarioFailed` to bench_result. run() sets it in both scenario-failure branches. exit_code() now returns BENCH_EXIT_FAILED for any scenarioFailed row right after the all-skipped check, before the enforcement check. Threshold breaches and UNTHRESHOLDED rows still follow enforceThresholds. Updated the doc comments in bench_runner.h (header exit-code paragraph, BENCH_EXIT_FAILED, exit_code, run) and benchmark.h (fail, is_measured, the new field). Also updated the --enforce line of the USAGE text, the README paragraph, a README Deviations bullet on 08_testing.md 3.6's 'iff --enforce' sentence, and the CMakeLists header comment.
- **F551** MINOR A threshold entry without the selected class, or with a misspelled key, passes with nothing checked (`tests/benchmarks/src/bench_runner.cpp:95`). Confirmed: read_class_threshold returned an all-zero threshold for an absent class, an absent key read as 0.0, and summarise skips zero limits, so a missing or misspelled class or key gave PASSED. Changes in bench_runner.cpp: (1) a new anonymous helper limits_anything(); run() uses the class threshold only when it limits something, otherwise the row is UNTHRESHOLDED and fails under --enforce. An 'allocs' gate alone still counts as a limit, as the plan says. (2) read_scenario rejects entry members other than desktop/ci/allocs, and read_class_threshold rejects class members other than p50_ms/p95_ms/p99_ms, both with INVALID_ARGUMENT naming the scenario, class and member. (3) run_all calls a new private warn_unmatched_thresholds(). It prints 'benchmark: thresholds entry '<name>' matches no registered scenario', sorted, for every entry that matches none of the registered descriptors (not only the filtered ones), and the exit code is unchanged. Docs updated: the bench_runner.h comments (load_thresholds, load_thresholds_text, run, run_all), the benchmark.h bench_threshold and UNTHRESHOLDED comments, and the README thresholds.json row, warning paragraph (including the compiled-out-module note) and a Deviations bullet. Checked the real tests/benchmarks/thresholds.json: it loads under the strict parser, and an actual conjure_benchmarks run (desktop with --no-enforce, ci with --enforce) printed no stale-entry warning and exited 0.
- **F605** MINOR Worker-count determinism test never fans the parallel integrator out (`tests/integration/test_determinism.cpp:101`). Confirmed: at 7 movers per frame the integrator always ran a single grain. A temporary build with SPAWN_PER_FRAME = 7 plus the new check failed all three determinism cases with 'CHECK( 1 > 1 )'. Fix in tests/integration/test_determinism.cpp: SPAWN_PER_FRAME 7 -> 24 (steady population about 2,160 movers in 6 chunks, so at least 3 grains). Added a file-local std::atomic<u32> sMaxIntegratorGrains (the 's' prefix follows the file-scope-static convention), which integrate_movers raises from context.last_grain_count() after its dispatch. run_sequence resets it to 0 before the frame loop and then checks sMaxIntegratorGrains.load() > 1. Extended the header comment with the sizing arithmetic.

### runtime (2) (commit `eca8770`)

- **F470** MINOR `conjure_runtime --help` shows nothing in a Distribution build (`runtime/src/main.cpp:232`). Confirmed at runtime/src/main.cpp:231-234: --help only called print_usage(stdout), so a WIN32_GUI Distribution build showed nothing. runtime_platform.h now declares has_standard_output() and show_info_dialog(). On Windows a shared has_standard_handle(DWORD) backs both has_standard_error and has_standard_output (GetStdHandle plus GetFileType != FILE_TYPE_UNKNOWN), and a shared show_dialog(title, text, icon) backs show_error_dialog (MB_ICONERROR) and show_info_dialog (MB_ICONINFORMATION). The POSIX backend probes STDOUT_FILENO with fcntl and its show_info_dialog does nothing. In main.cpp, print_usage is split into usage_text() (std::format) and print_usage(FILE*). The help branch now prints and flushes stdout, then shows usage_text() in the information box when has_standard_output() is false. The file header comments are updated.
- **F565** MINOR Runtime README says the bindings module is not implemented yet (`runtime/README.md:161`). Confirmed: runtime/README.md said 'the module is not implemented yet' and 'once the conjure::bindings target exists', and the runtime/CMakeLists.txt comment called bindings optional 'at this point ... until then'. engine/src/bindings/CMakeLists.txt:6 builds the module whenever CONJURE_WITH_SCRIPTING is on, and main.cpp adds the subsystem under CONJURE_RUNTIME_WITH_BINDINGS. README step 6 now says 'when the target exists'. The deviation bullet now says the module is skipped when CONJURE_WITH_SCRIPTING is off. The CMakeLists comment is replaced with the plan's wording.

## Not a bug

### rhi (3)

- **F687** MINOR null_device::stats() copies up to 4,096 marker-name strings on every call (`engine/src/rhi/backends/null/null_device.cpp:65`). null_device::stats() (null_device.cpp:65-68) does copy markerNames, but the header contract is about recording: 'its own bookkeeping is allocation-free in steady state' (null_device_stats.h:14-19), and reads are documented as a snapshot ('Read a snapshot with device::null_stats()', :12). The only callers are tests (tests/rhi, tests/renderer, and tests/integration/test_assets_renderer_headless.cpp:338,372, once per case). No benchmark, engine or editor path reads it per frame, so the claimed contradiction and cost do not occur.
- **F688** MINOR FORMAT_NAMES is sized by COUNT but nothing proves it has COUNT initialisers (`engine/src/rhi/texture_format.cpp:15`). Every mistake the finding describes is already caught by runtime tests. A trailing empty FORMAT_NAMES entry makes parse_texture_format("") succeed, because iequals("", "") is true (string_utils.h:169-179). tests/rhi/test_texture_format.cpp:120-126 lists "" as rejected, so that case fails. If EXPECTATIONS is not updated either, its value-initialised last row has format UNKNOWN and 'the expectation table covers every enumerator in order' (:53-58) fails. If EXPECTATIONS is updated, the to_string comparison at :100-104 fails.
- **F748** MINOR Shader index blob paths are unconfined and blobs are not verified against the recorded sourceHash (`engine/src/rhi/shader_library.cpp:295`). The facts are right (shader_library.cpp:295 joins the index's blob path unchecked, and sourceHash is unused), but the security premise is not. index.json and the blobs are build output in one trusted directory: <executable>/shaders, or engine_config::rendering.shaderDirectory, which is set only programmatically (engine_config.h:83, renderer_subsystem.cpp:198-204) and is not read from any project file. Anyone who can rewrite index.json can rewrite the blobs it points at, so path confinement or a hash stored in the same index adds no protection. The unused sourceHash is tracked as F294.

### core (1)

- **F252** MINOR parse_log_level reports an unknown name as OFF, so every caller must re-check (`engine/src/core/foundation/log.cpp:53`). Returning OFF for unknown names is the documented contract (log.h:48 'Returns OFF for unknown names', docs/api/core/logging-and-assertions.md:93-94 'validate configuration input'), and every caller validates. engine/src/engine/command_line.cpp:174 accepts 'off' and rejects typos. engine/src/editor/core/commands/commands_console.cpp:17 and commands_session.cpp:56 both reject OFF with 'expected trace, debug, info, warn, error or fatal', where OFF as a minimum filter level is deliberately not allowed. The finding's failure scenario ('commands_session.cpp:55 does not check at all - "verbose" silently becomes OFF') is wrong: line 56 rejects it.

### ecs (1)

- **F255** MINOR is_reserved accepts any generation for an index past the record table (`engine/src/ecs/world.cpp:465`). is_reserved is private (world.h:556) with a single caller, the flush plan loop (world_structural.cpp:631). Before that loop, flush grows the record table to cover every target index below the allocator's peak (world_structural.cpp:518-543), and new records get generation 1 (world.cpp:482-485). A forged {i, 7} with i inside the issued range therefore meets a FREE record of generation 1 and is rejected at world.cpp:468-469. The past-the-table branch (world.cpp:465-466) is only reachable for an index at or above the peak read at :518, where it returns false unless another thread reserves ids during the flush. The suggested 'generation == 1' rule would also be wrong in general: release_reserved (world_structural.cpp:274-283) hands indices back with generation 2+.

### editor (1)

- **F583** MINOR A screenshot whose PNG encode is in flight when the service dies is never answered (`engine/src/editor/core/screenshot_service.cpp:276`). The service is never destroyed while its dispatcher lives. It is created once in the editor_context constructor (editor_context.cpp:53), and rebind keeps it (only cancel_pending at :153). It dies only in ~editor_context, whose body first calls `mDispatcher->cancel_all(CANCELLED, ...)` (:69), and rebind calls cancel_all at :151. That answers the parked request whose PNG is still being encoded, so the reply is never lost. The suggested fix would be unsafe: async_reply holds a raw command_dispatcher pointer (editor_command.h:54), and the dispatcher is destroyed with the context, so calling reply.fail in the dead-service branch would be a use-after-free.

### managed (1)

- **F450** MINOR Element kind of scalar fields differs between managed (INT32) and the native writers (same as kind) (`managed/Conjure.Managed/Runtime/ScriptClassInfo.cs:252`). elementKind is documented as meaningful for LIST only: script_class_registry.h:27 '// LIST only', and design 03_scripting.md:159 and :209 default it to INT32. Every reader consults it only when kind == LIST (script_fields.cpp:553-564, 625-660). Native's own json_to_blob writes the registry's elementKind (script_fields.cpp:591), which is the same INT32 managed reports, so the managed collector and native codec already agree byte for byte. Only the convenience helpers (script_fields.cpp:295-320) put kind there, which no reader checks.

### physics (1)

- **F427** MINOR add_layer reports a null name as DUPLICATE_LAYER (`engine/src/physics/physics_layers.cpp:102`). The behaviour is the documented and tested contract. physics_layers.h:118 says add_layer returns 'DUPLICATE_LAYER (also for the null id)', tests/physics/test_physics_layers.cpp:123 pins it, and 14_physics.md:111 lists only LAYER_LIMIT_REACHED and DUPLICATE_LAYER for add_layer. The audio module uses the same convention (DUPLICATE_GROUP for a taken or null name, docs/api/audio/playing-sounds.md:219). from_json's LAYER_CONFIG_INVALID for an empty string is a JSON-shape error raised before add_layer is reached (physics_layers.cpp:56-61), so the two codes describe different failures.
